🦌 DeerFlow - 2.0🦌 DeerFlow - 2.0
英语 | [中文](./README_zh.md) | [日语](./README_ja.md) | [法语](./README_fr.md) | [俄语](./README_ru.md) | [葡萄牙语](./README_pt.md) | [韩语](./README_ko.md) English | [中文](./README_zh.md) | [日本語](./README_ja.md) | [Français](./README_fr.md) | [Русский](./README_ru.md) | [Português](./README_pt.md) | [한국어](./README_ko.md)
> 2026 年 2 月 28 日，DeerFlow 在版本 2 发布后登上 GitHub Trending 🏆 #1。 > On February 28th, 2026, DeerFlow claimed the 🏆 #1 spot on GitHub Trending following the launch of version 2. 万分感谢我们出色的社区——是你们促成了这一成就！💪🔥 Thanks a million to our incredible community — you made this happen! 💪🔥
DeerFlow（**D**eep **E**xploration and **E**fficient **R**esearch **Flow**，深度探索与高效研究工作流）是一个开源的**超级智能体运行框架（super agent harness）**，通过协调**子智能体**、**记忆**和**沙箱**，在**可扩展技能**的支持下完成几乎任何任务。 DeerFlow (**D**eep **E**xploration and **E**fficient **R**esearch **Flow**) is an open-source **super agent harness** that orchestrates **sub-agents**, **memory**, and **sandboxes** to do almost anything — powered by **extensible skills**.
https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/a8bcadc4-e040-4cf2-8fda-dd768b999c18 https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/a8bcadc4-e040-4cf2-8fda-dd768b999c18
> [!NOTE] > [!NOTE]
> **DeerFlow 2.0 是从零开始重写的版本。**它与 v1 不共享任何代码。 > **DeerFlow 2.0 is a ground-up rewrite.** It shares no code with v1. 如果你在寻找最初的 Deep Research 框架，该框架仍在 [`1.x` 分支](https://github.com/bytedance/deer-flow/tree/main-1.x)上维护——仍然欢迎向该分支贡献代码。 If you're looking for the original Deep Research framework, it's maintained on the [`1.x` branch](https://github.com/bytedance/deer-flow/tree/main-1.x) — contributions there are still welcome. 当前的活跃开发已转移至 2.0。 Active development has moved to 2.0.

用一句话完成智能体设置One-Line Agent Setup
如果你使用 Claude Code、Codex、Cursor、Windsurf 或其他编程智能体，可以用一句话向其提供设置指令： If you use Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Windsurf, or another coding agent, you can hand it the setup instructions in one sentence: 
Help me clone DeerFlow if needed, then bootstrap it for local development by following https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bytedance/deer-flow/main/Install.md
该提示词面向编程智能体。 That prompt is intended for coding agents. 它指示智能体在需要时克隆仓库，在 Docker 可用时选择 Docker，并在停止时给出下一条需要执行的确切命令，以及仍需用户提供的所有缺失配置。 It tells the agent to clone the repo if needed, choose Docker when available, and stop with the exact next command plus any missing config the user still needs to provide.
快速入门Quick Start
配置Configuration
应用程序在 SQLite 和 PostgreSQL 上执行 SQL JSON 写入时，会将非有限浮点值（`NaN`、`Infinity`、`-Infinity`）规范化为 `null`，包括存储在数据库中的智能体定义和技能变更记录中的此类值。 Application SQL JSON writes normalize non-finite floats (`NaN`, `Infinity`, `-Infinity`) to `null` on SQLite and PostgreSQL, including DB-backed agent definitions and skill-mutation records. 现有行不会被重写。 Existing rows are not rewritten.
设置 `run_events.backend: db` 时，调用方提供的 ISO 8601 事件时间戳会在存储前规范化为 UTC，从而在 SQLite 丢弃时区信息时保留其所表示的时间点。 With `run_events.backend: db`, caller-supplied ISO 8601 event timestamps are normalized to UTC before storage, preserving their instant when SQLite drops timezone information. 不带偏移量的时间戳按 UTC 处理。 Timestamps without an offset are treated as UTC. 此前存储的时间戳不会被重写。 Previously stored timestamps are not rewritten.
运行事件流包含 `run.terminal`，用于表示所属工作进程已确认的最终 Run event streams include `run.terminal` for the owning worker's confirmed final
运行状态，而 `run.end` 仍用于描述每次根图执行的完成情况。 Run status, while `run.end` continues to describe each root graph completion.
终态发布采用尽力而为的方式；事件持久化失败不会撤销 Terminal publication is best effort; event persistence failure does not undo the
运行结果，也不会阻止流结束。 Run outcome or prevent stream completion. 使用内置 SQL 运行存储和事件存储进行恢复时， Recovery with built-in SQL Run and Event stores
若两者共享同一个引擎，则每次受保护的事件写入使用一个池化连接，因此 sharing an engine uses one pooled connection for each guarded event write, so it
即使连接池中仅有一个可用连接槽位，也能进行发布。 can publish with only one pool slot available. 参见[运行事件流](backend/docs/RUN_EVENT_STREAM.md#authoritative-run-outcome)。 See [Run Event Stream](backend/docs/RUN_EVENT_STREAM.md#authoritative-run-outcome).
PostgreSQL 异步 ORM 连接和离线命令接受 `postgres://` PostgreSQL async ORM connections and the offline command accept `postgres://`
和 `postgresql://` URL，包括带有 `?sslmode=disable` 或 `?sslmode=verify-full` 的 URL。 and `postgresql://` URLs, including `?sslmode=disable` or `?sslmode=verify-full`.
SSL 模式会被保留；相互冲突的 `ssl`/`sslmode` 设置会被拒绝。 SSL modes are preserved; conflicting `ssl`/`sslmode` settings are rejected.
显式指定的 `channel_binding` 选项会被拒绝，因为 asyncpg 无法强制落实 Explicit `channel_binding` options are rejected because asyncpg cannot enforce
这些选项。 them. 同步 psycopg 和 LangGraph 的 DSN 保留其 libpq 选项。 Synchronous psycopg and LangGraph DSNs retain their libpq options.
使用支持隔离防护（fencing）的 SQL RunStore 和 `DbRunEventStore` 时，经宿主授权的 With a fencing-capable SQL RunStore and `DbRunEventStore`, host-authorized
生产者写入会校验持久化的 Run 实例代次、生产者纪元和状态 producer writes validate the persisted Run incarnation, producer epoch and state
即使设置了 `run_events.producer_fencing: "off"`，也仍会如此。 even with `run_events.producer_fencing: "off"`. 过期的 Journal 刷写操作在 Stale Journal flushes after
接管或删除之后，因此可能在默认配置下被拒绝。 takeover or deletion may therefore be rejected in the default configuration.
在 PostgreSQL 上，这些写入会持有 Thread Fence 和 RunRow 锁直至提交。 On PostgreSQL, these writes hold the Thread Fence and RunRow lock through commit.
检查点和分支历史的导入使用单独的宿主授权，该授权绑定到 Checkpoint and branch history imports use a separate host grant bound to a
持久化的线程操作预留以及已捕获的线程标识。 durable thread-operation reservation and the captured thread identity. 严格 Strict
模式允许这些导入，而不会将合成的轮次 ID 视为已获准接入的 Run； mode permits these imports without treating synthetic turn IDs as admitted Runs;
删除、预留撤销或租约到期都会使其授权失效。 deletion, reservation revocation or lease expiry invalidates their grant.
首次人工输入之前的消息保留其现有的合成轮次 0。 Messages preceding the first human input retain their existing synthetic turn 0.
在 PostgreSQL 上，`run_events.producer_fencing: "strict"` 还会启用 On PostgreSQL, `run_events.producer_fencing: "strict"` additionally enables
数据库级授权检查，以防范原始 SQL 和不了解该机制的旧版写入程序 database-level authorization checks against raw SQL and unaware legacy writers
在离线切换之后进行的写入。 after an offline cutover. `"off"` 设置会使应用层的生产者 The `"off"` setting keeps application-level producer
校验保持启用。 validation active. 激活之前，数据库 INSERT 触发器会跳过其 Before activation, the database INSERT trigger skips its
Thread/RunRow 锁，并持续持有共享切换锁，直到每个 INSERT Thread/RunRow locks and retains a shared cutover lock until each INSERT
事务结束。 transaction ends. 这不会移除上述应用层锁。 This does not remove the application locks above. 关闭模式下的 Off-mode
事件 INSERT 操作要求使用 `READ COMMITTED`（也接受 PostgreSQL 的 `READ UNCOMMITTED`）； event INSERTs require `READ COMMITTED` (also accepting PostgreSQL's `READ UNCOMMITTED`);
`REPEATABLE READ` 和 `SERIALIZABLE` 会被拒绝，以防止过期的策略快照 `REPEATABLE READ` and `SERIALIZABLE` are rejected to prevent stale policy snapshots
绕过激活机制。 from bypassing activation. 与激活操作竞争失败的写入会回滚 A write that loses a race with activation rolls back
并返回 SQLSTATE `40001`；请使用严格模式下的生产者权限重试整个事务。 with SQLSTATE `40001`; retry the entire transaction with strict producer authority.
激活要求停止所有 Gateway，并使用离线切换命令； Activation requires stopping every Gateway and using the offline cutover command;
所有重启的 Gateway 必须使用相同的切换 ID。 all restarted Gateways must use the same cutover ID. 这不会恢复缺失的 This does not recover missing
终态事件。 terminal events. 激活步骤： To activate:
在所有位置部署此版本，并设置 `run_events.producer_fencing: "off"`。 Deploy this version everywhere with `run_events.producer_fencing: "off"`.

停止接收新的接入，并等待现有接入和完成钩子处理完毕。 drain admissions and completion hooks. 仅凭 Run 处于终态并不能 A terminal Run status alone does not
证明其生产者已完成所有待处理工作。 prove its producer has drained.
停止所有 Gateway、Worker 和其他数据库客户端，包括检查点保存器的 Stop every Gateway, Worker and other database client, including checkpointer
连接池和监控客户端，并在激活期间使其保持停止状态。 pools and monitoring clients, and keep them stopped during activation.
在仓库根目录下，使用已安装的后端环境，运行： From the repository root, using the installed backend environment, run: 
   uv run --no-sync --project backend python backend/scripts/run_producer_fencing.py \
     --config config.yaml --cutover-id deployment-2026-10-27 --all-gateways-stopped
如果存在其他数据库会话，或仍有待处理／运行中的 The command refuses activation if other database sessions or pending/running
Run，该命令将拒绝激活。 Runs remain. 使用所有 Gateway 共享的部署 ID。 Use a deployment ID that every Gateway will share.
设置 `database.backend: postgres`、`run_events.backend: db`、 Set `database.backend: postgres`, `run_events.backend: db`,
`run_events.producer_fencing: strict` 以及 `run_events.producer_fencing: strict` and
在所有位置设置 `run_events.producer_cutover_id: deployment-2026-10-27`，然后重启。 `run_events.producer_cutover_id: deployment-2026-10-27` everywhere, then restart.
验证新建的 Run 能持久化其初始事件、Journal/Hook、投递事件和终止 Verify a new Run persists its initial event, Journal/Hook, delivery and terminal
事件，并正常关闭；不知晓此次切换的旧写入程序必须遭到数据库拒绝。 events and closes normally; unaware old writers must receive a database rejection.
激活状态会持久保留：切换 ID 缺失或不匹配，以及降级为 `"off"` Activation is durable: missing or mismatched cutover IDs and downgrades to `"off"`
或使用不受支持的存储，都会导致启动失败。 or unsupported stores fail startup. 此版本要求安排受控停机； This release requires controlled downtime;
不支持混合版本的滚动切换。 it does not support a mixed-version rolling cutover. 崩溃的生产者可能保持 A crashed producer may stay
开启状态，直到被显式关闭，或通过经授权的接管／删除操作撤销；不会 open until explicitly closed or revoked by authorized takeover/deletion; no
引入生产者过期任务或终止状态对账任务。 producer-expiry or terminal-reconciliation job is introduced.
定时任务的孤儿恢复机制会携带所捕获的 Run 实例标识和保留策略 Scheduled-task orphan recovery carries the captured Run incarnation and retention
修订版本来发布投递和终止凭据，严格模式下也是如此。 revision when publishing delivery and terminal evidence, including in strict mode.
未确认的发布会与已提交的接管操作分别记录；它 Unconfirmed publication is logged separately from the committed takeover; it does
不会撤销已恢复的 Run，也不会将新的发布尝试加入队列。 not undo the recovered Run or queue a new publication attempt.
运维人员可以通过 Operators can extend lead-agent, subagent, and DeerMem extraction prompts with
按字面原样前置／追加内容的配置来扩展主智能体、子智能体和 DeerMem 提取提示词，而无需编辑源模板。 literal prepend/append configuration without editing source templates. 请参阅 See
[提示词叠加配置](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#prompt-overlays)。 [prompt overlays](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#prompt-overlays).
可选的逐模型 [`request_admission`](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#model-request-admission) Optional per-model [`request_admission`](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#model-request-admission)
会调节请求发送节奏，以帮助将请求量控制在提供商每分钟请求数限制以内。 paces requests to help stay within provider request-per-minute limits.
此功能默认禁用；启用方法请参阅链接中的指南。 It is disabled by default; see the linked guide to enable it.
启用此功能会禁用 SDK 对外提供的重试机制，以及 Claude 和 Codex 适配器的 Enabling it disables exposed SDK retries and the Claude and Codex adapters'
内部重试循环，使中间件的重试再次经过请求准入控制。 internal retry loops so middleware retries pass through request admission again.
中间件重试也涵盖 HTTP 529 过载响应。 Middleware retries also cover HTTP 529 overload responses.
警告信息会指出被准入控制覆盖的 `retry_max_attempts` 值。 A warning identifies `retry_max_attempts` values overridden by admission.

同步流和异步流均保留这种兼容性：启用工具的 Both synchronous and asynchronous streams retain this compatibility: tool-enabled
流会根据非流式响应模拟生成分块，而未启用工具的流则保持原生流式传输。 streams simulate chunks from a non-streaming response, while no-tool streams stay native.
换行兼容机制会保留反引号和波浪号代码 Newline compatibility preserves literal escapes inside backtick and tilde code

**克隆 DeerFlow 仓库** **Clone the DeerFlow repository** 
   git clone https://github.com/bytedance/deer-flow.git
   cd deer-flow
**运行设置向导** **Run the setup wizard**
在项目根目录（`deer-flow/`）下运行： From the project root directory (`deer-flow/`), run: 
   make setup
这将启动一个交互式向导，引导你选择 LLM 提供商、可选的网页搜索，以及执行与安全偏好设置，例如沙箱模式、bash 访问权限和文件写入工具。 This launches an interactive wizard that guides you through choosing an LLM provider, optional web search, and execution/safety preferences such as sandbox mode, bash access, and file-write tools. 它会生成一个最小化的 `config.yaml`，并将你的密钥写入 `.env`。 It generates a minimal `config.yaml` and writes your keys to `.env`. 大约需要 2 分钟。 Takes about 2 minutes.
该向导还允许你配置一个可选的网页搜索提供商，或暂时跳过此步骤。 The wizard also lets you configure an optional web search provider, or skip it for now.
Brave 网页搜索会保留混合结果列表中的有效条目。 Brave web search preserves valid entries in mixed result lists. 格式错误的响应容器或不包含任何可用条目的列表会返回结构化的格式错误；结果缺失、为 null 或为空时，则保留现有的“未找到结果”响应。 Malformed response containers or lists containing no usable entries return a structured format error; missing, null, or empty results keep the existing "No results found" response. 格式错误还会记录格式错误容器的路径和类型，或记录不存在可用结果对象的情况，但不会包含搜索查询、凭据或载荷值。 Format errors also log the malformed container's path and type, or the absence of usable result objects, without including search queries, credentials, or payload values.
You.com、GroundRoute 和 Webz 在工作线程中初始化 HTTPX 客户端，以免同步加载 TLS 信任信息阻塞智能体的事件循环；请求和会话清理仍以异步方式进行。 You.com, GroundRoute and Webz initialize HTTPX clients in a worker thread so synchronous TLS trust loading does not block the agent event loop; requests and session cleanup remain asynchronous.
GroundRoute 网页搜索和抓取以相同方式处理 HTTP 成功响应中格式错误的载荷：载荷不是对象、`results` 容器不是列表，或非空列表中没有对象条目时，会返回该提供商的格式错误；对于其余部分有效的列表，则按顺序跳过其中的非对象条目。 GroundRoute web search and fetch contain malformed HTTP-success payloads the same way: a non-object payload, a `results` container that is not a list, or a non-empty list with no object entries returns the provider's format error, while non-object entries in an otherwise valid list are skipped in order. 结果缺失、为 null 或为空时，保留现有的“未找到结果”响应。 Missing, null, or empty results keep the existing "No results found" response.
DuckDuckGo 网页搜索会区分执行失败与结果为空：速率限制、超时、请求被阻止、客户端设置失败、SDK 错误以及缺少 `ddgs` 依赖项等情况，会返回结构化的 `DuckDuckGo search failed: ...` 错误，而非“未找到结果”；真正为空的结果集则保留现有的“未找到结果”响应。 DuckDuckGo web search distinguishes execution failures from empty results: rate limits, timeouts, blocked requests, client-setup failures, SDK errors, and a missing `ddgs` dependency return a structured `DuckDuckGo search failed: ...` error instead of "No results found", while a genuinely empty result set keeps the existing "No results found" response. 结构化的错误文本使测试框架能够将服务中断归类为暂时性故障或配置问题，而不是未匹配到任何内容的查询。 The structured error text lets the harness classify an outage as transient or a configuration problem instead of a query that matched nothing.
Tavily 网页搜索和抓取以相同方式处理 HTTP 成功响应中格式错误的载荷：载荷不是对象，或 `results`/`failed_results` 容器不是列表时，会返回该提供商的格式错误；对于其余部分有效的列表，则按顺序跳过其中的非对象条目。 Tavily web search and fetch contain malformed HTTP-success payloads the same way: a non-object payload, or a `results`/`failed_results` container that is not a list, returns the provider's format error, and non-object entries in an otherwise valid list are skipped in order. 结果缺失、为 null 或为空时，保留现有响应（搜索为 `[]`，抓取为 `Error: No results found`）；缺失或为 null 的搜索字段（`title`、`url`、`content`）会规范化为空文本，抓取结果中非字符串类型的 `raw_content` 会先强制转换为文本，再按截断上限进行截断。 Missing, null, or empty results keep the existing response (`[]` for search, `Error: No results found` for fetch); missing or null search fields (`title`, `url`, `content`) normalize to empty text, and a non-string fetch `raw_content` is coerced to text before the truncation limit.
Sofya 网页搜索和抓取以相同方式处理 HTTP 成功响应中格式错误的载荷：载荷不是对象、`results` 容器不是列表，或非空列表中没有对象条目时，会返回该提供商的格式错误；对于其余部分有效的列表，则按顺序跳过其中的非对象条目。 Sofya web search and fetch contain malformed HTTP-success payloads the same way: a non-object payload, a `results` container that is not a list, or a non-empty list with no object entries returns the provider's format error, while non-object entries in an otherwise valid list are skipped in order. 结果缺失、为 null 或为空时，保留现有的“未找到结果”响应。 Missing, null, or empty results keep the existing "No results found" response.
Serper 网页搜索和图片搜索会保留混合结果列表中的有效条目。 Serper web and image search preserve valid entries in mixed result lists. `organic`/`images` 容器格式错误，或非空列表中没有对象条目时，会返回 Serper 的格式错误；容器缺失、为 null 或为空时，则保留现有的“未找到结果”/“未找到图片”响应。 A malformed `organic`/`images` container, or a non-empty list holding no object entries, returns Serper's format error; missing, null, or empty containers keep the existing "No results found" / "No images found" response. 格式错误仅记录容器字段名，不包含查询、凭据或载荷值。 Format errors log the container field name only, without queries, credentials, or payload values.
当目标页面的 HTTP 状态码为 400–599 时，即使抓取服务本身返回成功响应，Unbrowse 网页抓取也会保留提取的文本，并在其前面添加警告。 Unbrowse web fetch preserves the extracted text but prefixes a warning when the target page's HTTP status is 400–599, even when the scraping service itself returns a successful response. 空页面错误也会保留该状态码。 Empty-page errors retain that status too. 这两种消息都会包含所报告的最终 URL，先移除其中的用户名和密码，再取最多 500 个字符；如果最终 URL 无法解析为具有主机的 HTTP(S) URL，则将其省略。 Both messages include up to 500 characters of the reported final URL after removing any username and password; final URLs that cannot be parsed as HTTP(S) URLs with a host are omitted.
腾讯云 WSA 网页搜索以相同方式处理 HTTP 成功响应中格式错误的载荷：`Pages` 容器不是列表，或非空容器中没有可用页面时，会返回该提供商的格式错误；对于其余部分有效的容器，则跳过其中格式错误的页面条目，从而保留有效条目。 Tencent Cloud WSA web search contains malformed HTTP-success payloads the same way: a `Pages` container that is not a list, or a non-empty container holding no usable page, returns the provider's format error, while malformed page entries in an otherwise valid container are skipped so the valid rows survive. `Pages` 缺失、为 null 或为空时，保留现有的“未找到结果”响应。 Missing, null, or empty `Pages` keep the existing "No results found" response.
Serply 网页搜索在所有垂直搜索类别中都以相同方式处理 HTTP 成功响应中格式错误的载荷：`results`/`entries`/`articles` 容器不是列表，或非空列表中没有对象条目时，会返回该提供商的格式错误；对于其余部分有效的列表，则按顺序跳过其中的非对象条目。 Serply web search contains malformed HTTP-success payloads the same way in every vertical: a `results`/`entries`/`articles` container that is not a list, or a non-empty list with no object entries, returns the provider's format error, while non-object entries in an otherwise valid list are skipped in order. 容器缺失、为 null 或为空时，保留现有的“未找到结果”响应。 Missing, null, or empty containers keep the existing "No results found" response.
SearXNG 网页搜索以相同方式处理 HTTP 成功响应中格式错误的载荷：载荷不是对象、响应体不是 JSON、`results` 容器不是列表，或非空列表中没有对象条目时，会返回该提供商的格式错误；对于其余部分有效的结果页，则按顺序跳过其中的非对象条目，并在每次跳过时记录该条目的位置，从而保留有效条目，并为提供商端的变化留下痕迹。 SearXNG web search contains malformed HTTP-success payloads the same way: a payload that is not an object, a body that is not JSON, a `results` container that is not a list, or a non-empty list holding no object entries returns the provider's format error, while non-object entries in an otherwise valid page are skipped in order, each skip logged with its entry position, so the valid rows survive and provider-side drift leaves a trace. `results` 缺失、为 null 或为空时，保留现有的空结果（`[]`）。 Missing, null, or empty `results` keep the existing empty result (`[]`). 这对自托管实例尤为重要：位于反向代理或强制门户之后的实例可能返回 HTML 或纯文本错误页面，却使用 HTTP 200 状态码；如果将其解读为空搜索结果，就会以“无匹配结果”结束研究循环。 This matters most for a self-hosted instance: one behind a reverse proxy or captive portal answers an HTML or plain-text error page with HTTP 200, and reading that as an empty search ends a research loop as "no hits".
InfoQuest 网页搜索和图像搜索以相同方式处理 HTTP 请求成功但载荷格式错误的情况：如果 `search_result` 封装不是对象，或者 `organic`/`top_stories.items`/`images_results` 容器不是列表或不含任何可用条目，则返回该提供方的格式错误；而对于其余部分有效的载荷，其中格式错误的条目会被跳过，以保留可用的结果行。 InfoQuest web and image search contain malformed HTTP-success payloads the same way: a `search_result` envelope that is not an object, or an `organic`/`top_stories.items`/`images_results` container that is not a list or that holds no usable entry, returns the provider's format error, while malformed entries in an otherwise valid payload are skipped so the usable rows survive. 容器缺失、为 null 或为空时，仍沿用现有的空结果。 Missing, null, or empty containers keep the existing empty result. 格式错误日志仅记录容器或条目的位置，不包含查询内容或载荷值。 Format errors log the container or entry position only, without queries or payload values.
InfoQuest 网页搜索和图像搜索通过共享的公开 URL 策略筛查结果 URL，并剔除被拒绝的结果行。 InfoQuest web and image search screen result URLs through the shared public-URL policy and omit rejected rows. 缺少必需的 `search_result` 封装的响应会返回格式错误，而不会暴露提供方的原始载荷。 Responses without the required `search_result` envelope return a format error instead of exposing the raw provider payload.
Jina、Browserless 和 InfoQuest 网页抓取使用所请求页面的 URL（或可用的 HTML 基准 URL）解析相对链接和图像源地址，因此返回的 Markdown 包含完整的目标地址。 Jina, Browserless, and InfoQuest web fetches resolve relative links and image sources using the requested page URL (or a usable HTML base URL), so returned Markdown includes complete destinations. 链接解析会保留周围的 HTML 源代码，包括格式不规范页面的格式。 Link resolution preserves the surrounding HTML source, including malformed-page formatting.
对于自托管的 Browserless、Crawl4AI、Firecrawl 和 fastCRW 抓取后端，必须先确保其出站访问与私有网络、环回网络、链路本地网络及云元数据网络隔离，然后才能在每个工具上设置 `network_isolation_confirmed: true`。 Self-hosted Browserless, Crawl4AI, Firecrawl, and fastCRW fetch backends require outbound isolation from private, loopback, link-local, and cloud-metadata networks before setting `network_isolation_confirmed: true` on each tool. DeerFlow 默认拒绝私有或无法验证的后端，因为这些服务会自行处理重定向、DNS 解析和子资源。`allow_private_addresses` 允许有意使用内部目标 URL，但不会绕过后端检查。 DeerFlow rejects private or unverifiable backends by default because those services handle redirects, DNS resolution, and subresources themselves. `allow_private_addresses` permits intentional internal target URLs and does not bypass the backend check. 参见[委托抓取部署](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#delegated-fetch-backend-isolation)；`make doctor` 会报告将被拒绝的后端配置。 See [delegated fetch deployment](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#delegated-fetch-backend-isolation); `make doctor` reports backend configurations that would be refused.
Jina 抓取支持通过工具配置中的 `max_retries`（默认值为 `0`）和 `retry_budget_seconds`（默认值为 `30`）选择启用有界重试。 Jina fetches support opt-in bounded retries via `max_retries` (default `0`) and `retry_budget_seconds` (default `30`) in the tool configuration. 有效的 `Retry-After` 提示会为 HTTP 429/503 设置最短等待时间；没有有效提示的 429 仍会终止处理。 Valid `Retry-After` hints set a minimum wait for HTTP 429/503; 429 without a valid hint stays terminal. 如果提示要求的等待时间无法容纳在剩余预算内，则停止重试。 Hints that cannot fit the remaining budget stop retries. 本地退避仍采用随机化方式。 Local backoff remains randomized. 重试可能增加上游请求次数和成本；参见 [Jina 抓取重试](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-fetch-retries)。 Retries may increase upstream requests and cost; see [Jina fetch retries](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-fetch-retries).
在 DEBUG 级别下，Jina 会输出一条长度受限的抓取完成摘要，其中包含尝试次数、耗时与退避秒数、结果和停止原因。 At DEBUG level, Jina emits one bounded crawl completion summary with attempts, elapsed/backoff seconds, outcome and stop reason. 有关配置和关联方法，请参见[诊断](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-crawl-diagnostics)。 See [diagnostics](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-crawl-diagnostics) for configuration and correlation.
Jina 还支持选择启用工具设置 `max_response_bytes`（正整数；省略或设为 null 时禁用）。 Jina also accepts an opt-in `max_response_bytes` tool setting (positive integer; omitted/null disables it). 它会在提取之前终止对解码后大小超限的响应的处理，不会返回部分成功结果，也不会重试。 It stops oversized decoded responses before extraction, with no partial success or retry. 这不会改变 4096 字符的输出上限，也不会限制 HTTPX 解压器的内存分配或网络传输带宽；参见[响应预算](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-response-byte-budget)。 This leaves the 4096-character output cap unchanged and does not bound HTTPX decompressor allocations or wire bandwidth; see [response budget](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-response-byte-budget).
Jina 还可以通过选择启用工具附加配置 `request_admission`，限制并发 HTTP 尝试次数和先进先出（FIFO）等待。 Jina can also bound concurrent HTTP attempts and FIFO waiting with the opt-in `request_admission` tool extra. 每个进程中的客户端和智能体线程共享同一个不可变预算；各副本拥有独立预算。 One immutable budget is shared across clients and agent threads in each process; replicas have independent budgets. 参见 [Jina 请求准入](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-request-admission)。 See [Jina request admission](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#jina-request-admission).
可随时运行 `make doctor`，以验证设置并获取可执行的修复建议。 Run `make doctor` at any time to verify your setup and get actionable fix hints.
设置向导和健康检查支持传统终端编码，包括 The setup wizard and health check support legacy terminal encodings, including
Windows cp936，并通过替换不受支持的字符，使这两个命令都能执行完成。 Windows cp936, replacing unsupported characters so both commands can finish.

线程清单遵循本地启动器的运行时路径：检出目录中 `.env` 的 Thread manifests follow the local launcher's runtime paths: checkout `.env`

Doctor 的内部工具探测也使用 UTF-8 解码，并替换无效 Doctor's internal tool probes also decode UTF-8 with replacement for invalid

请求线程清单时，非空的 `DEER_FLOW_HOME` 会选择 When a thread manifest is requested, a nonempty `DEER_FLOW_HOME` selects the
运行时数据目录，而非检出目录中的陈旧数据。 runtime data directory instead of stale checkout data. 外部数据路径 External data paths
在报告中显示为 `{DEER_FLOW_HOME}`；文件内容仍不包含在内。 appear as `{DEER_FLOW_HOME}` in the report; file contents remain excluded.
运行时路径设置也来自检出目录中的 `.env`，但 shell 导出的环境变量 Runtime path settings also come from the checkout `.env`, with shell exports
具有更高优先级。 taking precedence. 相对路径以检出目录为基准解析，与 `make dev` 一致。 Relative paths resolve from the checkout as in `make dev`.
如果未显式指定主目录，则已配置的 `DEER_FLOW_PROJECT_ROOT` 会选择其 Without an explicit home, a configured `DEER_FLOW_PROJECT_ROOT` selects its
`.deer-flow` 目录；外部路径在报告中使用该变量名表示。 `.deer-flow` directory; external paths use that variable name in the report.
> **高级 / 手动配置**：如果您倾向于直接编辑 `config.yaml`，请改为运行 `make config`，以复制完整模板。 > **Advanced / manual configuration**: If you prefer to edit `config.yaml` directly, run `make config` instead to copy the full template. 可选依赖项的自动检测支持带有或不带字节顺序标记（BOM）的 UTF-8 配置文件。 Optional dependency auto-detection accepts UTF-8 configuration files with or without a byte-order mark (BOM). 它首先检查现有的 `DEER_FLOW_CONFIG_PATH`，然后检查 `DEER_FLOW_PROJECT_ROOT/config.yaml`，最后才检查检出目录中的默认位置。 It checks an existing `DEER_FLOW_CONFIG_PATH` first, then `DEER_FLOW_PROJECT_ROOT/config.yaml`, before the checkout's default locations. 当检出目录中没有 `config.yaml` 时，本地启动也支持使用项目根目录中的配置。 Local startup also accepts the project-root configuration when the checkout has no `config.yaml`. 完整参考请参见 `config.example.yaml`，其中包括基于 CLI 的提供商（Codex CLI、Claude Code OAuth）、OpenRouter、Responses API、子智能体运行时上限（如 `subagents.max_total_per_run`）等。 See `config.example.yaml` for the complete reference including CLI-backed providers (Codex CLI, Claude Code OAuth), OpenRouter, Responses API, subagent runtime caps such as `subagents.max_total_per_run`, and more.

DeepSeek 回归测试随常规后端测试套件离线运行。 DeepSeek regression tests run offline with the normal backend suite. 若要验证 To verify

这些需主动启用的测试会向 DeepSeek 发送简短请求，并可能产生费用； These opt-in tests send short requests to DeepSeek and may incur charges;

YAML 模型在此页面中保持只读，并在名称冲突时具有优先权。 YAML models remain read-only in this page and take precedence on name conflicts.
托管模型追加在 YAML 模型之后；编辑会应用于新的配置 Managed models are appended after YAML models; edits apply to new configuration
快照，而正在进行的运行则保留其现有快照。 snapshots, while active runs retain their existing snapshot. 禁用某个模型 Disabling a model
会使其无法再被选择或解析，因此请在禁用前更新所有显式引用该模型的自定义智能体或定时 removes it from future selection/resolution, so update any custom-agent or scheduled
任务定义。 task definitions that explicitly reference it before disabling it.
托管模型由整个部署共享，而非归属于个人 API 密钥配置，并且 Managed models are shared by the deployment, not personal API-key profiles, and
仍受现有模型授权策略的约束。 remain subject to the existing model authorization policy.
加密的目录和生成的本地加密密钥存储在 The encrypted catalog and a generated local encryption key are stored in
`$DEER_FLOW_HOME/managed-models/`（默认路径为 `.deer-flow/managed-models/`）。 `$DEER_FLOW_HOME/managed-models/` (default `.deer-flow/managed-models/`). 请持久化保存 Persist
并备份**整个目录**，限制文件系统访问权限，并将其共享给 and back up the **whole directory**, restrict filesystem access, and share it
需要使用同一目录的 Gateway 工作进程或副本。 across Gateway workers/replicas that should use the same catalog. 本地密钥 The local key
通过文件系统权限加以保护；加密无法防范 is protected by filesystem permissions; encryption does not protect against
能够同时读取这两个文件的人。 someone who can read both files. 密钥丢失后，需要从备份中恢复。 Losing the key requires restoring the backup.
如果无法解密目录，读写操作将失败，而不会替换该目录。 Reads and writes fail if the catalog cannot be decrypted, rather than replacing it.
此存储独立于 SQL 后端，并支持只读 YAML 挂载。 This storage is independent of the SQL backend and works with read-only YAML mounts.

手动配置模型的示例 Manual model configuration examples 
   models:
     - name: gpt-4o
       display_name: GPT-4o
       use: langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI
       model: gpt-4o
       api_key: $OPENAI_API_KEY

     - name: openrouter-gemini-2.5-flash
       display_name: Gemini 2.5 Flash (OpenRouter)
       use: langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI
       model: google/gemini-2.5-flash-preview
       api_key: $OPENROUTER_API_KEY
       base_url: https://openrouter.ai/api/v1

     - name: opper-claude-sonnet-4-6
       display_name: Claude Sonnet 4.6 (Opper)
       use: langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI
       model: claude-sonnet-4-6
       api_key: $OPPER_API_KEY
       base_url: https://api.opper.ai/v3/compat

     - name: api-route-gpt-6.1-sol
       display_name: GPT 6.1 Sol (API Route)
       use: langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI
       model: gpt-6.1-sol
       api_key: $API_ROUTE_API_KEY
       base_url: https://global.api-route.com/v1

     - name: gpt-5-responses
       display_name: GPT-5 (Responses API)
       use: langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI
       model: gpt-5
       api_key: $OPENAI_API_KEY
       use_responses_api: true
       output_version: responses/v1

     - name: qwen3-32b-vllm
       display_name: Qwen3 32B (vLLM)
       use: deerflow.models.vllm_provider:VllmChatModel
       model: Qwen/Qwen3-32B
       api_key: $VLLM_API_KEY
       base_url: http://localhost:8000/v1
       supports_thinking: true
       when_thinking_enabled:
         extra_body:
           chat_template_kwargs:
             enable_thinking: true
OpenRouter 及类似的兼容 OpenAI 的网关应使用 `langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI` 并配合 `base_url` 进行配置。 OpenRouter and similar OpenAI-compatible gateways should be configured with `langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI` plus `base_url`. 如果你倾向于使用提供商专属的环境变量名，请显式将 `api_key` 指向该变量（例如 `api_key: $OPENROUTER_API_KEY`）。 If you prefer a provider-specific environment variable name, point `api_key` at that variable explicitly (for example `api_key: $OPENROUTER_API_KEY`).
设置向导中也提供了 [API Route](https://www.api-route.com)。 [API Route](https://www.api-route.com) is also available in the setup wizard. 它将你的密钥保存为 `API_ROUTE_API_KEY`，并使用上述 Chat Completions 端点。 It saves your key as `API_ROUTE_API_KEY` and uses the Chat Completions endpoint above. 模型的可用性取决于你的密钥所属的组及其权限；在替换 `config.yaml` 中的模型之前，请查看需要身份验证的 `GET https://global.api-route.com/v1/models` 模型目录，并原样使用其返回的 ID，不要添加上游提供商前缀。 Model availability depends on your key's group and permissions; check the authenticated `GET https://global.api-route.com/v1/models` catalog before replacing the model in `config.yaml`, and use its returned ID unchanged without an upstream provider prefix.
`write_file` 工具的输出预算提示使用当前模型的有效 `max_tokens`。 The `write_file` tool's output-budget hint uses the active model's effective `max_tokens`. 如果限制缺失或不可用，包括 YAML 中的 `.inf`、`-.inf`、`.nan`，以及大到无法用于字符数估算的值，则会省略此提示，而不会导致工具组装失败；提供商特定的模型验证仍然适用。 Missing or unusable limits, including YAML `.inf`, `-.inf`, `.nan`, and values too large for the character estimate, omit this hint without failing tool assembly; provider-specific model validation still applies.
要通过 `/v1/responses` 路由 OpenAI 模型，请继续使用 `langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI`，并设置 `use_responses_api: true` 和 `output_version: responses/v1`。 To route OpenAI models through `/v1/responses`, keep using `langchain_openai:ChatOpenAI` and set `use_responses_api: true` with `output_version: responses/v1`.
主智能体也能识别 Responses 输出 token 数量达到上限的情况（`status: incomplete` 且 `incomplete_details.reason: max_output_tokens`），包括流式响应中的此类情况。 The lead agent also recognizes Responses output-token caps (`status: incomplete` with `incomplete_details.reason: max_output_tokens`), including streaming responses. 它会阻止工具执行，从后续模型请求中移除被阻止的调用，并保留文本和推理内容。 It suppresses tool execution, removes the suppressed calls from later model requests, and preserves text and reasoning. 达到输出上限的工具调用响应会包含一条可见提示。 A capped tool-call response includes a visible notice. 纯文本输出达到上限时，会保留响应并结束当前轮次，不会因待办事项未完成而重试；其他不完整原因仍沿用现有处理方式。 Text-only output caps preserve the response and end the turn without unfinished-todo retries; other incomplete reasons retain their existing handling.
当旧版配置中的思考功能被禁用时，在推断方言的过程中，`when_thinking_enabled` 模板中值为空的 `extra_body`、`thinking` 和嵌套的 `extra_body.thinking` 键会被视为不存在。 When thinking is disabled on a legacy profile, blank `extra_body`, `thinking`, and nested `extra_body.thinking` keys in a `when_thinking_enabled` template are treated as absent during dialect inference. 当思考功能启用时，模板值会按配置转发。 When thinking is enabled, template values are forwarded as configured.
对于提供商接口约定与 DeerFlow 通用思考功能／推理强度假设不同的模型，可以为每个模型声明一个取值为映射的 `reasoning:` 块（包含思考功能的 `unsupported`/`optional`/`required` 状态、可接受的推理强度值及其别名和默认值、载荷方言，以及推理历史要求）。 Models whose provider contract differs from DeerFlow's generic thinking/effort assumptions can declare a per-model mapping-valued `reasoning:` block (thinking `unsupported`/`optional`/`required`, the accepted effort values with aliases and a default, the payload dialect, and the reasoning-history requirement). 设置向导中的 Z.AI GLM-5.3-Flash 配置使用了这一机制：每次前台和后台调用都保持思考功能开启，推理强度选择器提供该模型自身的 `low`/`high`/`max` 级别。 The setup wizard's Z.AI GLM-5.3-Flash profile uses it: thinking stays on for every foreground and background call, and the effort selector offers the model's own `low`/`high`/`max` levels. Ollama 现有的布尔值设置 `reasoning: true` 仍作为提供商的原生设置，并转发给 ChatOllama。 Ollama's existing boolean `reasoning: true` remains a native provider setting and is forwarded to ChatOllama. 将配置迁移到自定义推理强度 `path` 时，请从该配置和思考模板中移除所有旧的 `reasoning_effort` 设置；配置验证会拒绝残留的该键。 When migrating a profile to a custom effort `path`, remove any old `reasoning_effort` setting from the profile and thinking templates; configuration validation rejects the leftover key. 切换到未声明支持某个提供商专属推理强度值的旧版模型时，聊天界面会清除已记住的该值。 The chat UI drops a remembered provider-specific effort when switching to a legacy model that does not advertise it. 未包含该块的配置会保留其现有的提供商行为。 Profiles without the block keep their existing provider behavior. 有关其结构及等效的手动配置，请参阅 `config.example.yaml`。 See `config.example.yaml` for the shape and the equivalent manual configuration.
对于 vLLM 0.19.0，请使用 `deerflow.models.vllm_provider:VllmChatModel`。 For vLLM 0.19.0, use `deerflow.models.vllm_provider:VllmChatModel`. 对于 Qwen 风格的推理模型，DeerFlow 通过 `extra_body.chat_template_kwargs.enable_thinking` 切换推理功能，并在多轮工具调用对话中保留 vLLM 的非标准 `reasoning` 字段。 For Qwen-style reasoning models, DeerFlow toggles reasoning with `extra_body.chat_template_kwargs.enable_thinking` and preserves vLLM's non-standard `reasoning` field across multi-turn tool-call conversations. 为保持向后兼容，旧版 `thinking` 配置会自动规范化，而不会修改模型默认值或调用方拥有的 `extra_body` 字典；复用的字典可以在不同请求之间更改旧版开关的值。 Legacy `thinking` configs are normalized automatically for backward compatibility, without modifying model defaults or caller-owned `extra_body` dictionaries; a reused dictionary can change the legacy switch between requests. 如果端点在每个流式数据块中都报告累计用量快照，请设置 `cumulative_stream_usage: true`，以便 DeerFlow 将这些快照转换为各数据块的增量；该选项默认禁用，且在无法获得稳定的补全 id 时保持用量数据不变。 If the endpoint reports a cumulative usage snapshot on every streaming chunk, set `cumulative_stream_usage: true` so DeerFlow converts those snapshots into per-chunk deltas; the option is disabled by default and leaves usage unchanged when a stable completion id is unavailable. 推理模型可能还要求在启动服务器时指定 `--reasoning-parser ...`。 Reasoning models may also require the server to be started with `--reasoning-parser ...`. 如果你的本地 vLLM 部署接受任意非空 API 密钥，你仍可以将 `VLLM_API_KEY` 设置为占位值。 If your local vLLM deployment accepts any non-empty API key, you can still set `VLLM_API_KEY` to a placeholder value.
当为使用以下配置的模型启用提示缓存时 When prompt caching is enabled for a model configured with
`deerflow.models.claude_provider:ClaudeChatModel`，DeerFlow 会保留思考 `deerflow.models.claude_provider:ClaudeChatModel`, DeerFlow preserves thinking
和已隐去的思考历史，而不会将缓存断点直接设置在 and redacted-thinking history without placing cache breakpoints directly on
这些块上。 those blocks. 扩展思考模式下的工具后续交互可以继续使用提示缓存。 Extended-thinking tool follow-ups can keep using prompt caching.
以 CLI 为后端的提供商示例： CLI-backed provider examples: 
   models:
     - name: gpt-5.4
       display_name: GPT-5.4 (Codex CLI)
       use: deerflow.models.openai_codex_provider:CodexChatModel
       model: gpt-5.4
       supports_thinking: true
       supports_reasoning_effort: true

     - name: claude-sonnet-4.6
       display_name: Claude Sonnet 4.6 (Claude Code OAuth)
       use: deerflow.models.claude_provider:ClaudeChatModel
       model: claude-sonnet-4-6
       max_tokens: 4096
       supports_thinking: true
当 `auto_thinking_budget: true`（默认值）时，`ClaudeChatModel` 会对手动设置的扩展思考预算进行规范化。 `ClaudeChatModel` normalizes manual extended-thinking budgets when `auto_thinking_budget: true` (the default). 省略预算或将其设为 null 时，预算取 `max_tokens` 的 80%，且至少为 1024 个 token。 An omitted or null budget gets 80% of `max_tokens`, with a minimum of 1024 tokens. 显式指定的预算必须是至少为 1024 的整数，并且对于普通思考模式，必须严格小于 `max_tokens`；输出上限为 1024 或更低时，会在本地报错。 Explicit budgets must be integers of at least 1024 and, for ordinary thinking, strictly below `max_tokens`; output limits of 1024 or less fail locally. 对于受支持的手动交错思考模式，请配置工具并设置 `betas: ["interleaved-thinking-2025-05-14"]`：思考预算可以等于或超过取正值的输出上限。 For supported manual interleaved thinking, configure tools and `betas: ["interleaved-thinking-2025-05-14"]`: the thinking budget may equal or exceed the positive output limit. 有关支持的模型，请参阅 [Anthropic 的交错思考规则](https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/extended-thinking#interleaved-thinking-in-manual-mode)。`auto_thinking_budget: false` 会跳过此规范化和验证；禁用思考和自适应思考的行为保持不变。 See [Anthropic's interleaved-thinking rules](https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/extended-thinking#interleaved-thinking-in-manual-mode) for supported models. `auto_thinking_budget: false` bypasses this normalization and validation; disabled and adaptive thinking are unchanged.
Codex CLI 读取 `~/.codex/auth.json` Codex CLI reads `~/.codex/auth.json`
Codex 函数工具会保留包装式或扁平式字典定义中显式设置的 `strict: true` 或 `strict: false`。 Codex function tools preserve explicit `strict: true` or `strict: false` in wrapped or flat dictionary definitions. 设置缺失或为 null 时，保留提供商的默认值。`bind_tools` 对字典和 `BaseTool` 模式应用相同的转换。 Missing or null settings keep the provider default. `bind_tools` applies the same conversion to dictionaries and `BaseTool` schemas.
Codex 在 `bind_tools` 和直接调用中均遵循 `tool_choice`：`auto`、`none`、`required`，或函数名称/选择项。`any` 和 `True` 表示 `required`；`None` 和 `False` 保留提供商的默认值。 Codex honors `tool_choice` in `bind_tools` and direct calls: `auto`, `none`, `required`, or a function name/selection. `any` and `True` mean `required`; `None` and `False` keep the provider default. 每次调用指定的选择会覆盖已绑定的策略。 Per-call choices override the bound policy. 绑定的选择在绑定时进行验证；直接调用和覆盖设置在发送 HTTP 请求前进行验证。`required` 要求至少有一个函数工具，且按名称指定的选择必须与所提供的某个函数匹配。`auto` 和 `none` 在没有工具时也可使用。 Bound choices are validated when binding; direct calls and overrides are validated before HTTP. `required` needs at least one function tool, and named selections must match a supplied function. `auto` and `none` also work without tools.
当 token 用量为 null、被省略或为空时，已完成的 Codex 响应仍会返回其文本和工具调用；用量元数据仍不可用。 Completed Codex responses still return their text and tool calls when token usage is null, omitted, or empty; usage metadata remains unavailable.
Codex 模型提供商会返回已完成的响应，无须等待 SSE 连接关闭。 The Codex model provider returns completed responses without waiting for the SSE connection to close. 失败或未完成的响应会报告提供商给出的错误或原因；部分输出不会作为成功的回答返回。 Failed or incomplete responses report the provider's error or reason; partial output is not returned as a successful answer. 非对象形式的错误详情会以文本形式报告。 Non-object error details are reported as text.
`ClaudeChatModel` 在其提供商重试循环中遵循以整数秒数和 HTTP 日期形式给出的 `Retry-After` 提示。 `ClaudeChatModel` honors integer-second and HTTP-date `Retry-After` hints in its provider retry loop. 日期使用 UTC；过去的日期会触发立即重试，而提示无效或延迟超过 24 小时时，则保留本地退避策略。 Dates use UTC; past dates retry immediately, while invalid hints or delays beyond 24 hours retain local backoff.
Claude Code 接受 `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`、`ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`、`CLAUDE_CODE_CREDENTIALS_PATH` 或 `~/.claude/.credentials.json` Claude Code accepts `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`, `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`, `CLAUDE_CODE_CREDENTIALS_PATH`, or `~/.claude/.credentials.json`
`CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FILE_DESCRIPTOR` 接受以 UTF-8 编码传入的令牌，并在同一进程中为后续模型实例复用该令牌。 `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FILE_DESCRIPTOR` accepts a UTF-8 token handoff and reuses it for later model instances in the same process. 无法解码的传入内容会被跳过，以便 Claude Code 仍可尝试其覆盖配置指定的凭据文件或默认凭据文件。 Undecodable handoffs are skipped so Claude Code can still try its override or default credentials file.
CLI 凭据 JSON 文件接受带或不带 BOM 的 UTF-8 编码，不受主机区域设置影响。`make doctor` 在检查 CLI 身份验证时也接受相同的文件。 CLI credential JSON files accept UTF-8 with or without a BOM, independently of the host locale. `make doctor` accepts the same files when checking CLI authentication. 文本编码无效的来源会被视为不可读；覆盖配置指定的文件无效时，Claude Code 仍可尝试其默认文件。 Invalid text encoding is treated as an unreadable source; Claude Code can still try its default file after an invalid override.
ACP 智能体条目与模型提供商相互独立——如果配置 `acp_agents.codex`，请将其指向 Codex ACP 适配器，例如 `npx -y @zed-industries/codex-acp` ACP agent entries are separate from model providers — if you configure `acp_agents.codex`, point it at a Codex ACP adapter such as `npx -y @zed-industries/codex-acp`
ACP 智能体中仅含命令名或使用绝对路径的 `command` 会在智能体启动前完成解析，因此通过 npm 安装的启动器在 Windows 上也能正常工作：`npx` 或 `mcode` 会通过其 `npx.cmd`/`mcode.cmd` 包装脚本启动，而不会因找不到命令而失败；`C:\Users\me\AppData\Roaming\npm\mcode` 这样的绝对路径会解析为 npm 的无扩展名 shell 脚本旁的 `mcode.cmd` 包装脚本，而不会因 `WinError 193` 而失败。 A bare or absolute ACP agent `command` is resolved before the agent starts, so npm-installed launchers work on Windows too: `npx` or `mcode` is spawned as its `npx.cmd`/`mcode.cmd` shim instead of failing with a command-not-found error, and an absolute path such as `C:\Users\me\AppData\Roaming\npm\mcode` resolves to the `mcode.cmd` shim beside npm's extensionless shell script instead of failing with `WinError 193`. 查找过程使用智能体子进程实际可见的 `PATH`（若设置了 `acp_agents..env.PATH`，则使用该值，否则使用 Gateway 的 `PATH`），并向进程启动操作传入绝对路径，因此相对 `PATH` 条目不会在智能体自身的工作区中被重新解释。 The lookup uses the `PATH` the agent subprocess will actually see (`acp_agents..env.PATH` when set, otherwise the Gateway's) and hands the spawn an absolute path, so a relative `PATH` entry cannot be re-interpreted inside the agent's own workspace. 相对路径形式的 `command`（例如 `bin/mcode`）按配置原样使用；无法解析的命令仍会连同下方的安装指引一起报告。 A relative `command` (for example `bin/mcode`) is used as configured, and a command that cannot be resolved is still reported with the install guidance below.
每个 ACP 智能体的 `timeout_seconds`（默认值：1800）是初始化、会话创建和提示处理共用的时间预算，从子进程启动后开始计时。 Each ACP agent's `timeout_seconds` (default: 1800) is one shared budget for initialization, session creation, and the prompt, starting after the subprocess launches. 超时时，DeerFlow 会中止调用并关闭子进程，然后返回错误。 On timeout, DeerFlow aborts the invocation and closes the subprocess before returning an error. 工作区/MCP 准备和子进程清理不计入此预算。 Workspace/MCP preparation and subprocess cleanup are outside this budget. SDK 在此截止时间到达前引发的 `TimeoutError` 会保留其自身的错误消息。 A `TimeoutError` raised by the SDK before this deadline expires retains its own error message.
如果 ACP 智能体在回答前退出（例如由于尚未通过身份验证或某个配置参数有误），DeerFlow 会在几秒内报告其退出码，而不会等待 `timeout_seconds` 到期，并将智能体 stderr 的末尾内容记录到 Gateway 日志中。 If an ACP agent exits before answering (for example because it is not authenticated or a configured argument is wrong), DeerFlow reports its exit code within a few seconds instead of waiting for `timeout_seconds`, and records the end of the agent's stderr in the Gateway log. 智能体的 stderr 会被持续读取，因此即使智能体输出大量日志，也不会因管道已满而阻塞。 Agent stderr is read continuously, so an agent that logs heavily cannot stall on a full pipe.
MiniMax Code 直接支持 ACP 协议。 MiniMax Code speaks ACP directly. 安装并完成身份验证后，将其添加为 ACP 智能体： Install and authenticate it, then add it as an ACP agent: 
   npm install --global @minimax-ai/code
   mcode login
   acp_agents:
     mcode:
       command: mcode
       args: ["acp"]
       description: MiniMax Code for implementation, refactoring, debugging, and repository tasks
       auto_approve_permissions: false
`mcode` 必须位于 Gateway 进程的 `PATH` 中；仅在 Docker 宿主机上安装它，并不会使其在 Gateway 容器内可用。 `mcode` must be on the Gateway process's `PATH`; installing it only on the Docker host does not make it available inside the Gateway container. DeerFlow 在每个线程各自的 ACP 工作区中通过 `invoke_acp_agent` 调用它，并转发已启用的 MCP 服务器。 DeerFlow invokes it through `invoke_acp_agent` in a per-thread ACP workspace and forwards enabled MCP servers. 对于不可信的任务，请保持 `auto_approve_permissions: false`；仅当 mcode 必须编辑文件或运行命令，且你信任该任务时，才启用此设置。 Keep `auto_approve_permissions: false` for untrusted tasks; enable it only when mcode must edit files or run commands and you trust the task.
在 macOS 上，如有需要，请显式导出 Claude Code 身份验证信息： On macOS, export Claude Code auth explicitly if needed: 
   eval "$(python3 scripts/export_claude_code_oauth.py --print-export)"
导出工具在打印令牌、输出 shell export 语句或写入凭据文件之前，会拒绝格式错误的凭据容器，以及非字符串或空白的访问令牌。 The exporter rejects malformed credential containers and non-string or blank access tokens before printing a token, emitting a shell export, or writing a credentials file. 有效令牌会原样导出，文件导出则会保留完整的凭据容器。 Valid tokens are exported unchanged, and file exports preserve the full credential container.
也可以在 `.env` 中手动设置 API 密钥（推荐），或在 shell 中将其导出： API keys can also be set manually in `.env` (recommended) or exported in your shell: 
   OPENAI_API_KEY=your-openai-api-key
   TAVILY_API_KEY=your-tavily-api-key
`make dev` 和 `make start` 将 `.env` 作为数据读取，绝不会将其作为 shell 脚本执行，因此， `make dev` and `make start` read `.env` as data, never as a shell script, so a

运行应用程序Running the Application
部署资源配置Deployment Sizing
选择 DeerFlow 的运行方式时，可将下表作为实用的起点： Use the table below as a practical starting point when choosing how to run DeerFlow: 
| Deployment target | Starting point | Recommended | Notes |
| Local evaluation / `make dev` | 4 vCPU, 8 GB RAM, 20 GB free SSD | 8 vCPU, 16 GB RAM | Good for one developer or one light session with hosted model APIs. `2 vCPU / 4 GB` is usually not enough. |
| Docker development / `make docker-start` | 4 vCPU, 8 GB RAM, 25 GB free SSD | 8 vCPU, 16 GB RAM | Image builds, bind mounts, and sandbox containers need more headroom than pure local dev. |
| Long-running server / `make up` | 8 vCPU, 16 GB RAM, 40 GB free SSD | 16 vCPU, 32 GB RAM | Preferred for shared use, multi-agent runs, report generation, or heavier sandbox workloads. |
这些数值涵盖 DeerFlow 本身。 These numbers cover DeerFlow itself. 如果你还托管了本地大语言模型（LLM），请单独为该服务规划资源配置。 If you also host a local LLM, size that service separately.
对于持久运行的服务器，推荐采用 Linux 加 Docker 作为部署环境。macOS 和 Windows 最好用作开发或评估环境。 Linux plus Docker is the recommended deployment target for a persistent server. macOS and Windows are best treated as development or evaluation environments.
如果 CPU 或内存使用率持续处于满载状态，请先减少并发运行数，再升级到下一档资源配置。 If CPU or memory usage stays pinned, reduce concurrent runs first, then move to the next sizing tier.
选项 1：Docker（推荐）Option 1: Docker (Recommended)
需要 Docker Desktop / Docker Engine 和 **Docker Compose v2.24+** Requires Docker Desktop / Docker Engine and **Docker Compose v2.24+**
（`docker compose version`）。 (`docker compose version`). 较旧的 Compose 客户端无法解析可选的 Older Compose clients cannot parse the optional
`env_file` 语法，该语法位于 `docker/docker-compose.yaml` 和 `docker/docker-compose-dev.yaml` 中。 `env_file` syntax in `docker/docker-compose.yaml` and `docker/docker-compose-dev.yaml`.
**开发**（热重载、源码挂载）： **Development** (hot-reload, source mounts): 
make docker-init    # Pull sandbox image (only once or when image updates)
make docker-start   # Start services (auto-detects sandbox mode from config.yaml)
make docker-logs    # View logs
`make docker-start` 仅在 `config.yaml` 使用 provisioner 模式（配置了 `sandbox.use: deerflow.community.aio_sandbox:AioSandboxProvider` 和 `provisioner_url`）时才启动 `provisioner`。 `make docker-start` starts `provisioner` only when `config.yaml` uses provisioner mode (`sandbox.use: deerflow.community.aio_sandbox:AioSandboxProvider` with `provisioner_url`). 直接使用 Compose 的用户可以在开发命令中添加 `--profile provisioner`，以启用相同模式；默认服务栈不需要 Kubernetes。 Direct Compose users can add `--profile provisioner` to the dev command for the same mode; the default stack does not require Kubernetes.
设置 `USERDATA_PVC_NAME` 后，沙箱数据、额外挂载内容和 Lark 代理凭据共用一个 `user-data` 卷，并通过独立的 `subPath` 分别挂载，从而避免在 NFS CSI 驱动上重复引用同一个持久卷声明。 With `USERDATA_PVC_NAME`, sandbox data, extra mounts and Lark broker credentials share one `user-data` volume with separate `subPath` mounts, avoiding duplicate references to the same claim on NFS CSI drivers.
Docker 构建默认使用上游 `uv` 软件包源。 Docker builds use the upstream `uv` registry by default. 网关镜像使用 `uv sync --locked` 安装依赖，而 `uv.lock` 将软件包索引固定为 `https://pypi.org/simple`，因此 `UV_INDEX_URL` 必须保持该值：将其导出为 `https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple` 等镜像源会导致构建失败，并显示 ``The lockfile at `uv.lock` needs to be updated, but `--locked` was provided``（可在已同步的环境中使用 `cd backend && UV_INDEX_URL=https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple uv sync --locked --check` 复现；如果环境本身已过期，那么无论使用哪个索引，`--check` 都会转而报告环境过期，因此可使用 `uv lock --check` 在不同步的情况下查看该问题）。 The gateway image installs with `uv sync --locked`, and `uv.lock` pins `https://pypi.org/simple`, so `UV_INDEX_URL` has to keep that value: exporting a mirror such as `https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple` fails the build with ``The lockfile at `uv.lock` needs to be updated, but `--locked` was provided`` (reproduce in an already-synced environment with `cd backend && UV_INDEX_URL=https://pypi.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/simple uv sync --locked --check`; if the environment itself is stale, `--check` reports that instead for either index, so use `uv lock --check` to see it without syncing). 从镜像源安装需要重新生成锁文件或去掉 `--locked`，这两种做法都会改变镜像安装的内容，因此，对于打算向上游贡献的检出代码，请保留默认索引。`NPM_REGISTRY=https://registry.npmmirror.com` 与此无关：它适用于前端镜像中的 `pnpm` 安装，以及网关镜像中的 Lark CLI 安装（`@larksuite/cli`，由 `backend/Dockerfile` 使用 npm 安装）。 Installing from a mirror requires regenerating the lock or dropping `--locked`, and both change what the image installs, so keep the default index for a checkout you intend to contribute back. `NPM_REGISTRY=https://registry.npmmirror.com` is independent of this: it applies to the frontend image's `pnpm` install and to the gateway image's Lark CLI install (`@larksuite/cli`, which `backend/Dockerfile` installs with npm).
本地 AIO 沙箱的控制流量始终采用直连：环回地址和私有地址、 Local AIO sandbox control traffic is always direct: loopback/private addresses,
单标签集群主机名，以及 Docker/Podman 内部主机名均不会继承 single-label cluster hosts, and Docker/Podman internal hostnames do not inherit
`HTTP_PROXY` 或 `HTTPS_PROXY`。 `HTTP_PROXY` or `HTTPS_PROXY`. 外部沙箱的完全限定域名（FQDN）和公网 IP 仍然 External sandbox FQDNs and public IPs still
遵循环境中的代理设置。 honor environment proxy settings.
在原生运行时关闭且 MCP 拆除完成后，Gateway 会关闭已初始化的 After native runtime shutdown and MCP teardown, Gateway closes an initialized
沙箱提供程序，包括最初由工作线程中的工具调用创建的 AIO 沙箱。 sandbox provider, including AIO sandboxes first created by a worker-thread tool call.
它不会仅为执行清理而启动尚未使用的沙箱提供程序。 It does not start an unused sandbox provider solely for cleanup.
后端进程会在下一次访问配置时自动读取 `config.yaml` 的更改，因此在开发期间更新模型元数据无需手动重启。 Backend processes automatically pick up `config.yaml` changes on the next config access, so model metadata updates do not require a manual restart during development.
当 API Gateway runs use the top-level `recursion_limit` in `config.yaml` when an API
请求未提供 `recursion_limit` 时，Gateway 运行会使用 `config.yaml` 中的顶层 `recursion_limit`。 request does not provide one. 默认值为 `100`；有效的单次请求取值 The default is `100`; valid per-request values
具有更高优先级，而 `max_recursion_limit`（默认为 `1000`）为两者设定上限。 take precedence, and `max_recursion_limit` (default `1000`) caps both. 更改 Changes
会在下一次运行时生效，无需重启 Gateway。 apply to the next run without restarting the Gateway. 此顶层设置 This top-level setting

统一的 nginx 端点默认采用同源方式，且不发送浏览器 CORS 标头。 The unified nginx endpoint is same-origin by default and does not emit browser CORS headers. 如果运行的是跨源或通过端口转发访问的浏览器客户端，请将 `GATEWAY_CORS_ORIGINS` 设置为以逗号分隔的精确源地址，例如 `http://localhost:3000`；随后，Gateway 会应用 CORS 允许列表及相应的 CSRF 源检查。 If you run a split-origin or port-forwarded browser client, set `GATEWAY_CORS_ORIGINS` to comma-separated exact origins such as `http://localhost:3000`; the Gateway then applies the CORS allowlist and matching CSRF origin checks.
启用细粒度授权后，Live Browser 连接既要求具备 `threads:write` 权限，也要求拥有该线程，即使只是查看画面也不例外：同一个连接也能控制浏览器。 When fine-grained authorization is enabled, Live Browser connections require `threads:write` as well as ownership of the thread, even when only viewing frames: the same connection can control the browser. 权限检查在建立连接时执行。 Permission checks run when connecting. 升级后请重启 Gateway，以断开旧代码所允许建立的会话。 Restart Gateway after upgrading to disconnect sessions admitted by older code.
随附的 Docker、本地和 Helm nginx 配置均会代理 Live Browser 的 WebSocket 升级请求，并为静默流设置 600 秒的读取超时。 The bundled Docker, local and Helm nginx configs proxy Live Browser WebSocket upgrades with a 600-second read timeout for quiet streams. 对于集群内的 Gateway，Helm 使用 nginx 默认的连接超时和发送超时。 Helm uses nginx's default connection and send timeouts for the in-cluster Gateway. 任何额外的 Ingress 或反向代理也必须允许 WebSocket 升级。 Any additional Ingress or reverse proxy must also allow WebSocket upgrades.
浏览器登录使用 `HttpOnly` 会话 Cookie。 Browser login uses `HttpOnly` session cookies. 登录页面提供“保持登录状态”选项；当请求使用 HTTPS（包括可信的 `X-Forwarded-Proto: https`）或 localhost HTTP 时，该选项会延长浏览器会话的有效期。 The login page offers a "keep me signed in" option that extends the browser session when the request is HTTPS (including trusted `X-Forwarded-Proto: https`) or localhost HTTP. localhost 例外规则使用直接请求中的 `Host`，并忽略转发的主机标头。 The localhost exception uses the direct request `Host` and ignores forwarded host headers. 公共 HTTP 部署（包括许多临时沙箱 URL）默认回退为使用会话 Cookie。 Public HTTP deployments, including many temporary sandbox URLs, fall back to session cookies by default. DeerFlow 从不将密码存入浏览器存储；用户界面可能仅记住电子邮件地址。 DeerFlow never stores the password in browser storage; the UI may remember only the email address.
如果身份验证刷新在临时网络中断期间失败，可见的 If an authentication refresh fails during a temporary network outage, a visible
标签页会在网络连接恢复时或之后发生标签页 tab rechecks the unknown session when connectivity returns or on a later tab
切换时，重新检查状态未知的会话。 switch. 后台标签页收到的重新连接事件会被记录，直到该标签页变为 Reconnects received in a background tab are remembered until it becomes
可见状态，无需等待标签页切换节流限制解除。 visible, without waiting for the tab-switch throttle. 身份仅会在 Identity is restored only
收到成功的身份验证响应后恢复；若已确认会话过期或用户明确退出登录， after a successful auth response; a confirmed expired session or explicit logout
仍需重新登录。 still requires signing in again.
密码表单接受含有非 ASCII 字符的电子邮件地址，并会去除电子邮件地址首尾的空白字符。 Password forms accept non-ASCII email addresses and trim surrounding email whitespace.
登录要求密码至少包含六个字符；创建账户或选择 Sign-in requires at least six password characters; creating an account or choosing
新密码则要求至少八个字符。 a new password requires eight. 这些检查会在发送请求之前运行。 These checks run before sending a request.
密码会原样提交，包括其前后的空格。 Passwords, including surrounding spaces, are submitted unchanged.
OIDC/SSO 授权端点可能包含提供方的查询参数，例如 `?prompt=login`。 OIDC/SSO authorization endpoints may include provider query parameters such as `?prompt=login`. DeerFlow 在添加登录请求时会保留这些参数，包括为 `authorization_endpoint` 配置了覆盖值的情况。 DeerFlow preserves those parameters when adding the login request, including for configured `authorization_endpoint` overrides.
通过 SSO 登录后，DeerFlow 会将你带回要求你登录的页面，而非默认工作区。 After an SSO sign-in, DeerFlow returns you to the page that asked you to sign in instead of the default workspace. 已登录时打开登录页面，也会以相同方式跳转到其 `next` 目标。 Opening the login page while already signed in does the same for its `next` target.
管理员可以在 **设置 → 用户** 中停用和恢复账户（仅限管理员；普通用户无法看到此部分）。 Administrators can suspend and restore accounts from **Settings → Users** (admin-only; the section is hidden for ordinary users). 该页面列出每个账户的电子邮箱、角色和状态，并提供与 `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/{id}` 相同的启用/禁用操作；最后一个仍处于启用状态的管理员账户不能被禁用，Gateway 返回的拒绝详情会原样显示。 The page lists each account's email, role, and status and exposes the same enable/disable operation as `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/{id}`; the last remaining active admin cannot be disabled, and the Gateway's rejection detail is shown verbatim. 被禁用的账户会被所有身份验证入口拒绝，其现有会话会在下一次请求时终止，并显示注明该账户已被禁用的登录页面。 A disabled account is rejected by every authentication surface, and its live sessions end on the next request with a login page that states the account has been disabled. 对于被禁用的账户，密码登录仍会刻意返回“电子邮箱或密码不正确”，以免向持有凭据的人透露账户状态。 Password login for a disabled account deliberately still answers "incorrect email or password" so the state is not disclosed to whoever holds the credentials.
DeerFlow 仍使用 `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` 请求头来还原代理后方面向浏览器的协议方案和源（origin）。 DeerFlow still uses `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` headers to recover the browser-facing scheme and origin behind a proxy. 随附的 nginx 会设置 `X-Forwarded-Proto`，但会保留上游的 HTTPS 值，且不会覆盖所有转发请求头。 The bundled nginx sets `X-Forwarded-Proto`, but preserves an upstream HTTPS value and does not overwrite every forwarded header. 请配置外层可信代理，使其在流量到达 DeerFlow 之前替换或移除客户端提供的转发请求头。 Configure the outer trusted proxy to replace or strip client-supplied forwarding headers before traffic reaches DeerFlow.
> [!IMPORTANT] > [!IMPORTANT]
> Gateway 仍在进程内持有活动的运行任务，因此生产环境默认使用单个 Gateway 工作进程（`GATEWAY_WORKERS=1`）。 > The Gateway still owns active run tasks in process, so production defaults to a single Gateway worker (`GATEWAY_WORKERS=1`). 多工作进程部署需要 Postgres、Redis 流桥接器（`stream_bridge.type: redis`）、`run_ownership.heartbeat_enabled: true` 和 `run_events.backend: db`；进程本地的内存/JSONL 事件存储无法跨工作进程强制保证投递回执的唯一性。 Multi-worker deployments require Postgres, the Redis stream bridge (`stream_bridge.type: redis`), `run_ownership.heartbeat_enabled: true`, and `run_events.backend: db`; process-local memory/JSONL event stores cannot enforce singleton delivery receipts across workers. Kubernetes 副本在每个 Pod 中运行一个工作进程，而工作进程数无法反映这些副本：请使用 `deployment.multi_instance: true`（或 `DEER_FLOW_MULTI_INSTANCE=1`，Helm chart 等部署工具可根据副本数设置此值）声明这些副本，以便启动检查强制执行相同的前提条件，而不是保持不生效。 Kubernetes replicas run one worker per Pod, which the worker count cannot see: declare them with `deployment.multi_instance: true` (or `DEER_FLOW_MULTI_INSTANCE=1`, which deploy tooling such as a Helm chart can set from its replica count) so the startup gate enforces the same prerequisites instead of staying inert. 已声明的实例如果存储凭据（`channel_connections`，或使用 OAuth `refresh_token` 授权方式的 MCP 服务器，其轮换令牌通过数据库共享），还必须共享同一个用于静态数据加密的 `DEER_FLOW_CREDENTIALS_KEY`（Helm chart 和 `make up` 会生成此密钥；请将其备份，参见 [backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#credentials-encryption-key-deer_flow_credentials_key)）。 Declared instances that store credentials (`channel_connections`, or MCP servers using the OAuth `refresh_token` grant, whose rotating tokens they share through the database) must also share one at-rest `DEER_FLOW_CREDENTIALS_KEY` (the Helm chart and `make up` generate it; back it up, see [backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#credentials-encryption-key-deer_flow_credentials_key)). 该桥接器支持跨工作进程共享 SSE 投递，以及有界的 `Last-Event-ID` 重放。 The bridge shares SSE delivery and bounded `Last-Event-ID` replay across workers. 当无法确认被裁剪的重连游标是否支持完整重放，或者订阅者已进入空流等待状态、却在首次投递之前落后于保留范围时，Memory 和 Redis 会发出机器可读的 SSE `gap` 事件，而不是静默返回部分重放结果；Web UI 会重新加载持久化的线程/事件状态，并从仍保留的流尾部继续接收。 When a trimmed reconnect cursor cannot be verified for complete replay, or a subscriber that already established an empty-stream wait falls behind before its first delivery, Memory and Redis emit a machine-readable SSE `gap` event instead of silently returning a partial replay; the Web UI reloads durable thread/event state and resumes from the retained tail. 当游标恰好对应 Memory 桥接器最近淘汰的事件时，无需恢复即可重放：该事件之后的所有事件仍然保留。 The Memory bridge can replay without recovery when the cursor is its exact last evicted event: every successor is still retained. 更早的或无法验证的已淘汰游标仍会触发恢复。 Older or unverifiable evicted cursors still trigger recovery. 如果重放期间已被观测到的 Redis 流遭到删除，也会发出缺口信号，使较慢的订阅者重新加载持久化状态，而不是在清理后无限发送心跳。 An already-observed Redis stream removed during replay also signals a gap, so slow subscribers reload durable state instead of heartbeating forever after cleanup. Redis 还会通过短期租约跟踪终态流的生产者：如果工作进程在提交运行终态之后、发布 END 之前终止，SSE 和 `/wait` 会在租约到期后结束，而不必等待保留流的 TTL 到期。 Redis also tracks terminal stream producers with a short lease: if a worker dies after committing a terminal run status but before publishing END, SSE and `/wait` finish after the lease expires instead of waiting for the retained stream's TTL. 租约协调会将已终止工作进程的运行标记为错误，持久化其投递回执，发布流终止标记，安排保留流的清理，并更新受影响的线程状态。 Lease reconciliation marks runs from dead workers as errors, persists their delivery receipts, publishes the terminal stream marker, schedules retained-stream cleanup, and updates the affected thread status. Redis 的 END 发布具有原子性和幂等性：所有者重试和恢复重试会确认已有标记，而不会追加新标记；一旦 END 成为流的尾部，就无法再追加事件；清理后不具有所有权的恢复重试也不会重新创建该流。 Redis END publication is atomic and idempotent: owner and recovery retries confirm an existing marker without appending another, events cannot be appended once END is the stream tail, and an unowned recovery retry after cleanup does not recreate the stream. 对于较早的 END 后面仍有迟到数据的旧版流，仍可继续接收数据。 Legacy streams with an earlier END followed by late data can still accept data. Redis 兼容性扫描（`stream_bridge.legacy_end_scan_enabled: true`）也会在首次发布 END 时运行；只有在旧版写入程序停止运行，且所有旧版重放缓冲区均已过期或被移除后，才可禁用该扫描，随后重启每个 Gateway，以使用仅检查尾部的 END 检测方式。 The Redis compatibility scan (`stream_bridge.legacy_end_scan_enabled: true`) also runs on first END publication; disable it only after old writers stop and all legacy replay buffers expire or are removed, then restart every Gateway to use tail-only END detection. SSE、`/wait` 和内部流消费者使用 `stream_bridge.heartbeat_interval_seconds`（默认值为 `15`）执行空闲时的存活检查；修改此值需要重启 Gateway。 SSE, `/wait`, and internal stream consumers use `stream_bridge.heartbeat_interval_seconds` (default `15`) for idle liveness checks; changing it requires a Gateway restart. 格式错误的 Redis 重连 ID 会直接实时接收新事件，而不重放保留的缓冲区；保留缓冲区的滚动 TTL（`stream_ttl_seconds`）仍仅作为清理保障机制，而非运行超时限制。 Malformed Redis reconnect IDs live-tail new events instead of replaying the retained buffer, and the rolling retained-buffer TTL (`stream_ttl_seconds`) remains a cleanup safety net rather than a run timeout. 当应用数据库为 SQLite 或 Postgres 时（`auth.local.throttle_storage: auto`，默认值），`POST /api/v1/auth/login/local` 的登录失败计数器和锁定状态（`auth.local.max_login_attempts` / `lockout_seconds`）保存在共享的 `login_throttle` 表中，因此所有副本针对每个客户端 IP 共同实施一次锁定；`memory` 保留原有的进程级计数器，在 N 个副本下会给攻击者 N × `max_login_attempts` 次猜测机会，并在启动时记录警告。 Failed-login counters and lockouts for `POST /api/v1/auth/login/local` (`auth.local.max_login_attempts` / `lockout_seconds`) are kept in the shared `login_throttle` table whenever the application database is SQLite or Postgres (`auth.local.throttle_storage: auto`, the default), so every replica enforces one lockout per client IP; `memory` keeps the historical per-process counter, which under N replicas hands an attacker N × `max_login_attempts` guesses and logs a startup warning. 当应用数据库为 SQLite 或 Postgres 时，IM 聊天与线程的绑定保存在共享的 `channel_thread_bindings` 表中（现有的 `channels/store.json` 会在启动时导入一次，并重命名为 `store.json.migrated`）；在 Web UI 中输入的 IM 机器人凭据保存在共享的 `channel_runtime_configs` 表中，并使用 `DEER_FLOW_CREDENTIALS_KEY` 加密（现有的 `channels/runtime-config.json` 会导入一次，随后删除），因此在任一实例上保存的配置都会在一次租约续期内应用到提供方连接；每个提供方的平台连接通过共享的 `channel_leases` 表由一个实例持有——`channels.leader_lease`（默认为 `auto`，检测到多进程信号时即启用）会在持有者终止时将连接移交给另一个实例，而在启用了 GitHub 以外的 IM 通道时，启动检查会拒绝 `enabled: false`（参见 [IM 通道领导者租约](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#im-channel-leader-lease-channelsleader_lease)）；后续消息缓冲区仍需要自身的多工作进程协调机制。 IM chat-to-thread bindings live in the shared `channel_thread_bindings` table whenever the application database is SQLite or Postgres (an existing `channels/store.json` is imported once at startup and renamed `store.json.migrated`); IM bot credentials entered in the web UI live in the shared `channel_runtime_configs` table, encrypted with `DEER_FLOW_CREDENTIALS_KEY` (an existing `channels/runtime-config.json` is imported once and then deleted), so a save on any instance reaches the provider's connection within one lease renewal; each provider's platform connection is held by one instance through the shared `channel_leases` table — `channels.leader_lease` (default `auto`, on wherever the multi-process signal is) hands it to another instance when the holder dies, and the startup gate refuses `enabled: false` while an IM channel other than GitHub is enabled (see [IM channel leader lease](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#im-channel-leader-lease-channelsleader_lease)); follow-up buffers still need their own multi-worker coordination. 要在单台机器上试用此拓扑，`make dev-multi` 会启动两个共享 Postgres、Redis 和 `DEER_FLOW_HOME` 的 Gateway，`make dev-multi-check` 则运行跨实例检查；参见 [backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#local-two-gateway-harness) 中的本地双 Gateway 测试框架。 To try this topology on one machine, `make dev-multi` starts two Gateways on shared Postgres, Redis and `DEER_FLOW_HOME`, and `make dev-multi-check` runs the cross-instance checks; see the local two-Gateway harness in [backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#local-two-gateway-harness).
> >
> 上述生产者租约恢复行为由 `stream_bridge.producer_lease_recovery_enabled` 控制。 > The producer-lease recovery behavior above is gated by `stream_bridge.producer_lease_recovery_enabled`. 只要旧版 Gateway 二进制程序或其运行任务仍然存在，就保持禁用；只有在写入端部署更新和排空完成后，才在所有实例上启用。 Keep it disabled while old Gateway binaries or their runs remain; enable it on every instance only after the writer rollout and drain complete.

> 本地双 Gateway 测试框架默认禁用 `channels` 和 `channel_connections` 下的 IM 提供方。`DEERFLOW_MI_KEEP_CHANNELS=1` 会保留这些提供方；`channels.leader_lease` 保持原有配置。 > The local two-Gateway harness disables IM providers under both `channels` and `channel_connections` by default. `DEERFLOW_MI_KEEP_CHANNELS=1` preserves them; `channels.leader_lease` stays as configured.
> >
> 清理内存中的运行任务流或关闭其桥接器，会释放保留的 > Cleaning up an in-memory run stream, or closing its bridge, releases retained
> 载荷，并向尚未完成重放的订阅者发出重放缺口信号。 > payloads and signals a replay gap to subscribers with unfinished replay. 它们 They
> 重新加载持久化状态，而不是等待已分离的缓冲区或读取过时的 > reload durable state instead of waiting on a detached buffer or consuming stale
> 历史记录。 > history. 已完整读取终止流的现有读取方会保留其 Established readers that fully consumed a terminal stream retain their
> 正常的 `end` 信号。 > normal `end` signal. 初次连接时在与清理操作的竞争中落败的新读取方 A new reader whose initial attachment loses the race with
> 会收到缺口通知，以便恢复已保存的结果。 > cleanup receives a gap so it can recover the saved result.
> >
> 内存中的流事件会保留发布时的载荷。 > In-memory stream events retain the payload as it was published. 复用 Reusing a
> 生产者的数据对象或编辑收到的事件，不会改变其他 > producer's data object or editing a received event does not change another
> 订阅者的输出或重连时的重放内容。 > subscriber's output or reconnect replay.
> >
> 在 SSE 重放缺口恢复期间，Web UI 会在加载持久化状态时，保留由另一次运行占用的重连指针。 > During SSE replay-gap recovery, the Web UI preserves a reconnect pointer claimed by another run while durable state is loading. 较早的恢复过程在重新接入自身的流时，无法覆盖该指针。 An older recovery cannot overwrite that pointer when it rejoins its own stream. 即使指向其他运行的指针已经过时，也会被保留，因此下一次重新加载可能会先检查该运行，然后活动运行的恢复过程才重新接入，从而增加一个重连周期。 Even a stale foreign pointer is preserved, so the next reload may first check that run before active-run recovery rejoins, adding a reconnect cycle.
> >
> 当 run-manager > Store-only SSE and `/wait` consumers can finish after a heartbeat when run-manager
> 或计划任务协调过程恢复已失效工作进程的运行时，即使没有发布 END > or scheduled-task reconciliation recovers a dead worker's run, even if no END
> 标记，仅依赖存储的 SSE 和 `/wait` 消费方也可以在一次心跳后结束。 > marker was published. 常规完成仍会等待 END，以保留尾部事件。 Ordinary completions still wait for END to preserve tail events.
> >
> 在单进程 JSONL 部署中，取消已获准执行的事件存储修改操作时，会等待其后台文件 I/O、回滚和内部状态维护全部完成，然后才释放线程写锁。 > In single-process JSONL deployments, cancelling an admitted event-store mutation waits for its background file I/O, rollback, and bookkeeping to settle before releasing the thread write lock. 这可防止较早被取消的写入重新创建已删除的记录，或回滚之后成功的写入。 This prevents an older cancelled write from recreating deleted records or rolling back a later successful write. 因此，取消操作可能需要等待缓慢的存储操作；它不会停止正在进行的文件系统操作。 Cancellation can therefore wait on slow storage; it does not stop an in-flight filesystem operation. 仍在等待获取锁的调用方可以取消，而不会启动修改操作。 Callers still waiting to acquire the lock can cancel without starting a mutation. 跨多个线程的批处理会先等待当前线程组的操作全部完成，再传播取消信号；后续线程组不会启动。 A batch spanning multiple threads drains its current thread group before propagating cancellation; subsequent thread groups do not start.
> >
> 一次运行发布其流终止标记后，其进程本地的 `RunRecord` 在清理前仍会在现有的五分钟宽限期内保持可用；持久化运行历史仍可通过 `RunStore` 获取，而流桥接器则按其独立的清理计划保留待交付的尾部内容。 > After a run publishes its terminal stream marker, its process-local `RunRecord` remains available for the existing five-minute grace period before cleanup; durable run history remains available through `RunStore`, while the stream bridge retains its delivery tail on its separate cleanup schedule.
> >
> 使用内存运行存储时，将运行快照替换为归属另一线程的快照，会移除其原有的线程归属关系。 > With in-memory run storage, replacing a run snapshot under another thread removes its previous thread membership. 旧线程的历史记录和用量查询会将其排除，删除旧线程时也会保留已重新分配的运行。 Old-thread history and usage queries exclude it, and deleting the old thread preserves the reassigned run.
> >
> 运行取消请求可能被路由到任意 Gateway 工作进程。 > Run cancellation may land on any Gateway worker. 非所属工作进程现在会为仍在运行的所属工作进程持久化中断或回滚请求，后者会在续租时发现该请求并执行正常的取消流程；仅因负载均衡器的路由选择而导致的 409 将不再出现。 A non-owning worker now persists the interrupt or rollback request for the live owner, which observes it during lease renewal and performs the normal cancellation flow; load-balancer routing alone no longer produces a 409. 即使重试请求被路由到所属工作进程，也以第一个被接受的操作为准，且已接受的取消操作会与所属工作进程的完成操作进行原子竞争。 The first accepted action wins even if a retry lands on the owner, and accepted cancellation competes atomically with owner completion. 对于已失效的所属工作进程，仍按租约接管和孤立运行恢复流程处理。 Dead owners still follow lease takeover and orphan recovery. 因此，取消延迟的上限为租约心跳间隔。 Cancellation latency is therefore bounded by the lease heartbeat interval.
> 取消模型恢复探测，包括在其排队或等待重试期间取消，会使下一次调用能够检查提供方是否已恢复。 > Cancelling a model recovery probe, including while it is queued or waiting to retry, lets the next call check whether the provider has recovered. 取消操作不会计为提供方故障，也不会释放另一次调用正在执行的恢复探测。 Cancellation does not count as a provider failure or release another call's active recovery probe.
> >
> 模型重试会遵循提供方给出的有效 `Retry-After` 提示，最长可等待 24 小时，包括超过本地退避上限的等待时间。 > Model retries honor valid provider `Retry-After` hints up to 24 hours, including waits longer than the local backoff cap. 对于格式错误、非有限值或超过上限的提示，则采用常规退避。 Malformed, non-finite, or over-limit hints use normal backoff instead. 这适用于 LLM 错误处理和 Codex 重试路径中的数值型及 HTTP-date 型提示，以及 Claude 重试路径中以整数秒表示的提示。 This applies to numeric and HTTP-date hints in LLM error handling and Codex's retry path, and integer-second hints in Claude's retry path. Codex 优先采用数值型 `retry-after-ms` 提示；对于时间已过的 HTTP-date 型提示，则立即重试。 Codex prioritizes numeric `retry-after-ms` hints; past HTTP-date hints retry immediately.
> >
> 启用租约心跳后，暂时性的 RunStore 续租错误只会重试到最后一次确认的租约到期为止；随后，已失去有效租约的工作进程会取消本地执行，并阻止检查点、完成钩子、交付回执和线程状态的最终处理。 > With lease heartbeat enabled, a transient RunStore renewal error is retried only until the last confirmed lease expires; the stale worker then cancels local execution and suppresses checkpoint, completion-hook, delivery-receipt, and thread-status finalization. 已经在执行中的远程工具副作用仍可能不受本地取消操作的控制。 A remote tool side effect already in flight may still be outside local cancellation.
> >
> 协调过程使用原子接管声明，在选出候选对象后重新检查租约，因此所有者成功续租会优先于孤立运行恢复，且只有一个协调器能够报告某次运行已恢复。 > Reconciliation uses an atomic takeover claim that re-checks the lease after candidate selection, so a successful owner renewal wins over orphan recovery and only one reconciler can report a run as recovered. 当多个 Gateway 工作进程共享 Docker/AIO 或 E2B 沙箱后端时，还应配置 `sandbox.ownership.type: redis`；E2B 在后台启动和定期协调期间使用这些租约，以防重复或孤立沙箱清理操作终止仍在运行的其他工作进程的沙箱。 When multiple Gateway workers share the Docker/AIO or E2B sandbox backend, also configure `sandbox.ownership.type: redis`; E2B uses the leases during background startup and periodic reconciliation so duplicate/orphan cleanup cannot terminate a live peer's sandbox.

在多个 Gateway When rolling out the resume-command idempotency fix across multiple Gateway
工作进程或 Pod 中逐步部署恢复命令的幂等性修复时，将**所有带键的恢复提交和重试**（即 `command.resume` workers or Pods, route **all keyed resume submissions and retries** (`command.resume`
携带 `Idempotency-Key`，通过线程作用域的 `/runs`、`/runs/stream` 或 `/runs/wait` 提交） with `Idempotency-Key`, on thread-scoped `/runs`, `/runs/stream`, or `/runs/wait`)

如果您希望在本地运行服务： If you prefer running services locally:
前提条件：先完成上述“配置”步骤（`make setup`）。`make dev` 要求项目根目录中存在有效的 `config.yaml`。 Prerequisite: complete the "Configuration" steps above first (`make setup`). `make dev` requires a valid `config.yaml` in the project root. 设置 `DEER_FLOW_PROJECT_ROOT` 可显式指定该根目录，设置 `DEER_FLOW_CONFIG_PATH` 则可指向特定的配置文件。 Set `DEER_FLOW_PROJECT_ROOT` to define that root explicitly, or `DEER_FLOW_CONFIG_PATH` to point at a specific config file. 运行时状态默认存放在项目根目录下的 `.deer-flow` 中，可通过 `DEER_FLOW_HOME` 更改位置；技能默认存放在项目根目录下的 `skills/` 中，可通过 `DEER_FLOW_SKILLS_PATH` 更改位置。 Runtime state defaults to `.deer-flow` under the project root and can be moved with `DEER_FLOW_HOME`; skills default to `skills/` under the project root and can be moved with `DEER_FLOW_SKILLS_PATH`. 启动前，请运行 `make doctor` 验证您的设置。 Run `make doctor` to verify your setup before starting.
配置检查和智能体存储迁移通过在仓库根目录执行 `uv run --no-sync --project backend`，使用已安装的后端环境。 Configuration checks and agent-storage migrations use the installed backend environment through `uv run --no-sync --project backend` from the repository root. 这样可保留相对运行时路径和已安装的可选依赖项；请参阅[设置检查](backend/docs/SETUP.md#steps)和[智能体存储迁移](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#agent-storage)。 This preserves relative runtime paths and installed optional dependencies; see the [setup checks](backend/docs/SETUP.md#steps) and [agent-storage migration](backend/docs/CONFIGURATION.md#agent-storage).
在 Windows 上，请从 Git Bash 运行本地开发流程。 On Windows, run the local development flow from Git Bash. 基于 bash 的服务脚本不支持原生 `cmd.exe` 和 PowerShell shell，也不保证支持 WSL，因为某些脚本依赖 Git for Windows 提供的实用工具，例如 `cygpath`。 Native `cmd.exe` and PowerShell shells are not supported for the bash-based service scripts, and WSL is not guaranteed because some scripts rely on Git for Windows utilities such as `cygpath`.
文档中列出的根目录 `make` 命令会通过 Bash 调用仓库中的 `.sh` 文件 The documented root `make` commands invoke repository `.sh` files through Bash
，且调用方式是显式指定的。 explicitly. 因此，它们在源码归档包或文件系统中仍可正常工作 They therefore continue to work from source archives or filesystems

**检查先决条件**： **Check prerequisites**: 
   make check  # Verifies Node.js 22+, pnpm, uv, nginx
本地的 `make check`、`make install`、`make dev` 和 `make start` 入口在有可用的 `pnpm` 可执行文件时会直接使用它，否则回退到 `corepack pnpm`。 The local `make check`, `make install`, `make dev`, and `make start` entry points use a direct `pnpm` executable when available and otherwise fall back to `corepack pnpm`. 使用原生 Windows Python 时，共享运行器会先检查 `pnpm.cmd`，再按通用方式查找 `pnpm`；后者遵循 `PATH`/`PATHEXT`，可能选中同一 PATH 目录或更靠前的 PATH 目录中的 `.exe` 或 `.bat` 文件。 With native Windows Python, the shared runner checks `pnpm.cmd` before the generic `pnpm` lookup, which follows `PATH`/`PATHEXT` and may select an `.exe` or `.bat` in the same or an earlier PATH directory. Corepack 回退机制同样会先检查 `corepack.cmd`，再检查 `corepack`。 The Corepack fallback likewise checks `corepack.cmd` before `corepack`. POSIX Python 仍优先使用通用名称，在 MSYS/Cygwin 下运行时也是如此。 POSIX Python keeps the generic names first, including when running under MSYS/Cygwin. 运行器和诊断程序会将仓库路径解析为绝对路径，因此无论调用者的当前目录是什么，这些检查都能正常工作。 The runner and diagnostics resolve repository paths absolutely, so these checks work regardless of the caller's current directory. Corepack 从 `frontend/` 目录运行，因此会遵循 `frontend/package.json` 中固定的 `packageManager` 版本；无需启用全局 pnpm 垫片程序（shim）。 Corepack runs from `frontend/`, so it honors the `packageManager` version pinned in `frontend/package.json`; enabling a global pnpm shim is not required.
`make check` 将工具诊断信息按 UTF-8 解码，并保留 Unicode pnpm `make check` decodes tool diagnostics as UTF-8 and preserves Unicode pnpm
错误信息，即使主机使用的不是 UTF-8 编码。 errors on non-UTF-8 hosts. 无效的输出字节会被替换，以便检查器 Invalid output bytes are replaced so the checker
能够报告依赖项故障，而不是在解码输出时中止。 can report dependency failures instead of aborting while decoding output.

启动模式Startup Modes
DeerFlow 在 Gateway API 内运行智能体运行时。 DeerFlow runs the agent runtime inside the Gateway API. 开发模式启用热重载；生产模式使用预先构建的前端。 Development mode enables hot-reload; production mode uses a pre-built frontend. 
| | **Local Foreground** | **Local Daemon** | **Docker Dev** | **Docker Prod** |
| **Dev** | `./scripts/serve.sh --dev``make dev` | `./scripts/serve.sh --dev --daemon``make dev-daemon` | `./scripts/docker.sh start``make docker-start` | — |
| **Prod** | `./scripts/serve.sh --prod``make start` | `./scripts/serve.sh --prod --daemon``make start-daemon` | — | `./scripts/deploy.sh``make up` |
| Action | Local | Docker Dev | Docker Prod |
| **Stop** | `./scripts/serve.sh --stop``make stop` | `./scripts/docker.sh stop``make docker-stop` | `./scripts/deploy.sh down``make down` |
| **Restart** | `./scripts/serve.sh --restart [flags]` | `./scripts/docker.sh restart` | — |

将保持不变。 in their names are left alone.

`--skip-frontend-build`，如果直接调用 `./scripts/serve.sh --prod`）。 `--skip-frontend-build` when calling `./scripts/serve.sh --prod` directly). 这 This
需要显式启用：如果 `frontend/.next` 中没有已完成的构建，则会立即报错退出。 is opt-in: it fails fast when `frontend/.next` has no completed build.
Gateway 负责处理 `/api/langgraph/*`，并在 nginx 后方将这些兼容 LangGraph 的公共路径转换为其原生 `/api/*` 路由。 Gateway owns `/api/langgraph/*` and translates those public LangGraph-compatible paths to its native `/api/*` routers behind nginx.
