使用 `if_not_exists="create"` 的独立运行会将配置和运行元数据保留 Standalone runs using `if_not_exists="create"` retain config and run metadata
在新创建的线程上，其中包括可搜索的标签；对于重复的键，运行元数据具有 on the newly created thread, including searchable tags; run metadata takes
优先权。 precedence for duplicate keys. 线程所有权和 MCP 实例标识仍 Thread ownership and MCP incarnation remain
由服务器掌控，后续运行不会替换线程创建时的元数据。 server-owned, and later runs do not replace the thread's creation metadata.
对于通过 LangGraph Studio 或 For workflows that invoke `backend/langgraph.json` through LangGraph Studio or
直接通过 LangGraph Server 调用 `backend/langgraph.json` 的工作流，DeerFlow 会使用经过身份验证的身份信息， a direct LangGraph Server, DeerFlow consumes the authenticated identity
这些信息由该运行时发布，并将其用于自定义智能体配置/SOUL、用户 published by that runtime and uses it for custom-agent configuration/SOUL, user
技能及技能策略、上传内容、线程数据以及记忆读写。 skills and skill policy, uploads, thread data, and memory reads/writes. 这 This
使经过身份验证的运行不会使用共享的 `default` 文件系统存储桶，并且 keeps authenticated runs out of the shared `default` filesystem bucket, and the
由服务器掌控的身份信息优先于客户端提供的普通 `user_id` server-owned identity takes precedence over ordinary client-supplied `user_id`
值。 values. 电子邮件地址等外部身份标识会被映射为稳定的、 External identities such as email addresses are mapped to stable,
抗碰撞且可安全用作目录名的用户 ID，然后才访问 DeerFlow 存储。 collision-resistant directory-safe user IDs before accessing DeerFlow storage.
DeerFlow 的默认服务拓扑仍采用嵌入 Gateway 的运行时， The default DeerFlow service topology remains the Gateway-embedded runtime
如上文所述。 described above.
Gateway 运行会自动对在 `/mnt/user-data/outputs` 下创建或修改的产物强制执行原生交付：`present_files` 必须呈现至少一个由当前运行生成的输出，并且必须持久记录最终的 `run.delivery` 回执。 Gateway runs automatically enforce native delivery for artifacts created or modified under `/mnt/user-data/outputs`: `present_files` must present at least one output produced by the current run, and the terminal `run.delivery` receipt must be durably recorded. 在验证输出目录边界之前，会在生成该输出的同一经过身份验证的用户和线程作用域内解析虚拟产物路径。 Virtual artifact paths are resolved within the same authenticated user and thread scope that produced the output before the output-directory boundary is validated. 不生成输出产物的运行保持普通对话行为。 Runs that do not produce output artifacts keep ordinary conversational behavior.
在线程作用域内，以 `status: error` 结束的 `runs.wait()` 调用会报告当前运行的错误，而非先前的回答。 Thread-scoped `runs.wait()` calls that finish with `status: error` report the current run error instead of an earlier answer. 异步 Python LangGraph SDK 默认会抛出异常；传入 `raise_error=False` 可检查返回的状态和错误。 The asynchronous Python LangGraph SDK raises by default; pass `raise_error=False` to inspect the returned status and error.
DeerFlow 内置的自定义事件可通过两种 LangGraph 流式接口获取：原生客户端可以继续订阅 `stream_mode="custom"`，而基于回调的集成可以通过 `astream_events(version="v2")`，以 `on_custom_event` 记录的形式接收相同的载荷。 DeerFlow's built-in custom events are available through both LangGraph streaming interfaces: native clients can continue subscribing to `stream_mode="custom"`, while callback-based integrations can consume the same payloads as `on_custom_event` records from `astream_events(version="v2")`. 回调事件名称与载荷的 `type` 字段一致。 The callback event name matches the payload's `type` field.
Docker 生产环境部署Docker Production Deployment
`./scripts/deploy.sh` 支持分别执行构建和启动： `./scripts/deploy.sh` supports building and starting separately: 
# One-step (build + start)
./scripts/deploy.sh

# Two-step (build once, start later)
./scripts/deploy.sh build       # build all images
./scripts/deploy.sh start       # start pre-built images

# Stop
./scripts/deploy.sh down
高级Advanced
沙箱模式Sandbox Mode
DeerFlow 支持多种沙箱执行模式： DeerFlow supports multiple sandbox execution modes:
**本地执行**（直接在宿主机上运行沙箱代码） **Local Execution** (runs sandbox code directly on the host machine)
**Docker 执行**（在隔离的 Docker 容器中运行沙箱代码） **Docker Execution** (runs sandbox code in isolated Docker containers)
**结合 Kubernetes 的 Docker 执行**（通过资源配置服务在 Kubernetes Pod 中运行沙箱代码） **Docker Execution with Kubernetes** (runs sandbox code in Kubernetes pods via provisioner service)
可选的 [Tenki 提供程序](backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/tenki/README.md) The optional [Tenki provider](backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/tenki/README.md)
会保留终止失败的句柄以供重试，并拒绝在以下操作之后迟到的获取请求： retains failed termination handles for retry and rejects late acquisitions after
重置或关闭。 reset or shutdown. 如果关闭操作报告仍有未完成的工作，请在其 SDK 的 If shutdown reports outstanding work, retry after its SDK
工作线程完成或远程服务恢复后重试。 workers finish or the remote service recovers.
进程退出时，会记录尚未完成的清理工作，但不输出异常堆栈回溯。 At process exit, outstanding cleanup is logged without an exception traceback.

`/mnt/skills`）。 `/mnt/skills`) across its mounted categories. 模式始终相对于该 Patterns stay relative to that
根目录，且所有类别共用一个结果数量上限。 root, and one result limit covers all categories. 用户隔离、已禁用技能的 User isolation, disabled-skill
过滤以及只读挂载仍然生效。 filtering and read-only mounts still apply. 已配置的类别挂载，例如 Configured category mounts such as
`build` 或 `logs`，仍可被搜索；忽略规则在每个挂载内部生效。 `build` or `logs` remain searchable; ignore rules apply inside each mount. 发生故障的 A broken

对话状态中的沙箱引用归服务器所有。 Sandbox references in conversation state are server-owned. 外部运行 API 和 External run and
线程状态 API 会拒绝调用方提供的 `sandbox` 值；恢复 thread-state APIs reject caller-supplied `sandbox` values; when restoring a
检查点时，运行时会根据经过身份验证的用户 checkpoint, the runtime resolves the reference against the authenticated user
和线程解析该引用，然后工具才能复用它。 and thread before a tool can reuse it. 缺少运行时线程 ID 会引发 A missing runtime thread ID raises an
错误，即使引用的沙箱已被缓存也是如此。 error even when the referenced sandbox is cached.
当本地执行模式启用宿主机 Bash 时，DeerFlow 首先使用 `uname -s` 检测操作系统，然后在 Darwin 上使用 `sw_vers`。 When host Bash is enabled for Local Execution, DeerFlow starts OS detection with `uname -s`, then uses `sw_vers` on Darwin. 在 Linux 上，只有当前生效的沙箱策略允许时，它才会读取 `/etc/os-release` 等宿主机系统文件。 On Linux, it reads host system files such as `/etc/os-release` only when the active sandbox policy permits it. 宿主机文件系统路径检查仍然适用；路径被阻止后，系统会引导智能体使用获准的纯命令探测方式或虚拟路径，而不是重复执行被拒绝的命令。 Host filesystem path checks still apply; after a blocked path, the agent is directed to use a permitted command-only probe or virtual path instead of repeating the rejected command.

MCP 服务器MCP Server
工具步骤会明确标记执行失败。 Tool steps mark explicit execution failures. 计划任务步骤仅会在 Scheduled-task steps show completed
收到有效响应后才显示操作已完成，而不是在发出请求时就显示。 operations only after a valid response, rather than when the request is issued.
在聊天界面中，启用 **Token 用量 → 调试**，以查看通用工具/MCP 工具调用。 In the chat UI, enable **Token Usage → Debug** to inspect generic/MCP tool calls.
每个**工具详情**面板默认处于折叠状态，并显示工具名称、调用 ID、 Each **Tool details** panel starts collapsed and shows the tool name, call ID,
输入以及收到的结果或明确的错误。 input, and received result or explicit error. 过大的预览会被截断； Large previews are truncated;
名称长度超出剩余预览额度的字段会被省略，而不会被重命名。 fields whose names exceed the remaining preview budget are omitted rather than renamed.
结构化预览会保留完整的 JSON 语法，包括转义字符串和闭合定界符。 Structured previews retain complete JSON syntax, including escaped strings and closing delimiters.
当文本额度不足以显示下一个元素时，数组预览会停止；作为字面值的省略号会被保留。 Array previews stop when the text budget cannot display another element; literal ellipsis values are preserved.
数组末尾连续生成的标记会共用一个省略号，以表示被省略的后缀；后面仍有值的标记则保留其位置。 Consecutive generated markers at an array's end share one ellipsis indicating an omitted suffix; markers before later values retain their positions.
文本结果保留其原始表示形式，包括较大的数字 ID 和重复的 JSON 键，不会重新解析。 Text results retain their original representation, including large numeric IDs and duplicate JSON keys, without reparsing. 超出限制的文本会显示为其开头部分，并附有截断提示；结构化对象和数组则单独格式化。 Text exceeding the limit is shown as a prefix with a truncation notice; structured objects and arrays are formatted separately.
复制操作仅复制所显示的预览内容。 Copy actions copy only the displayed preview. 这是数据的前端视图 This is a frontend view of data
这些数据已由浏览器接收，且未经过额外的秘密信息脱敏层处理。 already received by the browser, without an additional secret-redaction layer.
在计划模式下，格式错误的 TODO 状态会返回常规的工具校验错误，而不会中止 token 归因，因此智能体可以修正该调用。 In plan mode, malformed TODO statuses return normal tool-validation errors without aborting token attribution, so the agent can correct the call.
工具生成的路径和 URL 可以作为简短的产物句柄保留，在上下文压缩后继续使用（`config.yaml` 中的 `tool_artifacts`）。 Tool-produced paths and URLs can be retained as short artifact handles across context compaction (`tool_artifacts` in `config.yaml`). 即使提供方重复使用调用 ID，句柄也能区分不同次出现的工具结果。 Handles distinguish separate tool-result occurrences, even when a provider reuses call IDs. 标准 MCP 图像 URL 会与文件引用一并捕获，其中包括来自 stdio 和 HTTP 服务器的结果。 Standard MCP image URLs are captured alongside file references, including results from stdio and HTTP servers. 检测到的文件 URL 会保留其查询字符串和片段标识符。 Detected file URLs preserve their query strings and fragments. 自由文本中的沙箱路径会保留成对 ASCII 引号、反引号或弯引号内的空格，包括使用反斜杠转义的外围引号。 Free-text sandbox paths retain spaces inside matching ASCII quotes, backticks, or curly quote pairs, including backslash-escaped wrappers. 未闭合的文本区段，以及包含换行符或制表符的定界值会被跳过。 Unterminated spans and delimited values containing a line break or tab are skipped. 自由文本中的产物引用会保留字面路径和 URL 中成对的 ASCII 及 CJK 括号，同时去除周围正文中的标点。 Free-text artifact references retain balanced ASCII and CJK brackets in literal paths and URLs while trimming surrounding prose punctuation. 启用个人身份信息（PII）脱敏后，模型可见的产物标签会遵循该策略；内部引用则保持完整，以供工具参数解析使用。 When PII redaction is enabled, model-visible artifact labels follow that policy; internal references stay intact for tool argument resolution. 配置的注册表容量限制使其保留最新的产物，而通过检查点保存的处理标识可防止已被逐出的结果在重启后再次被捕获。 The configured registry limit retains the newest artifacts, while checkpointed processing identities prevent evicted results from being recaptured after restart. 解析在授权检查和写入安全检查之前执行；未知或已过期的句柄会返回错误，且不会执行工具。 Resolution runs before authorization and write-safety checks; unknown or expired handles return an error without executing the tool. 较小的未知结构化结果可以作为完整 JSON 保留，大小上限为 4096 个 UTF-8 字节；空载荷或超限载荷会被跳过。 Small unknown structured results may be retained as complete JSON up to 4096 UTF-8 bytes; empty or oversized payloads are skipped. 句柄仅在所属智能体内有效：任务参数会将父智能体的句柄解析为具体引用，委派任务的报告必须返回具体引用，而非仅在子智能体内有效的句柄。 Handles are agent-local: task arguments resolve parent handles to concrete references, and delegated reports must return concrete references rather than child-local handles. 截断后的模型可见表示会报告省略的句柄数量。 A truncated model projection reports how many handles are omitted.
DeerFlow 支持通过可配置的 MCP 服务器和技能扩展其功能。 DeerFlow supports configurable MCP servers and skills to extend its capabilities.
不支持内联 MCP 音频结果。 Inline MCP audio results are not supported. Stdio 工具会像 HTTP/SSE 工具一样报告明确的错误，而不会将 base64 音频视为成功的文本结果。 Stdio tools report an explicit error, matching HTTP/SSE tools, rather than treating base64 audio as a successful text result.
启用持久化 MCP 后台任务后，智能体可以使用 `list_background_tasks(status="failed")` 或 `status="input_required"` 查找当前聊天中失败或等待输入的任务。 When durable MCP background tasks are enabled, agents can use `list_background_tasks(status="failed")` or `status="input_required"` to find failed tasks or tasks awaiting input in the current chat. 其他受支持的状态包括 `submitted`、`working`、`completed` 和 `cancelled`；省略状态参数会保留现有行为。 Other supported statuses are `submitted`, `working`, `completed`, and `cancelled`; omitting the status preserves existing behavior. 数据库会先应用筛选条件，再将结果限制为最近的 20 个匹配任务。 The database applies the filter before limiting results to the 20 most recent matching tasks. 与 `active_only=true` 结合使用时，两种筛选条件会同时生效：活跃状态为 `submitted`、`working` 和 `input_required`，因此终止状态会返回空列表。`GET /api/threads/{thread_id}/mcp-tasks` 同样支持在应用 `limit`（默认值为 50，范围为 1–100）之前使用 `status` 和 `active_only` 筛选条件；例如，`?status=failed&limit=20`。 When combined with `active_only=true`, both filters apply: active statuses are `submitted`, `working`, and `input_required`, so terminal statuses return an empty list. `GET /api/threads/{thread_id}/mcp-tasks` supports the same `status` and `active_only` filters before its `limit` (default 50, range 1–100); for example, `?status=failed&limit=20`. 未知状态会返回 422，省略筛选条件则保留现有响应。 Unknown statuses return 422, and omitting the filters preserves the existing response.
对于 HTTP/SSE MCP 服务器，支持 OAuth 令牌流程（`client_credentials`、`refresh_token`）。 For HTTP/SSE MCP servers, OAuth token flows are supported (`client_credentials`, `refresh_token`).
如果令牌响应中的 `expires_in` 值缺失、格式错误或超出范围，则使用一小时的默认有效期。 Missing, malformed, or out-of-range token response `expires_in` values use a one-hour default lifetime. 这也包括与当前时间相加后会导致到期时间戳溢出的有效期。 This includes lifetimes that cannot be added to the current time without overflowing the expiry timestamp.
持久化 HTTP/SSE 任务的状态查询和取消调用会根据持久化保存的任务所有者选择已配置的 `user_auth` 凭据，重启后也同样如此；每次请求中的秘密信息不会保留用于后台调用。 Durable HTTP/SSE task status and cancellation calls select configured `user_auth` credentials using the persisted task owner, including after restart; per-request secrets are not retained for background calls. 如果请求作用域内的凭据覆盖了提交时的身份验证凭据，则这两套凭据都必须有权访问同一个远程任务。 If a request-scoped credential overrides submit authentication, both credentials must authorize access to the same remote task.
对于 stdio MCP 服务器，可以使用 `tool_call_timeout` 配置每次工具调用的超时时间；对于 HTTP/SSE 服务器，持久化后台任务调用也遵循同一设置。 For stdio MCP servers, per-tool call timeouts can be configured with `tool_call_timeout`; durable background-task calls honor the same setting for HTTP/SSE servers as well.
持久化后台任务的状态查询和取消调用会在运行期间续期其认领权，因此，即使响应较慢，在最初的租约期限过后仍可保存。 Durable background-task status and cancellation calls renew their claims while they run, so a slow response can still be saved after the original lease duration. 如果失去所有权或无法确认续期，工作进程会停止其本地调用，并交由持久化状态机制进行恢复。 If ownership is lost or renewal cannot be confirmed, the worker stops its local call and leaves recovery to the durable state. 有关恢复和关闭行为，请参阅[控制调用租约](backend/docs/mcp-control-leases.md)。 See [control-call leases](backend/docs/mcp-control-leases.md) for recovery and shutdown behavior.
持久化 stdio 会话池在达到容量上限时仅逐出空闲会话。 The persistent stdio session pool evicts only idle sessions at capacity. 如果池中的所有会话都在执行工具，则新的会话请求会因容量错误而失败，并可在某次调用完成后重试。 If every pooled session is executing a tool, a new session request fails with a capacity error and can be retried after a call finishes.
对于 stdio 文件输出，不含路径的文件名会链接到由该次调用创建或修改、且与该文件名唯一匹配的文件。 For stdio file outputs, a bare filename is linked to a uniquely matching file created or changed by that call. 嵌入无关路径中的文件名会保持不变，其中包括 Windows 反斜杠路径中的文件名。 Filenames embedded in unrelated paths, including Windows backslash paths, are left intact. 显式远程 URL（`scheme://...`）会保留其原始查询参数和片段标识符，即使其中包含生成文件的名称或路径也如此，包括含有反斜杠转义引号或标记定界符的 URL；URL 之外的本地引用仍会正常转换。 Explicit remote URLs (`scheme://...`) retain their original query parameters and fragments even when these contain a generated file's name or path, including URLs with backslash-escaped quotes or markup delimiters; local references outside the URL still translate normally.
对于 HTTP/SSE 后台任务调用，`session_init_timeout` 单独限制连接建立（包括 SSE 端点事件）与 MCP 初始化的总时长；工具调用一旦开始，该限制便不再适用。 For HTTP/SSE background-task calls, `session_init_timeout` separately bounds connection setup (including the SSE endpoint event) and MCP initialization together; it stops applying once the tool call begins. 初始化超时错误会指明服务器及配置的时限。 Initialization deadline errors identify the server and configured time limit.
普通 `task` 子智能体在进行 MCP 调用时，会保留父运行所捕获的线程实例，包括旧版线程，因此委派会保持相同的生命周期作用域。 Ordinary `task` subagents retain the parent run's captured thread incarnation for MCP calls, including legacy threads, so delegation preserves the same lifecycle scope.
默认情况下，MCP 工具名称会添加 `_` 前缀，以防止不同服务器之间发生名称冲突。 MCP tool names are prefixed with `_` by default to prevent collisions across servers. 如果服务器已为自己的工具设置了命名空间，请在 `extensions_config.json` 中为该服务器设置 `tool_name_prefix: false`，以保留原始名称。 If a server already namespaces its own tools, set `tool_name_prefix: false` on that server in `extensions_config.json` to keep the original names. 只有在生成的名称在所有已启用服务器中仍保持唯一时，才应禁用此前缀。 Disable the prefix only when the resulting names remain unique across all enabled servers.
已登录用户的通知开关、默认模型、对话模式和推理强度会保存到其账户中，并在其他浏览器中或清除浏览器存储后恢复。 Signed-in users' notification toggle, default model, conversation mode, and reasoning effort are saved to their account and restored on other browsers or after clearing browser storage. 仍需在每台设备上分别授予浏览器通知权限。 Browser notification permission still needs to be granted on each device. 网络故障后会重试保存更改；未发送的更改在同一标签页重新加载后仍会保留。 Changes retry after network failures; unsent changes survive a reload in the same tab. 对不同字段的并发编辑会被保留；对于同一字段，以最后一次写入服务器的值为准。 Concurrent edits to different fields are preserved; for the same field, the last server write wins. 现有的未限定账户作用域的浏览器偏好设置不会自动上传，因为它们没有所属账户；升级后，请重新选择一次这些设置。 Existing unscoped browser preferences are not uploaded automatically because they have no account owner; reselect those settings once after upgrading. 静态演示和禁用身份验证的开发环境仍使用浏览器本地设置。 Static demos and auth-disabled development keep browser-local settings. 线程专属的模型覆盖设置和其他显示偏好设置仍保留在本地。 Thread-specific model overrides and other display preferences remain local.

能力中心将插件分为办公协作、文档与知识、搜索与研究、业务与数据以及开发与运维等类别。 Capability Center groups plugins by office collaboration, documents and knowledge, search and research, business and data, and development and operations. 该目录在现有 MCP 配置和 Lark 之外，还包含设置参考资料。 The directory includes setup references alongside existing MCP configurations and Lark. 推荐的集成和内置支持并不意味着连接已安装或经过验证；“已安装”筛选器仅显示已配置的 MCP 条目和已安装的 Lark。 Recommended integrations and built-in support do not imply an installed or verified connection; the Installed filter shows configured MCP entries and installed Lark only.
在 Web 界面中配置的个人 MCP 连接按用户持久化保存。 Personal MCP connections configured in the web interface are persisted per user.
更改部署级或个人 MCP 配置时发生的意外故障会返回固定的错误消息；异常详情保留在服务器日志中。 Unexpected failures when changing deployment or personal MCP configuration return fixed error messages; exception details stay in server logs.
部署级工具仍由所有用户共享。 Deployment tools remain shared. 管理员可以在 **Platform provided** 下添加、编辑、启用、禁用和删除共享 MCP 服务器；普通用户只能查看其状态，无法进行操作。 Administrators can add, edit, enable, disable and delete shared MCP servers under **Platform provided**; ordinary users see their status without controls. 个人插件开关仅影响当前登录用户的连接。 Personal plugin switches affect only the signed-in user's connections. 参见[连接归属](docs/capability-center.md#personal-and-deployment-mcp-configuration)。 See [connection ownership](docs/capability-center.md#personal-and-deployment-mcp-configuration).
有关插件清单、适配器注册和 Agent 能力选择，请参见 For plugin manifests, adapter registration, and Agent capability selection, see
[能力中心集成契约](docs/capability-center.md)。 [Capability Center integration contract](docs/capability-center.md).

联系人。 contacts. 保存配置不会执行任何外部写入操作。 Saving configuration performs no external write. 这些插件复用 These plugins reuse

插件品牌图标随软件一并打包在本地。 Plugin brand icons are bundled locally. 添加或编辑某个个人 MCP 插件时，用户可以上传 PNG、JPG 或 WebP 图像（最大 2 MB）、预览图像或恢复默认图标。 When adding or editing one personal MCP plugin, users can upload a PNG, JPG, or WebP image (up to 2 MB), preview it, or restore the default icon. 更改仅在点击保存后生效；自定义图标以标准化的 128px PNG 格式保存在服务器条目中仅用于显示的 `presentation.icon` 元数据中，可跨浏览器保留。 Changes take effect only after Save; custom icons persist across browsers as a normalized 128px PNG in the server entry's display-only `presentation.icon` metadata. 这些图标不会发送到 MCP 传输层。 They are not sent to the MCP transport.
能力中心 > 插件通过定向变更操作，每次添加、替换或删除一个 MCP 服务器，同时保留对其他同级条目的并发更改；删除操作使用通过 URL 指定目标且不含请求体的请求。 Capability Center > Plugins adds, replaces, and deletes one MCP server at a time through targeted mutations that preserve concurrent sibling changes; deletes use a bodyless URL-addressed request. 某个服务器上的无效 stdio 命令不再阻止切换另一个服务器的启用状态，而启用该无效服务器仍受命令允许列表约束，并会在 UI 中显示后端验证消息。 An invalid stdio command on one server no longer blocks toggling another, while enabling that invalid server remains protected by the command allowlist and surfaces the backend validation message in the UI.
对于 SSE/HTTP 服务器，定向更新同时接受 DeerFlow 的 `type` 字段和 MCP 规范的 `transport` 字段。 Targeted updates accept both DeerFlow's `type` field and the MCP-spec `transport` field for SSE/HTTP servers.
运行时的 MCP 和技能更新会以原子方式替换 `extensions_config.json`，因此写入中断不会导致共享配置被截断或仅部分写入。 Runtime MCP and skill updates replace `extensions_config.json` atomically, so an interrupted write cannot leave the shared configuration truncated or partially written. 读取同一文件的每个 Gateway 工作进程或实例都会在下次读取时获取新修订版本（缓存会检查文件的内容签名），因此通过一个副本进行的 MCP 和技能更改无需重启即可应用到其他副本；如果修订版本缺失、被截断或无效，则会保留先前的配置，直到完整版本就绪，包括文件在重新加载期间消失的情况。 Every Gateway worker or instance that reads the same file picks up a new revision on its next read (the cache checks the file's content signature), so MCP and skill changes made through one replica apply to the others without a restart; a missing, truncated, or invalid revision keeps the previous configuration until a complete one lands, including when the file disappears during a reload.
管理员执行 MCP 缓存重置时，会推进可写配置目录中持久保存的代际标记。 The admin MCP cache reset advances a durable generation marker in the writable config directory. 挂载同一目录的每个 Gateway 工作进程都会在下次查找前弃用自身缓存的工具和连接池中的会话；使用独立文件系统的副本不会自动纳入此机制的覆盖范围。 Every Gateway worker mounting that same directory retires its own cached tools and pooled sessions before the next lookup; replicas with independent filesystems are not implicitly covered. 如果没有可用的配置路径，API 则会报告此次重置仅在当前进程内生效。 If no config path is available, the API reports a process-local reset instead.
解析后的扩展配置及其记录的内容摘要来自同一次读取，因此，即使并发编辑之后又进行了保留时间戳的备份恢复，也不会导致不同的修订版本被无限期缓存。 The parsed extensions configuration and its recorded content digest come from the same read, so a racing edit followed by a timestamp-preserving backup restore cannot leave a different revision cached indefinitely.
对全局 `mcpInterceptors` 链的更改会重建每个服务器的工具包装器， Changes to the global `mcpInterceptors` chain rebuild every server's tool wrappers,
即使同时编辑了某个服务器的配置也是如此。 including when a server configuration is edited at the same time.
`extensions_config.json` 支持带有或不带前导字节序标记（BOM）的 UTF-8 编码，包括编辑器以带 BOM 的 UTF-8 格式保存的文件。 `extensions_config.json` accepts UTF-8 with or without a leading byte-order mark (BOM), including files saved as UTF-8 with BOM by an editor.
启用 MCP 工具延迟发现后，`tool_search` 支持通过显式选择使用字面关键词查询，例如 `keywords:notebook jupyter`。 When deferred MCP tool discovery is enabled, `tool_search` supports opt-in literal keyword queries such as `keywords:notebook jupyter`. 关键词以任意顺序匹配名称和描述，先按不同词项的覆盖数量排序，再按名称命中情况排序；排名相同时保留目录顺序。 Keywords match names and descriptions in any order, ranked by distinct term coverage and then name hits; ties retain catalog order. 此模式使用前缀后的前 256 个字符，以及最多 16 个以空白字符分隔的不重复词项，最多返回五个工具。 This mode uses the first 256 characters after the prefix and at most 16 unique whitespace-separated terms, returning up to five tools. 标点符号仍按字面含义处理。 Punctuation stays literal. 不带前缀的查询和 `+required` 排序保留现有的正则表达式语义；`select:tool_a,tool_b` 仍会获取所有名称完全匹配且区分大小写的工具。 Bare queries and `+required` ranking keep their existing regular-expression semantics; `select:tool_a,tool_b` still fetches all exact, case-sensitive name matches.
MCP 路由提示还可以针对匹配的请求优先选择特定的 MCP 工具，同时允许使用其他工具。 MCP routing hints can also prefer a specific MCP tool for matching requests without forbidding other tools. 当 `tool_search` 延迟加载 MCP 模式（schema）时，匹配的路由元数据可在模型调用之前，自动将最多 `tool_search.auto_promote_top_k` 个延迟加载的模式提升为可用状态。 When `tool_search` defers MCP schemas, matching routing metadata can auto-promote up to `tool_search.auto_promote_top_k` deferred schemas before the model call.
延迟加载的 MCP 模式被提升为可用状态后，其有效期持续到当前用户轮次结束。 Deferred MCP schema promotions last for the current user turn. 它们在该轮次的模型／工具循环及检查点恢复过程中保持可用，随后在新的用户消息开启下一轮次时重新变为延迟加载状态；下一轮次必须通过 `tool_search` 再次发现它们，或匹配其路由提示。 They remain active across that turn's model/tool loop and checkpoint resume, then become deferred again when a new user message starts the next turn; the next turn must discover them again through `tool_search` or match their routing hints. 恢复旧版尚在进行中的检查点时，即使路由提示也与该轮次匹配，先前搜索到的工具仍会被保留。 Resuming a legacy in-flight checkpoint preserves previously searched tools even when routing hints also match that turn.
针对延迟加载工具的 `tool_search` 正则表达式查询在整个目录范围内共享 100 ms 的执行时间预算，其中包括 `+required` 排序。 Deferred `tool_search` regex queries share a 100 ms execution budget across the catalog, including `+required` ranking. 模式的长度上限为 256 个字符，可搜索的名称和描述各自的长度上限为 65,536 个字符。 Patterns are limited to 256 characters and searchable names/descriptions to 65,536 characters each. 超出限制的查询会返回重试提示，且不会将部分结果提升为可用状态；请使用更简单的模式、`keywords:`，或通过 `select:` 指定精确名称。 Queries that exceed a limit return a retry hint without promoting partial results; use a simpler pattern, `keywords:`, or `select:` with exact names. 对于超长的目录字段，系统会报告工具名称、字段及其长度，并在 MCP 服务器名称可用时将其一并记入日志；请使用 `keywords:` 或 `select:`，因为简化模式无法缩短目录字段。 Oversized catalog fields are reported with the tool name, field and length, and logged with the MCP server name when available; use `keywords:` or `select:` because simplifying the pattern cannot shrink a catalog field. 精确选择仍不受上限约束，并绕过正则表达式限制；按字面量匹配的 `keywords:` 模式也会绕过正则表达式限制。 Exact selection remains uncapped and bypasses regex limits; the literal `keywords:` mode also bypasses regex limits. 无效的正则表达式语法仍会回退为字面量子串匹配。 Invalid regex syntax still falls back to a literal substring match.
OpenViking 用户可以在 `/mcp` 注册官方 Streamable HTTP 端点 OpenViking users can register the official Streamable HTTP endpoint at `/mcp`
并使用与所有者绑定的 USER API 密钥。 with an owner-bound USER API key. 原生 `forget` 工具的开放是为了 The native `forget` tool is exposed for
保持功能对等；删除操作不可逆，因此只有在获得 capability parity; deletion is irreversible, so it should be called only after
用户明确确认后，才应调用该工具。 explicit user confirmation. DeerFlow 不会强制要求此项确认。 DeerFlow does not enforce that confirmation. 这 This
由模型显式选择的 MCP 工具调用路径，可以与独立的自动化 explicit, model-selected MCP tool path can run alongside the separate automatic
OpenViking 记忆后端并行运行；它不会取代自动轮次采集或记忆检索。 OpenViking memory backend; it does not replace automatic turn capture or recall. 请参阅 See the
[OpenViking MCP 工具配置](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md#openviking-mcp-tools)。 [OpenViking MCP tools configuration](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md#openviking-mcp-tools).
Gateway 可以将 MCP 服务器的普通 `submit` / `status` / `cancel` 工具适配为持久化后台任务。 The Gateway can adapt an MCP server's ordinary `submit` / `status` / `cancel` tools into durable background tasks. Agent 只能看到已配置的提交工具和 DeerFlow 本地任务 ID；远程 ID 会在提交调用返回之前持久化，而状态查询和取消操作则保留在运行时内部。 The Agent sees only the configured submit tool and a DeerFlow-local task ID; remote IDs are persisted before the submit call returns, while status and cancel stay internal to the runtime. 轮询采用跨工作进程租约、指数重试退避、限定作用域的 MCP 会话、有容量限制的结果存储，以及重启恢复机制。 Polling uses cross-worker leases, exponential retry backoff, scoped MCP sessions, bounded result storage, and restart recovery. 状态工具的 `isError` 会作为有大小限制的诊断信息保留，并触发重试；服务器通过包含 `status: "failed"` 的正常结构化结果报告远程任务的永久失败结果。 A status-tool `isError` is retained as a bounded diagnostic and retried; servers report a permanent remote-task outcome through a normal structured result with `status: "failed"`. 远程轮询提示值必须为有限正数，且上限为 24 小时；制品引用 JSON 的大小上限为 64 KiB；任务和服务器标识符在持久化之前，会根据其持久化 SQL 列的限制进行验证。 Remote poll hints are finite positive numbers capped at 24 hours, artifact-reference JSON is limited to 64 KiB, and task/server identifiers are validated against their durable SQL column limits before persistence. 需要输入的状态更新和终态更新会通过幂等的 Agent 运行唤醒当前聊天，而 `list_background_tasks` 和 `cancel_background_task` 则让 Agent 能够管理任务，无须向用户索取远程句柄。 Input-required and terminal updates wake the current chat through idempotent Agent runs, while `list_background_tasks` and `cancel_background_task` let the Agent manage tasks without asking users for remote handles. 可通过 `GET /api/threads/{thread_id}/mcp-tasks`、其详情端点，以及 `POST /api/threads/{thread_id}/mcp-tasks/{task_id}/cancel` 访问当前线程的任务；当任务运行时实际启动后，Web UI 会在聊天页头提供同样安全的本地视图，支持实时状态刷新、取消操作，以及按需查看结果、制品、输入请求、状态错误和取消重试的详细信息。 Current-thread tasks are available through `GET /api/threads/{thread_id}/mcp-tasks`, its detail endpoint, and `POST /api/threads/{thread_id}/mcp-tasks/{task_id}/cancel`; when the task runtime actually starts, the Web UI exposes the same safe local view from the chat header with live status refresh, cancellation, and on-demand result, artifact, input-request, status-error, and cancellation-retry details. 默认禁用该功能的部署和使用内存后端的部署会隐藏该 UI，且不会轮询任务端点。 Default-disabled and memory-backend deployments hide that UI and do not poll the task endpoints. 失败的远程取消操作会保留在队列中，并采用退避机制重试；其最新错误信息（有大小限制）和尝试次数会持续显示在展开的任务卡片中。 A failed remote cancellation remains queued with backoff, and its latest bounded error and attempt count stay visible in the expanded task card. 在 `config.yaml` 中启用 `mcp_tasks`，在 `extensions_config.json` 中使用精确的原始工具名称配置 `task_toolsets`，并使用 SQL 数据库后端（`sqlite` 或 `postgres`）。 Enable `mcp_tasks` in `config.yaml`, configure `task_toolsets` with exact raw tool names in `extensions_config.json`, and use a SQL database backend (`sqlite` or `postgres`). 启用任务功能的服务器若更改连接、身份验证、拦截器、超时或绑定配置，就需要重启 Gateway，以防 Agent 工具发现和后台调用使用不同版本的配置。`input_required` 目前仅用于通知：DeerFlow 可以显示请求，但尚无法将用户的回答提交回远程任务。 Task-enabled server connection, authentication, interceptor, timeout, or binding changes require a Gateway restart so Agent tool discovery and background calls cannot use different configuration versions. `input_required` is notification-only for now: DeerFlow can display the request but cannot yet submit the user's answer back to the remote task.
对于配置了 `task_toolsets` 的部署级 HTTP/SSE 服务器，发现操作、普通 For deployment-level HTTP/SSE servers with `task_toolsets`, discovery, ordinary
工具调用和后台任务调用会在同一个 Gateway tools, and background task calls share OAuth token state within one Gateway
进程内共享 OAuth 令牌状态，即使工具缓存重置后也仍然如此。 process, including across tool-cache resets. 轮换后的刷新令牌保留在 Rotated refresh tokens stay in
内存中；它们不会写回配置，也不会在进程之间共享。 memory; they are not written back to configuration or shared across processes.
重启后，配置中的刷新令牌必须仍然有效。 After a restart, the configured refresh token must still be valid.
通知启动和失败的 Agent 运行投递采用有上限的指数退避机制，显示尝试次数，并在五次尝试失败后停止。 Notification launch and failed Agent-run deliveries use capped exponential backoff with a visible attempt count and stop after five failed attempts. 当有时限的常规释放操作超过其排空截止时间时，服务会继续保有所有权，直到该操作结束。 When a bounded ordinary release exceeds its drain deadline, the service retains ownership until it settles. 对于已被永久拒绝的目标（例如已删除的聊天），会立即将其转入死信队列，而不是无限重试或重新创建。 A permanently rejected target such as a deleted chat is dead-lettered immediately instead of retried forever or recreated. 取消端点在持久化记录请求后即返回；可能较慢的远程 MCP 调用及其重试调度由后台服务负责。 Cancellation endpoints return after durably recording the request; the background service owns the potentially slow remote MCP call and its retry schedule.
通知运行将可信的投递指令与经过边界封装的不可信远程事件载荷分开保存。 Notification runs keep their trusted delivery instruction separate from the framed, untrusted remote event payload. 是否暴露任务管理工具由进程启动时初始化的任务运行时控制，而不是由配置热读取控制，因此更改 `mcp_tasks` 需要重启 Gateway。`list_background_tasks` 和 `cancel_background_task` 是普通业务工具，与其他所有工具一样，受智能体自身的授权机制约束。 The process-started task runtime—not a hot config read—controls whether the task-management tools are exposed, so changing `mcp_tasks` requires a Gateway restart. `list_background_tasks` and `cancel_background_task` are ordinary business tools governed by the agent's own authorization, like every other tool.
详细说明请参阅 [MCP 服务器指南](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md)。 See the [MCP Server Guide](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md) for detailed instructions.

绝不能将凭据放入两个运行元数据位置中的任何一个 credentials must never be placed in either run metadata surface
（`metadata.auth_token` 或 `config.metadata.auth_token`）。 (`metadata.auth_token` or `config.metadata.auth_token`). 请参阅 [MCP 凭据迁移与清理](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md#migrating-legacy-mcp-credentials) See [MCP credential migration and cleanup](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md#migrating-legacy-mcp-credentials)

即时通信渠道IM Channels
DeerFlow 支持从消息应用接收任务。 DeerFlow supports receiving tasks from messaging apps. 渠道在配置后自动启动，所有渠道均无需公网 IP。 Channels auto-start when configured — no public IP required for any of them.
当渠道无法发送附件时，生成文件的回复会对此作出说明。 Generated-file replies explain when a channel cannot send attachments. 如果部分或全部上传失败，只要平台仍接受回复，DeerFlow 就会发送一条合并通知，列出未发送的文件名。 If some or all uploads fail, DeerFlow sends one combined notice naming the unsent files when the platform still accepts replies. 上传成功时不会额外发送重复的确认消息。 Successful uploads add no duplicate confirmation. 文件会保留在 DeerFlow 对话中；回复窗口过期时会记录日志，而不会改用主动消息发送。 Files remain in the DeerFlow conversation; an expired reply window is logged without switching to proactive messaging.
取消渠道重启会丢弃其待处理的配置重载，从而保留此后应用的较新运行时设置。 Cancelling a channel restart discards its pending configuration reload, preserving newer runtime settings applied afterward.
`channels/store.json` 中无效的 UTF-8 内容采用与格式错误的 JSON 相同的恢复策略： Invalid UTF-8 in `channels/store.json` follows the same recovery policy as malformed JSON:
SQL 迁移会发出警告并跳过该文件，保持文件不变，以便渠道能够启动； SQL migration warns and skips the file, leaving it unchanged so channels can start; the
内存后端的 JSON 存储会发出警告，并以空存储启动，在下次写入时替换该文件。 memory backend's JSON store warns and starts empty, replacing the file on its next write.
在 Windows 上，保存 JSON 格式的运行时渠道配置时，会等待持有竞争文件锁的进程释放锁 On Windows, JSON runtime channel-config saves wait for a competing file-lock holder
，且不受操作系统约十秒的截止时间限制。 without the operating system's roughly ten-second cutoff. 当等待达到 50 次重试时 A wait reaching 50 retries
（约十秒），会记录一次锁文件路径；永久性锁错误则会立即导致失败。 (about ten seconds) logs the lock-file path once; permanent lock errors fail immediately.
保存对话绑定、运行时渠道凭据和 WeChat 认证状态时，即使暂存文件清理也失败，仍会保留原始的 Conversation-binding, runtime channel-credential and WeChat auth-state saves preserve the original
持久化失败信息。 persistence failure when staged-file cleanup also fails. 其余清理操作仍会执行，并且警告会 Remaining cleanup still runs, and warnings
指出无法删除的副本。 identify copies that could not be removed. Buzz 会保留失败的快照以供重试，即使 Buzz keeps failed snapshots available for retry, even if
清理或日志记录失败也不例外。 cleanup or logging fails.
启动时，运行时凭据存储和 WeChat 认证存储会删除各自超过 24 小时的 `..deerflow-*.tmp` On startup, runtime credential and WeChat auth stores remove their own `..deerflow-*.tmp`
暂存文件。 staging files older than 24 hours. 新近创建的文件、符号链接和无关文件会予以保留。 Fresh files, symlinks and unrelated files are preserved. 较旧的 Older
匿名 `tmp*.tmp` 文件需要人工检查，因为无法安全地确定其写入者。 anonymous `tmp*.tmp` files require manual inspection because their writer cannot be identified safely.
DeerFlow 还可以在工作区界面中提供用户自有的即时通信渠道连接。 DeerFlow can also expose user-owned IM channel connections in the workspace UI. 启用 `channel_connections` 后，已登录用户可以通过侧边栏或“设置 > 渠道”绑定 Telegram、Slack、Discord、Feishu/Lark、DingTalk、WeChat、WeCom、QQ 或 Buzz。 When `channel_connections` is enabled, logged-in users can bind Telegram, Slack, Discord, Feishu/Lark, DingTalk, WeChat, WeCom, QQ, or Buzz from the sidebar / Settings > Channels. 它复用现有的出站 `channels.*` 传输机制，因此无需公网 IP 或服务提供方回调 URL。 It reuses the existing outbound `channels.*` transports, so no public IP or provider callback URL is required. 随后，收到的即时消息（IM）会在已连接的 DeerFlow 用户账户下运行。 Incoming IM messages then run under the connected DeerFlow user account. 有关设置和安全注意事项，请参阅 [IM 渠道连接](backend/docs/IM_CHANNEL_CONNECTIONS.md)。 See [IM Channel Connections](backend/docs/IM_CHANNEL_CONNECTIONS.md) for setup and security notes. 如果基于时间戳的查询无法继续推进，Buzz 的积压消息分页会停止并发出警告；移除渠道时，会同时关闭实时消息订阅和积压消息订阅。 Buzz backlog paging stops with a warning if timestamp-based queries cannot advance, and channel removal closes both live and backlog subscriptions.

**`config.yaml` 中的配置：** **Configuration in `config.yaml`:**
Discord 的 `channels.discord.allowed_guilds` 接受一个正数形式的服务器（guild） Discord's `channels.discord.allowed_guilds` accepts one positive numeric guild
ID（带引号或不带引号）或一个 YAML 列表。 ID (quoted or unquoted) or a YAML list. 未设置、设为 `null`、`[]` 或空白字符串时， Unset, `null`, `[]`, or a blank string
允许所有服务器。 allows all guilds. 无效条目会被忽略，并发出警告；任何其他 Invalid entries are ignored with a warning; any other
无法产生有效 ID 的配置值都会拒绝所有服务器，并记录错误。 configured value yielding no valid ID denies every guild and logs an error.
`allowed_channels` 接受一个频道 ID（带引号或不带引号），或一个由频道 ID 组成的 YAML 列表， `allowed_channels` accepts one channel ID (quoted or unquoted) or a YAML list

没有任何豁免，因此启用 `mention_only` 后，它会对所有频道生效。 no exemptions, so `mention_only` applies everywhere when enabled.
Discord 入站附件的下载大小上限为 25 MiB。 Discord inbound attachments have a 25 MiB download limit. 文件过大的提示 Oversized-file notices
会显示实际执行的大小上限，以便发送者调整附件大小或压缩附件。 show the enforced limit so senders can resize or compress the attachment. 
channels:
  # LangGraph-compatible Gateway API base URL (default: http://localhost:8001/api)
  langgraph_url: http://localhost:8001/api
  # Gateway API URL (default: http://localhost:8001)
  gateway_url: http://localhost:8001

  # Maximum queued or provider-reserved inbound messages (default: 1000)
  inbound_queue_maxsize: 1000
  # Fixed number of long-lived inbound handler workers (default: 5)
  max_concurrency: 5
  # Seconds to drain accepted work before cancelling active handlers (default: 3)
  shutdown_grace_period_seconds: 3

  # Optional: global session defaults for all mobile channels
  session:
    assistant_id: lead_agent  # or a custom agent name; custom agents are routed via lead_agent + agent_name
    context:
      thinking_enabled: true
      is_plan_mode: false
      subagent_enabled: false

  feishu:
    enabled: true
    app_id: $FEISHU_APP_ID
    app_secret: $FEISHU_APP_SECRET
    # domain: https://open.feishu.cn       # China (default)
    # domain: https://open.larksuite.com   # International

  qq:
    enabled: true
    app_id: $QQ_APP_ID
    client_secret: $QQ_CLIENT_SECRET
    allowed_users: []  # QQ OpenIDs, not QQ account numbers; empty allows all; one ID is one entry

  wecom:
    enabled: true
    bot_id: $WECOM_BOT_ID
    bot_secret: $WECOM_BOT_SECRET
    # Optional: extra host suffixes inbound media downloads may come from, in
    # addition to the built-in qq.com family and WeCom's official COS media
    # host (ww-aibot-img-1258476243.<region>.myqcloud.com); add one here if
    # WeCom rotates to a new COS account or media goes through a proxy
    allowed_media_hosts: []

  slack:
    enabled: true
    bot_token: $SLACK_BOT_TOKEN     # xoxb-...
    app_token: $SLACK_APP_TOKEN     # xapp-... (Socket Mode)
    allowed_users: []               # empty = allow all

  telegram:
    enabled: true
    bot_token: $TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN
    # Optional: render final Markdown replies as Telegram Rich Messages.
    rich_messages: false
    allowed_users: []               # numeric user IDs, not @usernames; empty = allow all

  wechat:
    enabled: false
    bot_token: $WECHAT_BOT_TOKEN
    ilink_bot_id: $WECHAT_ILINK_BOT_ID
    qrcode_login_enabled: true      # optional: allow first-time QR bootstrap when bot_token is absent
    allowed_users: []               # iLink user IDs; empty allows all; one ID is one entry
    polling_timeout: 35             # timing values must be positive finite seconds
    polling_retry_delay: 5
    qrcode_poll_interval: 2
    qrcode_poll_timeout: 180
    state_dir: ./.deer-flow/wechat/state
    max_inbound_image_bytes: 20971520
    max_outbound_image_bytes: 20971520
    max_inbound_file_bytes: 52428800
    max_outbound_file_bytes: 52428800
    # Inbound media downloads stream with the caps above and are restricted to
    # these host suffixes (plus *.qq.com and the cdn_base_url host by default)
    allowed_media_hosts: []

    # Optional: per-channel / per-user session settings
    session:
      assistant_id: mobile-agent  # custom agent names are also supported here
      context:
        thinking_enabled: false
      users:
        "123456789":
          assistant_id: vip-agent
          config:
            recursion_limit: 150
          context:
            thinking_enabled: true
            subagent_enabled: true

  dingtalk:
    enabled: true
    client_id: $DINGTALK_CLIENT_ID             # Client ID of your DingTalk application
    client_secret: $DINGTALK_CLIENT_SECRET     # Client Secret of your DingTalk application
    allowed_users: []                          # empty = allow all
    card_template_id: ""                       # Optional: AI Card template ID for streaming typewriter effect
注意事项： Notes:
`assistant_id: lead_agent` 会直接调用默认的 LangGraph 助手。 `assistant_id: lead_agent` calls the default LangGraph assistant directly.
如果将 `assistant_id` 设置为自定义智能体名称，DeerFlow 仍会通过 `lead_agent` 进行路由，并将该值作为 `agent_name` 注入，从而使自定义智能体的 SOUL／配置在 IM 渠道中生效。 If `assistant_id` is set to a custom agent name, DeerFlow still routes through `lead_agent` and injects that value as `agent_name`, so the custom agent's SOUL/config takes effect for IM channels.
IM 渠道工作进程会在内部调用 Gateway 的 LangGraph 兼容 API，并自动附加进程本地的内部身份验证信息，以及创建线程和运行所需的 CSRF Cookie／请求头对。 IM channel workers call Gateway's LangGraph-compatible API internally and automatically attach process-local internal auth plus the CSRF cookie/header pair required for thread and run creation.
入站工作的规模被限制为 `inbound_queue_maxsize` 条待处理消息，加上 `max_concurrency` 个活跃工作进程。 Inbound work is bounded to `inbound_queue_maxsize` pending messages plus `max_concurrency` active workers. 当容量耗尽时，套接字／轮询提供程序会在发送 DeerFlow 正在处理的确认消息之前丢弃新消息，并发出受频率限制的警告。 When capacity is exhausted, socket/polling providers drop new messages before sending DeerFlow's working acknowledgment and emit a rate-limited warning. Buzz 保持其重放游标不变，并重新连接以进行中继重放；GitHub Webhook 返回 `503`，将此次投递标记为失败，以便通过手动操作或 API 重新投递。 Buzz leaves its replay cursor unchanged and reconnects for relay replay; GitHub webhooks return `503`, marking the delivery failed for manual/API redelivery. 关闭时会立即停止接收新消息，在已接收的消息处理完毕期间保持通道传输可用，最长等待 `shutdown_grace_period_seconds`，随后取消正在运行的处理程序并等待其结束，最后关闭提供方资源；Gateway 的外层超时机制可以取消尚未完成的关闭过程，而不会使这些资源脱离管理。 Shutdown closes admission immediately, keeps channel transports available while accepted messages drain for up to `shutdown_grace_period_seconds`, then cancels and awaits active handlers before closing provider resources; the Gateway's outer timeout can cancel an incomplete shutdown without detaching those resources.
Telegram 的关闭过程会在超时或取消后保留正在进行的清理操作，以便重试时能够完成清理。 Telegram shutdown retains ongoing cleanup across a timeout or cancellation so a retry can finish it. 在启动期间停止时，还会取消初始化，并在终止工作线程的事件循环之前，关闭部分初始化的机器人的 HTTP 客户端。 Stopping during startup also cancels initialization and closes the partially initialized bot's HTTP clients before retiring its worker loop. 如果 Telegram 的事件循环已经停止，关闭过程会警告下载客户端可能仍处于打开状态，并释放已被放弃的清理状态；后续停止操作不会重复发出该警告。 If the Telegram loop has already stopped, shutdown warns that download clients may remain open and releases the abandoned cleanup state; later stops do not repeat that warning.
Slack 停止时，会等待正在建立的 Socket Mode 连接及其清理操作，最长等待五秒。 Slack stop waits up to five seconds for an in-flight Socket Mode connection and its cleanup. 如果清理仍未完成，系统会继续跟踪先前的实例，并可在清理完成后重试启动。 If cleanup is still pending, the previous instance stays tracked and a restart can be retried after it finishes. SDK 清理失败时会报告原始错误，并且仍可重试。 SDK cleanup failures report their original error and remain retryable.
在 Telegram 私聊中，即使所引用的用户消息或机器人消息已被删除，例如在运行期间清空了聊天记录，文本、附件和流式回复（包括“正在处理中……”）仍可送达。 In Telegram private chats, text, attachment and streaming replies (including "Working on it...") can still be delivered if their referenced user or bot message was deleted, such as after clearing chat history during a run. 群组中的回复目标要求保持不变，以保留论坛话题路由。 Group reply-target requirements remain unchanged to preserve forum routing.
在 Telegram 群组中，回复已送达的机器人回复或附件，会延续其对应的 DeerFlow 会话。 In Telegram groups, replying to a delivered bot response or attachment continues its DeerFlow conversation. 回复映射在重启后仍然保留，并且在绑定了用户连接时，其作用域始终限定于该连接。 Reply mappings survive restarts and stay scoped to the user's connection when one is bound. 新的、无关的群组消息仍会启动独立会话；私聊路由保持不变。 New, unrelated group messages still start separate conversations; private-chat routing is unchanged.
Feishu/Lark 现在会按映射到的 DeerFlow `thread_id` 对短时间内连续发送的后续消息进行排队，而不是立即显示通用的忙碌回复；话题回复则为每条消息保留一张卡片，其中包含简洁的源消息预览，并在排队、运行和最终状态的增量更新过程中持续保留该预览。 Feishu/Lark now queues rapid follow-up messages per mapped DeerFlow `thread_id` instead of immediately surfacing the generic busy reply, and topic replies keep a per-message card with a compact source-message preview across queued/running/final patches.
流式即时通信（IM）通道会将后端 `error` 事件视为传输故障，并采用相同的回复和重试流程。 Streaming IM channels treat backend `error` events like transport failures and follow the same reply and retry path. 通道会记录错误类型和错误消息，以便诊断。 The channel logs the error type and message for diagnosis.
在 `.env` 文件中设置相应的 API 密钥： Set the corresponding API keys in your `.env` file: 
# Telegram
TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN=123456789:ABCdefGHIjklMNOpqrSTUvwxYZ

# Slack
SLACK_BOT_TOKEN=xoxb-...
SLACK_APP_TOKEN=xapp-...

# Feishu / Lark
FEISHU_APP_ID=cli_xxxx
FEISHU_APP_SECRET=your_app_secret

# WeChat iLink
WECHAT_BOT_TOKEN=your_ilink_bot_token
WECHAT_ILINK_BOT_ID=your_ilink_bot_id

# WeCom
WECOM_BOT_ID=your_bot_id
WECOM_BOT_SECRET=your_bot_secret

# DingTalk
DINGTALK_CLIENT_ID=your_client_id
DINGTALK_CLIENT_SECRET=your_client_secret

在 `.env` 中设置 `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`，并在 `config.yaml` 中启用该通道。 Set `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` in `.env` and enable the channel in `config.yaml`.
机器人可接收文本、照片和文档（无论是否附带说明文字）。 The bot accepts inbound text, photos, and documents (with or without captions). 托管式 Bot API 的下载限制为每个附件 20 MB。 Hosted Bot API downloads are limited to 20 MB per attachment.

启用 **Socket Mode** → 生成具有 `connections:write` 权限范围的应用级令牌（`xapp-…`）。 Enable **Socket Mode** → generate an App-Level Token (`xapp-…`) with `connections:write` scope.

**Feishu / Lark 配置** **Feishu / Lark Setup**

添加权限：`im:message`、`im:message.p2p_msg:readonly`、`im:resource`。 Add permissions: `im:message`, `im:message.p2p_msg:readonly`, `im:resource`.
在**事件**下，订阅 `im.message.receive_v1` 并选择**长连接**模式。 Under **Events**, subscribe to `im.message.receive_v1` and select **Long Connection** mode.
复制应用 ID 和应用密钥。 Copy the App ID and App Secret. 在 `.env` 中设置 `FEISHU_APP_ID` 和 `FEISHU_APP_SECRET`，并在 `config.yaml` 中启用该通道。 Set `FEISHU_APP_ID` and `FEISHU_APP_SECRET` in `.env` and enable the channel in `config.yaml`.
机器人支持接收文本、图片和文件消息。 The bot supports inbound text, image, and file messages. 传入附件的下载限制为每个附件 20 MB。 Inbound attachment downloads are limited to 20 MB per attachment.
_（可选）_ 交互式澄清卡片：在 `channels.feishu` 下设置 `card_interaction_enabled: true`，使智能体基于选项的澄清问题呈现为可点击的按钮卡片，并保留问题前的所有背景信息。 _(Optional)_ Interactive clarification cards: set `card_interaction_enabled: true` under `channels.feishu` to let the agent's option-based clarifications render as clickable button cards, preserving any background context before the question. 然后，在开发者控制台中打开**回调配置**（回调配置——与**事件订阅**分开的独立选项卡），以**长连接**模式订阅 `card.action.trigger`，并发布新的应用版本。 Then, in the developer console, open **Callback Configuration** (回调配置 — a separate tab from **Event Subscriptions**), subscribe to `card.action.trigger` in **Long Connection** mode, and publish a new app version. 点击按钮即可完成澄清，其效果与输入选项完全相同；只有触发该澄清的用户才能操作对应卡片。 A click resolves the clarification exactly like typing the choice; only the user who triggered the clarification can operate its card. 通过按钮提交的回答会保存在会话历史记录中，后续澄清会在同一线程中继续进行。 Button answers are saved in the conversation history, and subsequent clarifications continue in the same thread.
如果 SDK 反复记录 `CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED`，请参阅[飞书 / Lark TLS 故障排查](backend/docs/IM_CHANNEL_CONNECTIONS.md#feishu--lark-tls-troubleshooting)，检查 Gateway 的 CA 信任配置。 If the SDK repeatedly logs `CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED`, see [Feishu / Lark TLS troubleshooting](backend/docs/IM_CHANNEL_CONNECTIONS.md#feishu--lark-tls-troubleshooting) to check the Gateway's CA trust configuration.
定时任务的结果和生命周期通知也可以推送给已绑定的飞书/Lark 用户，无需新的入站消息。 Scheduled-task result and lifecycle notices can also be pushed to a bound Feishu/Lark user, without a new inbound message. 这些通知使用与企业微信相同的通知发件箱和本地化摘要。 They use the same notification outbox and localized summaries as WeCom. 应用必须能够向该用户发送消息；请参阅[飞书定时任务通知](backend/docs/IM_CHANNEL_CONNECTIONS.md#feishu-scheduled-task-notifications)。 The app must be able to send to that user; see [Feishu scheduled-task notifications](backend/docs/IM_CHANNEL_CONNECTIONS.md#feishu-scheduled-task-notifications).
**微信配置** **WeChat Setup**
在 `config.yaml` 中启用 `wechat` 通道。 Enable the `wechat` channel in `config.yaml`.
在 `.env` 中设置 `WECHAT_BOT_TOKEN`，或设置 `qrcode_login_enabled: true`，以通过二维码完成首次初始化。 Either set `WECHAT_BOT_TOKEN` in `.env`, or set `qrcode_login_enabled: true` for first-time QR bootstrap.
当未设置 `bot_token` 且已启用二维码初始化时，请查看后端日志中 iLink 返回的二维码内容，并完成绑定流程。 When `bot_token` is absent and QR bootstrap is enabled, watch backend logs for the QR content returned by iLink and complete the binding flow.
二维码流程成功后，DeerFlow 会将获取的令牌持久化保存到 `state_dir` 下，以供后续重启时使用。 After the QR flow succeeds, DeerFlow persists the acquired token under `state_dir` for later restarts.
对于 Docker Compose 部署，请将 `state_dir` 放在持久卷上，以便在重启后保留 `get_updates_buf` 游标和已保存的认证状态。 For Docker Compose deployments, keep `state_dir` on a persistent volume so the `get_updates_buf` cursor and saved auth state survive restarts.
发送图片/文件时，会在读取过程中强制执行 `max_outbound_image_bytes` / `max_outbound_file_bytes` 限制（默认值为 20 MiB / 50 MiB），包括解析定位后仍继续增大的文件。 Outbound images/files enforce `max_outbound_image_bytes` / `max_outbound_file_bytes` (20 MiB / 50 MiB defaults) while reading, including files that grow after resolution. 超限的读取会在加密/上传前被拒绝，而不会发送截断后的前缀部分。 Oversize reads are rejected before encryption/upload instead of sending a truncated prefix. 非正数限制值会禁用相应的大小上限。 Non-positive limits disable the corresponding cap.
`allowed_users` 接受 iLink 用户 ID。 `allowed_users` takes iLink user IDs. 未设置、设为 `null`、`[]` 或空白字符串时，允许所有用户访问。 Unset, `null`, `[]`, or a blank string allows everyone. 单个 ID 被视为一个条目，而不是字符序列；未加引号且值为整数的数字会以该整数的文本形式存储。 A single ID is one entry, not a sequence of characters, and an unquoted integer-valued number is stored as that integer's text. 包含逗号或内部空白字符的标量字符串会触发警告日志，但仍被视为一个字面 ID；如需指定多个 ID，请使用 YAML 列表。 A scalar string containing commas or interior whitespace logs a warning but remains one literal ID; use a YAML list for multiple IDs. 无效条目会被忽略并记录警告；除上述允许所有用户的情况外，任何配置值若无法产生有效 ID，都会拒绝所有用户并记录错误。`/connect` 仍会在此项检查之前被接受，而被拒绝的发送者的消息会在下载入站媒体之前被丢弃。 Invalid entries are ignored with a warning; any other configured value that yields no valid ID denies every user and logs an error. `/connect` is still accepted before that check, and a denied sender is dropped before inbound media is downloaded.
关闭时会等待正在进行的游标写入完成。 Shutdown waits for in-flight cursor writes. 令牌过期时，DeerFlow 会先持久化保存游标重置结果并删除已保存的令牌，然后完成轮询器的取消操作。 On token expiry, DeerFlow persists the cursor reset and removes the saved token before completing poller cancellation.
即使同一条消息内或不同消息之间的文件名相同，入站附件也会分别保留独立的暂存副本。 Inbound attachments keep separate staged copies even when filenames match within one message or across messages. 其原始文件名会被保留，用于上传摄取。 Their original filenames are preserved for upload ingestion. 私有暂存副本会在摄取完成后，或消息被丢弃或处理失败时删除。 Private staged copies are removed after ingestion or when a message is dropped or fails.

**钉钉配置** **DingTalk Setup**
在[钉钉开发者控制台](https://open.dingtalk.com/)中创建钉钉应用，并启用**机器人**能力。 Create a DingTalk application in the [DingTalk Developer Console](https://open.dingtalk.com/) and enable **Robot** capability.

*（可选）* 如需启用流式 AI 卡片回复（打字机效果），请在[钉钉卡片平台](https://open.dingtalk.com/document/dingstart/typewriter-effect-streaming-ai-card)上创建 **AI 卡片**模板，然后将 `config.yaml` 中的 `card_template_id` 设置为该模板 ID。 *(Optional)* To enable streaming AI Card replies (typewriter effect), create an **AI Card** template on the [DingTalk Card Platform](https://open.dingtalk.com/document/dingstart/typewriter-effect-streaming-ai-card), then set `card_template_id` in `config.yaml` to the template ID. 你还需要申请 `Card.Streaming.Write` 和 `Card.Instance.Write` 权限。 You also need to apply for the `Card.Streaming.Write` and `Card.Instance.Write` permissions.
钉钉的 Markdown 回退显示机制会将表格显示为带列标签的块引用，保留单元格内转义的竖线（`\|`）以及开头或结尾的空单元格，使各值仍位于正确的列标签下。 DingTalk's Markdown fallback displays tables as labeled blockquotes, preserving escaped pipes (`\|`) within cells and empty leading or trailing cells so values stay under the correct column labels.
当提供方的令牌有效期无效或不超过五分钟的提前刷新时间时，钉钉令牌缓存会采用两小时的回退有效期，避免每次请求都刷新令牌。 DingTalk token caching uses a two-hour fallback when the provider's token lifetime is invalid or no longer than the five-minute refresh margin, avoiding a refresh on every request.
当 DeerFlow 在 Docker Compose 中运行时，即时通信（IM）通道在 `gateway` 容器内执行。 When DeerFlow runs in Docker Compose, IM channels execute inside the `gateway` container. 在这种情况下，不要将 `channels.langgraph_url` 或 `channels.gateway_url` 指向 `localhost`；请使用容器服务名称，例如 `http://gateway:8001/api` 和 `http://gateway:8001`，或者设置 `DEER_FLOW_CHANNELS_LANGGRAPH_URL` 和 `DEER_FLOW_CHANNELS_GATEWAY_URL`。 In that case, do not point `channels.langgraph_url` or `channels.gateway_url` at `localhost`; use container service names such as `http://gateway:8001/api` and `http://gateway:8001`, or set `DEER_FLOW_CHANNELS_LANGGRAPH_URL` and `DEER_FLOW_CHANNELS_GATEWAY_URL`.
**命令** **Commands**
连接渠道后，你就可以直接在聊天中与 DeerFlow 交互： Once a channel is connected, you can interact with DeerFlow directly from the chat: 
| Command | Description |
| `/new` | Start a new conversation |
| `/status` | Show current thread info |
| `/models` | List available models |
| `/model [name\|default]` | Show or pin the current conversation's model |
| `/memory` | View memory |
| `/agent list` | List your Custom Agents |
| `/agent use ` | Start a new conversation with a Custom Agent |
| `/help` | Show help |
> 不带命令前缀的消息会被视为普通聊天消息——DeerFlow 会创建一个线程，并以对话方式回应。 > Messages without a command prefix are treated as regular chat — DeerFlow creates a thread and responds conversationally.
智能体选择的作用域为会话：`/agent use ` 会开启一个新会话，并在线程元数据中固定使用该自定义智能体（Custom Agent）。 Agent selection is conversation-scoped: `/agent use ` starts a fresh conversation and pins that Custom Agent in the thread metadata. 现有会话绝不会中途切换智能体，所选智能体在 Gateway 重启后仍会保留，而且在 Web UI 中打开由即时通信（IM）创建的线程时，仍会通过同一个自定义智能体继续交互。 Existing conversations never switch agents midway, the selection survives a Gateway restart, and opening the IM-created thread in the Web UI continues through the same Custom Agent.
使用 `/agent use lead_agent` 可在新会话中恢复使用默认智能体。 Use `/agent use lead_agent` to return to the default agent in a new conversation.
模型选择的作用域同样为会话：`/model ` 会为*当前*会话固定一个模型——该模型会根据调用方可见的模型列表进行验证，并持久化到线程元数据中，因此在 Gateway 重启后仍会保留，且从下一条消息开始生效，无需开启新会话。`/model` 显示实际生效的模型及其来源，`/model default` 清除固定设置，而 `/models` 报告已固定的模型。 Model selection is conversation-scoped too: `/model ` pins a model to the *current* conversation — validated against the caller-visible model list, persisted in the thread metadata so it survives a Gateway restart, and applied from the next message without starting a new conversation. `/model` shows the effective model and its source, `/model default` clears the pin, and `/models` reports the pinned model.
请求追踪关联Request Trace Correlation
每个 Gateway HTTP 响应都带有一个 `X-Trace-Id` 标头。 Every Gateway HTTP response carries an `X-Trace-Id` header. 该 id 继承自 The id is inherited
传入的 `X-Trace-Id`（如果调用方发送了该标头），否则会生成一个，因此 from an inbound `X-Trace-Id` when the caller sends one and generated otherwise, so
代理或上游服务可以跨服务固定使用同一个 id。 a proxy or an upstream service can pin one id across services. 它无需 It needs no
配置，也无法关闭。 configuration and cannot be turned off.
对于在 HTTP 响应结束后仍继续执行的工作，同一个 id 仍会与之关联：脱离请求独立执行的 The same id stays attached to work that outlives the HTTP response: the detached
运行任务、该任务委派的任何子智能体，以及后台内存更新线程。 run task, any subagents it delegates to, and the background memory-update threads.
它以 `deerflow_trace_id` 的名称记录在运行记录中（可通过 runs API 查看）、 It is recorded as `deerflow_trace_id` on the run record (visible in the runs API),
线程的检查点元数据中，以及 Langfuse 追踪记录中。 in the thread's checkpoint metadata, and in Langfuse traces. 定时任务、MCP Scheduled tasks, MCP
任务通知运行以及 IM 渠道消息均在 HTTP 之外启动，并在每次发生时生成 task notification runs, and IM channel messages start outside HTTP and mint their
各自的 id。 own id per occurrence.
只有启用增强日志记录时，日志记录才会携带该 id： Log records carry that id only when enhanced logging is on: 
logging:
  enhance:
    enabled: true   # print trace_id into log records
    format: text    # or json
此功能默认关闭，因为启用它会改变日志格式。`logging` 的更改 This is off by default because turning it on changes the log format. `logging` is
需要重启才能生效，因此请编辑 `config.yaml` 并重启 Gateway。 restart-required, so edit `config.yaml` and restart the Gateway. 该设置 The setting
仅影响日志输出——id、响应标头和运行元数据均 affects log output only — the id, the response header, and the run metadata are
不受影响。 unaffected.
`deerflow_trace_id` 是 DeerFlow 的关联 id：它不是运行 id，也不是 `deerflow_trace_id` is a DeerFlow correlation id: it is not a run id, and it is not
提供方的原生追踪 id。 a provider's native trace id. 它也不是查找键——没有任何机制可以根据它解析出 It is not a lookup key either — nothing resolves a
线程或运行；请用它来关联日志行。 thread or a run from it; use it to correlate log lines. 如果在运行请求的 A `deerflow_trace_id` sent
`metadata` 或 `config.context` 中发送 `deerflow_trace_id`，它会被忽略并覆盖，因此 in a run request's `metadata` or `config.context` is ignored and overwritten, so
响应标头、日志和持久化的运行记录绝不会出现不一致。 the response header, the logs, and the persisted run can never disagree. 要固定一个 To pin a
关联 id，请发送 `X-Trace-Id` 标头。 correlation id, send the `X-Trace-Id` header.
Gateway 的运行历史还会为每次运行记录一条终态 `run.delivery` 回执， Gateway run history also records one terminal `run.delivery` receipt per run,
其中包括无输出的运行和崩溃后恢复的运行。 including zero-output and crash-recovered runs. 该回执会在以下操作之前持久化 The receipt is persisted before
正常执行期间持久保存的运行终态。 the durable terminal run status during normal execution. 孤儿运行恢复首先 Orphan recovery first
以原子方式取得已过期租约的所有权，然后以幂等方式回填回执， atomically claims an expired lease and then idempotently backfills the receipt,
从而使过时的恢复扫描无法覆盖仍在进行的运行的详细交付事实。 so a stale recovery scan cannot overwrite a live run's detailed delivery facts.
事件存储发生故障期间，回执持久化仍采用尽力而为的方式。 Receipt persistence remains best-effort during an event-store outage. 对于以下运行： Runs that
未通过检查点预检（或在等待先前的 fail checkpoint preflight (or are cancelled while waiting for prior
终结处理时被取消），仍保持现有的完成数据处理行为：它们会收到 finalization) keep the existing completion-data behavior: they receive the
零交付回执，但不会用 zero-delivery receipt but do not overwrite RunStore completion fields with an
空快照覆盖 RunStore 的完成字段。 empty snapshot.
当 `tool_progress.enabled` 为 true 时，同一运行事件历史还会记录 When `tool_progress.enabled` is true, the same run event history also records
结果质量防护的阶段变化。 result-quality guard phase changes. 它会记录循环检测决策，以及 It records loop-detection decisions and
主智能体和普通任务 deferred MCP tool promotions for both the lead agent and ordinary task

事件会标明新提升的延迟加载工具名称，以及这些工具是由路由元数据还是 events identify newly promoted deferred-tool names and whether routing metadata or
`tool_search` 选中的，而不会将搜索查询、路由关键词、 `tool_search` selected them, without copying the search query, routing keywords,
模式、参数、结果或目录哈希复制到提升事件本身中。 schemas, arguments, results, or catalog hash into the promotion event itself.
LangSmith 追踪LangSmith Tracing
DeerFlow 内置了 [LangSmith](https://smith.langchain.com) 集成，用于提供可观测性。 DeerFlow has built-in [LangSmith](https://smith.langchain.com) integration for observability. 启用后，所有 LLM 调用、智能体运行和工具执行都会被追踪，并可在 LangSmith 仪表板中查看。 When enabled, all LLM calls, agent runs, and tool executions are traced and visible in the LangSmith dashboard.
将以下内容添加到你的 `.env` 文件中： Add the following to your `.env` file: 
LANGSMITH_TRACING=true
LANGSMITH_ENDPOINT=https://api.smith.langchain.com
LANGSMITH_API_KEY=lsv2_pt_xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx
LANGSMITH_PROJECT=xxx
Langfuse 追踪Langfuse Tracing
DeerFlow 还支持通过 [Langfuse](https://langfuse.com) 为兼容 LangChain 的运行提供可观测性。 DeerFlow also supports [Langfuse](https://langfuse.com) observability for LangChain-compatible runs.
将以下内容添加到你的 `.env` 文件中： Add the following to your `.env` file: 
LANGFUSE_TRACING=true
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=pk-lf-xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=sk-lf-xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=https://cloud.langfuse.com
如果你使用自行托管的 Langfuse 实例，请将 `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL` 设置为你的部署 URL。 If you are using a self-hosted Langfuse instance, set `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL` to your deployment URL.
**追踪关联字段。** 每次智能体运行都会附加 Langfuse 的保留追踪属性，以便 Sessions 和 Users 页面自动显示相应信息： **Trace correlation fields.** Every agent run is annotated with Langfuse's reserved trace attributes so the Sessions and Users pages light up automatically:
`session_id` = LangGraph `thread_id` — 将同一对话的所有追踪归为一组 `session_id` = LangGraph `thread_id` — groups every trace of the same conversation
`user_id` = `get_effective_user_id()` 返回的有效用户（在无认证模式下回退为 `default`） `user_id` = effective user from `get_effective_user_id()` (falls back to `default` in no-auth mode)
`trace_name` = 助手 id（默认为 `lead-agent`） `trace_name` = assistant id (defaults to `lead-agent`)
`tags` = `[env:, model:]`（内置 Gateway 主智能体和嵌入式客户端的运行会在解析默认模型或回退模型后，为所选模型添加标签；自定义图工厂可能仅提供请求的模型；未设置环境时省略环境标签） `tags` = `[env:, model:]` (built-in Gateway lead-agent and embedded client runs tag the selected model after default or fallback resolution; custom graph factories may only provide the requested model; the environment tag is omitted when unset)
`metadata.deerflow_trace_id` = DeerFlow 请求关联 id，启用请求追踪关联时与 `X-Trace-Id` 一致 `metadata.deerflow_trace_id` = DeerFlow request correlation id, matching `X-Trace-Id` when request trace correlation is enabled
对于网关路径（`runtime/runs/worker.py::run_agent`）和嵌入式路径（`client.py::DeerFlowClient.stream`），这些字段都会在图调用的根层级注入 `RunnableConfig.metadata`，因此任何兼容 LangChain 的回调都可以读取它们。 These are injected into `RunnableConfig.metadata` at the graph invocation root for both the gateway path (`runtime/runs/worker.py::run_agent`) and the embedded path (`client.py::DeerFlowClient.stream`), so any LangChain-compatible callback can read them. 设置 `DEER_FLOW_ENV`（或 `ENVIRONMENT`），以按部署环境为追踪添加标签。 Set `DEER_FLOW_ENV` (or `ENVIRONMENT`) to tag traces by deployment environment.
Monocle 追踪Monocle Tracing
DeerFlow 还支持 [Monocle](https://github.com/monocle2ai/monocle)，这是一款面向智能体应用、基于 OpenTelemetry 的追踪器。 DeerFlow also supports [Monocle](https://github.com/monocle2ai/monocle), an OpenTelemetry-based tracer for agentic applications. 它会端到端记录每次运行：LLM 调用、智能体步骤，以及工具和 MCP 调用，同时记录其输入、输出、耗时和 token 数量。 It records each run end-to-end: LLM calls, agent steps, and tool and MCP invocations, with their inputs, outputs, timings, and token counts.
将以下内容添加到你的 `.env` 文件中： Add the following to your `.env` file: 
MONOCLE_TRACING=true
MONOCLE_EXPORTERS=file          # file, console, okahu, s3, blob, gcs (default: file)
OKAHU_API_KEY=okh_xxxxxxxx      # required only for the `okahu` exporter
每次运行都会向 `.monocle/` 写入一个追踪文件；在 [Monocle VS Code 扩展](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=OkahuAI.monocle-apptrace)中打开该文件，即可查看跨度（span）时间线和 token 数量。 Each run writes one trace file to `.monocle/`; open it in the [Monocle VS Code extension](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=OkahuAI.monocle-apptrace) to inspect the span timeline and token counts. 连接到智能体可观测性平台 [Okahu](https://www.okahu.ai)，以分析不同运行中的追踪记录，并开展基于追踪的评估和智能体式评估（通过 `okahu` 导出器）。 Connect to [Okahu](https://www.okahu.ai), an agent-observability platform, to analyze traces across runs and run trace-based and agentic evaluations (via the `okahu` exporter).
追踪记录会原样捕获跨度（span）的输入和输出——提示词、工具参数和模型响应——以及 token 用量和耗时。 Traces capture span inputs and outputs verbatim — prompts, tool arguments, and model responses — plus token usage and timings. `file` 导出器将这些数据保存在本地磁盘上，且从不轮换或清理，因此请定期清理 `.monocle/`；远程导出器（`okahu`、`s3`、`blob`、`gcs`）会将相同的数据发送到本机之外，因此仅启用你信任的目标位置。 The `file` exporter keeps them on local disk and never rotates or cleans them up, so prune `.monocle/` periodically; the remote exporters (`okahu`, `s3`, `blob`, `gcs`) send that same data off-box, so enable only destinations you trust. Monocle 在 Gateway 启动时初始化一次：配置错误（未知导出器、缺少 `OKAHU_API_KEY`）会在此时记录到日志中，追踪功能将保持关闭，直到 Gateway 重启。 Monocle is initialized once at Gateway startup: a configuration error (unknown exporter, missing `OKAHU_API_KEY`) is logged there and tracing stays off until the Gateway restarts.
使用多个提供商Using Multiple Providers
LangSmith 和 Langfuse 以 LangChain 回调的形式接入，因此可以同时启用两者，DeerFlow 会将每次运行报告给两者。 LangSmith and Langfuse attach as LangChain callbacks, so you can enable both and DeerFlow reports each run to both. 如果已启用的提供商缺少必需的凭据或初始化失败，DeerFlow 会立即报错并指出该提供商。 If an enabled provider is missing required credentials or fails to initialize, DeerFlow fails fast and names it. Monocle 使用全局 OpenTelemetry 提供器而非回调；Langfuse 共享该提供器，因此三者可以同时运行。 Monocle uses a global OpenTelemetry provider rather than a callback; Langfuse shares that provider, so all three can run together. 由于两个跨度处理器都挂载于同一个共享提供器，当 Monocle 和 Langfuse 同时启用时，Monocle 的导出器也会接收到 Langfuse 的跨度。 Because both span processors sit on the same shared provider, Monocle's exporters also see Langfuse's spans when both are enabled.
对于 Docker 部署，追踪功能默认关闭。 For Docker deployments, tracing is disabled by default. 在 `.env` 中设置 `LANGSMITH_TRACING=true` 和 `LANGSMITH_API_KEY` 即可启用。 Set `LANGSMITH_TRACING=true` and `LANGSMITH_API_KEY` in your `.env` to enable it.

从深度研究到超级智能体运行框架From Deep Research to Super Agent Harness
DeerFlow 最初是一个深度研究框架——社区在此基础上大展身手。 DeerFlow started as a Deep Research framework — and the community ran with it. 自发布以来，开发者已将其用途拓展到远超研究的领域：构建数据流水线、生成演示文稿、快速搭建仪表板、自动化内容工作流。 Since launch, developers have pushed it far beyond research: building data pipelines, generating slide decks, spinning up dashboards, automating content workflows. 这些都是我们从未预料到的用途。 Things we never anticipated.
这让我们认识到一个重要事实：DeerFlow 不仅仅是一个研究工具。 That told us something important: DeerFlow wasn't just a research tool. 它是一个**智能体运行支撑系统（harness）**——一种为智能体提供基础设施、使其能够真正完成工作的运行时。 It was a **harness** — a runtime that gives agents the infrastructure to actually get work done.
因此，我们从头重构了它。 So we rebuilt it from scratch.
DeerFlow 2.0 不再是一个需要你自行组装的框架。 DeerFlow 2.0 is no longer a framework you wire together. 它是一个超级智能体运行支撑系统——功能完备，且可全面扩展。 It's a super agent harness — batteries included, fully extensible. 它基于 LangGraph 和 LangChain 构建，开箱即用地提供智能体所需的一切：文件系统、记忆、技能、具备沙箱感知能力的执行机制，以及为复杂的多步骤任务制定计划并创建子智能体的能力。 Built on LangGraph and LangChain, it ships with everything an agent needs out of the box: a filesystem, memory, skills, sandbox-aware execution, and the ability to plan and spawn sub-agents for complex, multi-step tasks.
可以直接使用。 Use it as-is. 也可以将其拆解，按自己的需求改造。 Or tear it apart and make it yours.
