技能使 DeerFlow 能够完成*几乎任何事情*。 Skills are what make DeerFlow do *almost anything*.
标准的智能体技能（Agent Skill）是一种结构化的能力模块，即一个定义工作流、最佳实践以及支持资源引用的 Markdown 文件。 A standard Agent Skill is a structured capability module — a Markdown file that defines a workflow, best practices, and references to supporting resources. DeerFlow 内置了用于研究、报告生成、幻灯片制作、网页制作、图像和视频生成等任务的技能。 DeerFlow ships with built-in skills for research, report generation, slide creation, web pages, image and video generation, and more. 但真正强大之处在于可扩展性：可以添加自己的技能、替换内置技能，或将它们组合成复合工作流。 But the real power is extensibility: add your own skills, replace the built-in ones, or combine them into compound workflows.
视频生成技能根据 `.png`、 The video-generation skill derives reference-image MIME metadata from `.png`,
`.jpg`、`.jpeg`、`.webp` 和 `.gif` 文件扩展名（不区分大小写）推导参考图像的 MIME 元数据。 `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.webp`, and `.gif` filename extensions (case-insensitive). 一个 A
提交的参考图像若缺少扩展名或扩展名无法识别，就会引发明确的错误 submitted reference with a missing or unrecognized extension raises a clear error
，发生在提交至 API 之前。 before API submission. 文件名必须与其内容相符；图像字节不会被 Filenames must match their contents; image bytes are not
验证或转换，且提供商特有的格式限制仍然适用。 validated or converted, and provider-specific format restrictions still apply.
当提供商返回格式错误、空白或无法识别的音频时，[音乐生成技能](skills/public/music-generation/SKILL.md#output-handling)会保留现有输出。 The [music-generation skill](skills/public/music-generation/SKILL.md#output-handling) preserves existing output when the provider returns malformed, empty or unrecognized audio.
随附的 skill-creator 触发评估器会持续读取 CLI 输出，直至文件结束（EOF），因此，已完成的子进程不会导致已记录的技能触发被误判为假阴性。 The bundled skill-creator trigger evaluator drains CLI output through EOF, so a completed subprocess does not turn a recorded skill trigger into a false negative.
随附的 skill-creator 描述优化器采用与技能验证一致的方式读取 YAML 前置元数据，保留带引号的字符串和多行描述。 The bundled skill-creator description optimizer reads YAML frontmatter consistently with skill validation, preserving quoted strings and multiline descriptions. 其脚本需要 PyYAML，技能包验证器也同样需要。 Its scripts require PyYAML, as does the skill package validator. 无效的技能元数据会通过 stderr 报告，并返回非零退出状态，而不是输出回溯信息。 Invalid skill metadata is reported on stderr with a nonzero exit status rather than a traceback.
技能采用渐进式加载——仅在任务需要时加载，而非一次性全部加载。 Skills are loaded progressively — only when the task needs them, not all at once. 这使上下文窗口保持精简，让 DeerFlow 即使搭配对 token 用量敏感的模型也能良好运行。 This keeps the context window lean and makes DeerFlow work well even with token-sensitive models.
内置的视频生成命令会将失败信息输出到 stderr，并以 The bundled video-generation command reports failures on stderr and exits with
状态码 1 退出，包括所选提供商未配置 API 密钥的情况。 status 1, including when the selected provider has no API key. 调用方可以检查 Callers can check
退出状态，然后再展示输出文件。 the exit status before presenting an output file.
内置的播客生成命令会返回非零退出状态，并在 The bundled podcast-generation command returns a nonzero exit status and writes
生成失败时将诊断信息写入 stderr。 diagnostics to stderr when generation fails. 调用方可以先检查状态，再 Callers can check the status before
展示音频；已有的 MP3 或生成的转录文本并不能证明生成成功。 presenting audio; an existing MP3 or generated transcript is not proof of success.
内置的图表可视化命令在遇到未知图表工具、 The bundled chart-visualization command does the same for an unknown chart tool,
缺少 `tool` 的规格说明或图表服务 HTTP 请求失败时，也会采取同样的处理方式。 a spec missing `tool`, or a chart-service HTTP failure. 成功生成的图表 URL， Successful chart URLs,
包括电子表格（`generate_spreadsheet`）的 URL，仍输出到 stdout。 including spreadsheets (`generate_spreadsheet`), stay on stdout. 对于成败混合的批次， A mixed batch
仍会打印成功生成的图表的 URL，然后以状态码 1 退出。 still prints URLs for the charts that succeeded, then exits with status 1.
启用延迟技能发现时，`describe_skill` 会根据名称和描述中经过 Unicode 规范化且有长度限制的意图词覆盖情况，对已安装的技能进行排序。 When deferred skill discovery is enabled, `describe_skill` ranks installed skills by bounded, Unicode-normalized intent-term coverage across names and descriptions. 因此，自然表述的多词请求无需包含某个完全匹配的短语，也能找到相关技能，同时仍可使用精确的 `select:` 查询和要求名称匹配的 `+prefix` 查询。 Natural multi-term requests can therefore find a relevant skill without requiring one exact phrase, while exact `select:` and required-name `+prefix` lookups remain available. 排序搜索最多使用 256 个字符，最多返回五个结果；精确的 `select:` 列表不会被截断，并会返回目录中所有被请求的匹配项。 Ranked searches use up to 256 characters and return up to five results; exact `select:` lists are not truncated and return all requested catalog matches.
技能目录构成一个包边界：DeerFlow 一旦找到其 `SKILL.md`，该包下嵌套的 `SKILL.md` 文件（例如评估测试夹具）便仍被视为辅助数据，不会注册为运行时技能。 A skill directory is a package boundary: once DeerFlow finds its `SKILL.md`, nested `SKILL.md` files under that package (for example evaluation fixtures) remain supporting data and are not registered as runtime skills. 这既适用于托管集成包，也适用于公共技能和自定义技能。 This applies to managed integration packs as well as public and custom skills. 没有自身 `SKILL.md` 的命名空间目录仍可用于组织嵌套技能。 Namespace directories without their own `SKILL.md` can still group nested skills.
发现过程会跟随运维人员管理的目录符号链接，但会跳过指回祖先目录的链接，以免循环命名空间反复扫描同一目录树。 Discovery follows operator-managed directory symlinks, but skips links back to an ancestor directory so a cyclic namespace does not repeatedly rescan the same tree. 仍支持指向同一外部技能目录树的独立链接。 Independent links to the same external skill tree remain supported.
技能 Markdown 文件和内置文本资源使用 UTF-8。 Skill Markdown and bundled text resources use UTF-8. 技能创建器的 CLI 和审查实用工具会显式使用 UTF-8 读写文本，以确保本地化技能在不同操作系统上的行为一致。 Skill-creator CLI and review utilities read and write text explicitly as UTF-8 so localized skills behave consistently across operating systems.
导出独立技能时，可以从任意目录通过文件路径运行内置技能创建器的 `scripts/package_skill.py`，也可以在技能创建器目录中以 `python -m scripts.package_skill` 运行。 For standalone skill exports, the bundled skill creator's `scripts/package_skill.py` can be run by file path from any directory, or as `python -m scripts.package_skill` from the skill-creator directory. 这两个入口都会在写入 `.skill` 归档文件之前验证技能。 Both entry points validate the skill before writing a `.skill` archive.
自定义技能的写入和安全扫描的暂存处理会保留所提交的换行符， Custom-skill writes and security-scan staging preserve submitted line endings
在所有平台上均如此，因此扫描器检查的是将要存储的字节。 on every platform, so the scanner checks the bytes that will be stored.
技能创建器在汇总基准测试结果时会使用 `timing.json` 中记录的 token 计数，即使 Skill-creator benchmark aggregation uses recorded `timing.json` token counts even
`grading.json` 已包含运行时长也是如此。 when `grading.json` already contains the run duration. 记录为零的计数会被 Recorded zero counts are
保留；只有在缺少 token 数据时，输出字符数才仍作为旧版替代指标使用。 preserved; output character counts remain a legacy proxy only when token data is missing.
如果 `timing.json` 数据不可读、不是 UTF-8 编码、格式错误或不是对象，则会发出警告， Unreadable, non-UTF-8, malformed, or non-object `timing.json` data emits a warning
并将其视为缺失的测量数据。 and is treated as missing measurements. 其 token 和时长字段接受有限的、 Its token and duration fields accept finite,
非负数值（包括零）；无效字段会触发警告，指出文件 non-negative numbers (including zero); invalid fields emit a warning naming the file
和字段名称，并独立使用现有的回退机制，同时保留有效的测量数据 and field and use the existing fallback independently, preserving valid measurements
及其他运行的数据。 and other runs.
`SKILL.md` 的 YAML 前置元数据必须以独占一行的 `---` 开始和结束（允许尾随空格或制表符）。 `SKILL.md` YAML frontmatter must open and close with `---` on its own line (trailing spaces or tabs are allowed). 像 `---suffix` 这样的行不是结束分隔符。 A line such as `---suffix` is not a closing delimiter.
自定义技能历史记录会保留已保存内容和元数据中的 Unicode 行分隔符，使这些修订版本在历史记录查看和回滚时仍然可读。 Custom skill history preserves Unicode line separators inside saved content and metadata, keeping those revisions readable for history inspection and rollback. 格式错误的 JSON 历史记录仍会被拒绝。 Malformed JSON history records are still rejected.
随附的 `github-deep-research` API 客户端在没有 `requests` 的情况下也能运行，使用的是 The bundled `github-deep-research` API client also works without `requests`, using
Python 标准库中的 HTTP 客户端。 Python's standard-library HTTP client. 其备用实现会保留空格、Unicode 字符以及 Its fallback preserves spaces, Unicode, and
搜索查询和标签筛选条件中的保留字符。 reserved characters in search queries and label filters.
文件读取接受未经处理的仓库相对路径，并保留空格、Unicode 字符、 File reads accept raw repository-relative paths and preserve spaces, Unicode,
`#`、`?` 和字面百分号，无论使用哪种 HTTP 客户端。 `#`, `?`, and literal percent signs with either HTTP client.
目录列表会请求仓库自身的默认分支，因此，对于一个 Directory listings ask for the repository's own default branch, so a repository that
既不使用 `main` 也不使用 `master` 的仓库，返回的是其当前目录树，而非过时分支的目录树。 uses neither `main` nor `master` reports its current tree instead of a stale branch.
当 GitHub 或本地输出限制导致列表被截断时，目录列表会予以提示。 They indicate when GitHub or the local output limit truncates the listing.
`issues` 命令会排除拉取请求，并继续获取后续页面，以达到 The `issues` command excludes pull requests and follows additional pages to fill
所请求的议题数量上限；拉取请求仍可通过 `prs` 命令获取。 its requested issue limit; the `prs` command remains available for pull requests.
用户可以在请求开头使用 `/skill-name`，为单个轮次显式激活已启用的技能，例如 `/data-analysis analyze uploads/foo.csv`。 Users can explicitly activate an enabled skill for a single turn by starting the request with `/skill-name`, for example `/data-analysis analyze uploads/foo.csv`. DeerFlow 会将该技能的 `SKILL.md` 加载为当前轮次的隐藏上下文，同时将基础提示词的内容限定为技能元数据。 DeerFlow loads that skill's `SKILL.md` as hidden current-turn context while leaving the base prompt limited to skill metadata. 斜杠激活机制会遵循技能禁用设置、自定义智能体的技能白名单，以及 `/new` 和 `/help` 等现有频道命令。 Slash activation respects disabled skills, custom-agent skill whitelists, and existing channel commands such as `/new` and `/help`.
数据分析的 Markdown 导出会对列名和值中的竖线及反斜杠进行转义，并将单元格中的换行呈现为 ``，使多行数据保持在其表格单元格内。 Data-analysis Markdown exports escape pipes and backslashes in column names and values, and render cell line breaks as `` so multiline data stays within its table cell.
数据分析的 JSON 导出要求结果列名唯一。 Data-analysis JSON exports require unique result column names. 对于那些 For joins that
返回 `id` 等重复列名的连接操作，请使用 SQL 别名（`AS left_id`、`AS right_id`） return repeated names such as `id`, use SQL aliases (`AS left_id`, `AS right_id`)
然后再导出。 before exporting. 重复列名会在输出文件打开之前引发错误， Duplicate names cause an error before the output file is opened,
从而保留任何现有文件。 preserving any existing file.
CLI 会在简短的 stderr 错误信息中列出重复的列名，并以非零退出码退出， The CLI names the repeated columns in a short stderr error and exits nonzero,
因此调用方无需阅读回溯信息即可修正别名。 so callers can correct their aliases without reading a traceback.
JSON 导出同样会拒绝非有限数值（`NaN` 和无穷大），包括 JSON exports likewise reject non-finite numbers (`NaN` and infinities), including
嵌套在列表或结构体中的值，并且会在打开目标文件之前执行此检查。 values nested in lists or structs, before opening the destination. 请将这些值过滤掉 Filter them
或在 SQL 中将其替换为 `NULL`；CLI 会报告错误并保留任何 or replace them with `NULL` in SQL; the CLI reports an error and preserves any
现有输出，而不会写入无效的 JSON。 existing output rather than writing invalid JSON.
当不同输入之间发生名称冲突时，**data-analysis** 技能会保留每个 CSV 文件或 Excel 工作表。 The **data-analysis** skill preserves each CSV file or Excel sheet when names collide across inputs. 请显式[准备其 Python 环境](skills/public/data-analysis/SKILL.md#environment-setup)，安装 DuckDB 和 openpyxl；缺少软件包时，系统会给出环境配置说明，而不会在分析过程中自行安装。 [Prepare its Python environment](skills/public/data-analysis/SKILL.md#environment-setup) explicitly with DuckDB and openpyxl; missing packages report setup instructions instead of installing themselves during analysis. 查询和汇总时，请使用 `inspect` 输出的 SQL 名称（例如 `report`、`report_1`）。 Use the SQL names printed by `inspect` (such as `report`, `report_1`) for queries and summaries. 其缓存依据按顺序排列的输入文件基本名称及内容建立，因此重命名输入或调整输入顺序会重建表映射，而不会悄然选择不同的数据源。 Its cache follows ordered input basenames and contents, so renaming or reordering inputs rebuilds their table map instead of silently selecting a different source. 加引号的原始名称也能直接解析，无需改写查询文本；上传路径中的单引号和列名中的双引号会在加载、检查、查询和汇总的整个过程中得到保留。 Quoted original names also resolve without rewriting query text; apostrophes in upload paths and double quotes in column names are preserved through loading, inspection, queries, and summaries. CSV、JSON 和 Markdown 导出既接受当前工作目录中的简单文件名（例如 `report.csv`），也接受嵌套的相对路径和绝对路径。 CSV, JSON, and Markdown exports accept bare filenames such as `report.csv` in the current working directory as well as nested relative and absolute paths.
某条回答加载技能后，其工具栏会包含**使用的技能**。 After an answer loads skills, its toolbar includes **Skills used**. 将鼠标悬停在图标上或点击图标，即可查看技能及其来源；将鼠标悬停在技能名称上时，名称会显示下划线。 Hover over or click the icon to see the skills and their sources; hovering a skill name underlines it. 选择某个技能，即可在可调整大小的侧面板中查看其 `SKILL.md`（移动端使用抽屉式面板）。 Select a skill to inspect its `SKILL.md` in the resizable side panel (a drawer on mobile). 该视图使用在通过已配置的读取工具成功加载技能或显式通过斜杠激活技能时捕获的快照，因此之后对技能的编辑或删除不会改写其历史记录。 The view uses snapshots captured during successful configured read-tool loads or explicit slash activation, so later edits or removal of a skill do not rewrite its history. 每次运行中，重复加载仅显示一次，并按首次加载的顺序排列。 Repeated loads appear once per run, in first-load order. 范围读取和有界快照会被标记为部分内容；未捕获相关证据的较早对话不会显示此菜单。 Range reads and bounded snapshots are labeled as partial; older conversations without captured evidence do not show this menu. 复制操作会返回捕获的 Markdown，其中包括 YAML 前置元数据。 Copy returns the captured Markdown, including YAML frontmatter. 相对于包的链接和图像仍作为可读引用呈现，而不会使用户跳转离开对话。 Package-relative links and images remain readable references rather than navigating away from the conversation. 通过其他工具（例如 shell 命令）执行的技能加载，不会根据回答文本进行推断。 Skill loads performed through other tools, such as shell commands, are not inferred from answer text.
技能前置元数据中的 `allowed-tools` 是为与其他智能体框架互操作而保留的兼容性元数据：DeerFlow 会解析、验证、导出和描述它，但绝不会据此筛选模型可见的工具模式、拒绝工具调用或改写工具搜索中的工具提升操作。 A skill's `allowed-tools` frontmatter is compatibility metadata carried for interop with other agent frameworks: DeerFlow parses, validates, exports, and describes it, but it never filters model-visible tool schemas, refuses tool calls, or rewrites tool-search promotions. 仅在自定义智能体或子智能体的 `skills` 允许列表中启用、宣告或列出某项技能，同样不会改变智能体的工具集。 Merely enabling, advertising, or listing a skill in a custom agent or subagent `skills` allowlist never changes an agent's toolset either. 实际的工具权限由 Agent 配置和授权机制决定：组装时的提供方筛选、运行时的 Guardrail 强制执行、护栏允许列表以及延迟提升目录共同确定权限上限。 Real tool authority lives in Agent configuration and authorization: assembly-time provider filtering, runtime Guardrail enforcement, guardrail allowlists, and the deferred-promotion catalog own the permission ceiling. 技能的可见性和激活仍分别受到约束：斜杠命令激活会遵循技能禁用状态和自定义智能体白名单，而 `skill:activate` 授权决策仍同时控制显式斜杠命令激活和自主加载 `SKILL.md`。 Skill visibility and activation remain independently enforced: slash activation honors disabled skills and custom-agent whitelists, and the `skill:activate` authorization decision still gates both explicit slash activation and autonomous `SKILL.md` loads.
通过 Gateway 安装 `.skill` 归档文件时，DeerFlow 接受标准的空格分隔 `allowed-tools`、可选的前置元数据以及与 Claude 兼容的 `argument-hint` 字段，而不会拒绝其他方面均有效的外部技能。 When you install `.skill` archives through the Gateway, DeerFlow accepts standard space-separated `allowed-tools`, optional frontmatter metadata, and the Claude-compatible `argument-hint` field instead of rejecting otherwise valid external skills. `allowed-tools` 仍支持 YAML 列表，并保留运行时名称的精确形式。 YAML lists remain supported for `allowed-tools` and preserve exact runtime names. `WebFetch`、`WebSearch`、`Glob`、`Grep` 和 `Read` 等精确的可移植拼写分别映射到 DeerFlow 的 `web_fetch`、`web_search`、`glob`、`grep` 和 `read_file` 工具；小写或其他未知的标量名称保持不变，以便自定义工具和 MCP 工具保留其运行时拼写的精确形式。 Exact portable spellings such as `WebFetch`, `WebSearch`, `Glob`, `Grep`, and `Read` map to DeerFlow's `web_fetch`, `web_search`, `glob`, `grep`, and `read_file` tools; lowercase or otherwise unknown scalar names remain unchanged so custom and MCP tools keep their exact runtime spelling. `Bash(tvly *)` 等带括号的条目会被解析为单个字面条目，其中包含空格、引号内文本和转义括号；此类带作用域的声明仍只是字面意义上的兼容性元数据，不会授权或限制命令——决定哪些内容可以运行的是 DeerFlow 的 Agent 配置和授权机制，而非工具参数。 Parenthesized entries such as `Bash(tvly *)` are tokenized as one literal entry, including spaces, quoted text, and escaped parentheses; such scoped declarations remain literal compatibility metadata and do not authorize or restrict commands — DeerFlow's Agent configuration and authorization, not tool arguments, decide what may run.
禁用某项技能也会将其从沙箱文件系统视图中移除，因此 shell 命令和结构化文件工具遵循相同的启用状态。 Disabling a skill also removes it from the sandbox filesystem view, so shell commands and structured file tools follow the same enabled state. 本地、Docker/AIO、hostPath provisioner 以及新创建的 E2B 沙箱均通过仅包含已启用技能的投影视图提供 `/mnt/skills`；当公共、自定义、旧版或托管集成技能被切换启用状态、编辑、创建、删除或安装时，这些投影视图会随之更新。 Local, Docker/AIO, hostPath provisioner, and newly created E2B sandboxes source `/mnt/skills` from enabled-only projections that update when public, custom, legacy, or managed integration skills are toggled, edited, created, deleted, or installed. 结构化 `read_file` 调用（包括行范围读取和写入前读取检查）使用沙箱提供方的挂载映射，因此获取沙箱时记录的用户身份仍是权威依据。 Structured `read_file` calls (including line ranges and read-before-write checks) use the sandbox provider's mount mapping, so the user identity captured when the sandbox was acquired remains authoritative. 托管集成包仍然共享，而它们在投影文件系统中的可见性遵循各用户的启用状态。 Managed integration packages remain shared, while their projected filesystem visibility follows each user's enabled state. 多工作进程 Gateway 在重建用户投影视图时会重新读取磁盘上的启用状态，因此，一个工作进程处理的状态切换会在另一个工作进程下次获取沙箱时得到遵循。 Multi-worker Gateways re-read on-disk enable state while rebuilding user projections, so a toggle handled by one worker is honored by another worker's next sandbox acquire. 现有 E2B 沙箱会保留创建时的快照，直到重新创建。 Existing E2B sandboxes retain their creation-time snapshot until they are recreated. 由 PVC 支持的 provisioner 技能目前保留其配置的 PVC 快照和布局；动态 PVC 实体化另行跟踪。 PVC-backed provisioner skills keep their configured PVC snapshot/layout for now; dynamic PVC materialization is tracked separately.
Agent 技能允许列表所使用的对话专属技能视图会验证已复制文件的 Chat-specific skill views used by an Agent's skill allowlist verify copied file
内容，然后才复用沙箱。 contents before sandbox reuse. 被编辑的指令、缺失或被注入的文件， Edited instructions, missing or injected files,
以及发生变化的链接，都会在下次获取时依据规范技能进行修复； and changed links are repaired from the canonical skills on the next acquire;
共享存储上的 inode 和时间戳漂移不会强制触发重建。 inode and timestamp drift on shared storage does not force a rebuild. 完整性 Integrity
检查会读取投影文件，因此较大的包会增加获取时的读取 I/O。 checks read the projected files, so larger packages increase acquire-time read I/O.
保存技能投影清单时，即使临时文件清理或其日志记录也失败，仍会保留原始错误或中断。 Skill projection manifest saves preserve the original error or interruption even if temporary-file cleanup or its logging also fails. 清单文件保留其私有文件系统权限。 Manifest files retain their private filesystem permissions.

对于 `LocalSandboxProvider`，这是一个受管理的工具路径边界，而非主机文件系统隔离。 For `LocalSandboxProvider`, this is a managed tool-path boundary rather than host filesystem isolation. 仅在禁用主机 bash 时（默认状态）才接受显式的逐 Agent 技能策略，因为主机子进程可以不经提供程序的虚拟路径映射而直接访问规范路径。 Explicit per-Agent skill policies are accepted only while host bash is disabled (the default), because a host subprocess can address canonical paths without using the provider's virtual-path mappings. 当文件系统边界必须在允许 shell 访问的同时仍可强制实施时，请使用 Docker/AIO、Kubernetes 配置器或 E2B。 Use Docker/AIO, the Kubernetes provisioner, or E2B when the filesystem boundary must remain enforceable alongside shell access.
当本地沙箱使用 PowerShell 时，映射后的主机路径中的空格和 shell 元字符会根据调用方的引号上下文进行转义；通配符展开会予以保留。 When the local sandbox uses PowerShell, mapped host-path spaces and shell metacharacters are escaped for the caller's quoting context; wildcard expansion is preserved. 公开的 `bash` 工具现有的工作区前缀使用 `&&` 和 POSIX 路径引用方式：该分隔符要求使用 PowerShell 7，且不支持包含单引号的工作区路径。 The public `bash` tool's existing workspace prefix uses `&&` and POSIX path quoting: it requires PowerShell 7 for that separator and does not support workspace paths containing apostrophes. 这些工作区前缀限制与虚拟路径映射相互独立。 These workspace-prefix limitations are separate from virtual-path mapping.

Python 的 UTF-8 模式。 of Python's UTF-8 mode. 如果没有可用的控制台代码页，则使用系统 OEM If no console page is available, it uses the system OEM
代码页，并以 ANSI 作为最后的备选。 page, with ANSI as a last resort. 原生程序可能选择不同的输出 Native programs can choose a different output
编码；必要时，应将这些程序配置为与控制台代码页一致。 encoding; configure those programs to match the console page when needed.

> **沙箱信任边界：**浏览器永远不会收到 Lark 应用密钥，但 > **Sandbox trust boundary:** the browser never receives the Lark app secret, but
> 智能体会话会在沙箱内运行 `lark-cli`，因此每个用户的 > agent conversations run `lark-cli` inside the sandbox, so the per-user

在 Lark 代理模式下，AIO 新启动的 Bash 运行实例会关闭继承的管道标准输入（stdin）。 In Lark broker mode, AIO's fresh Bash runs close inherited pipe stdin. 持久化 Persistent

如果受信任的运维人员通过 MinIO、NFS 或 CSI 等外部挂载管理所配置的技能目录，管理员可以在更改文件后调用 `POST /api/skills/reload`。 If a trusted operator manages the configured skills directory through an external mount such as MinIO, NFS, or CSI, an administrator can call `POST /api/skills/reload` after changing files. 此操作会使处理该请求的 Gateway 进程中的技能提示词缓存失效，在限定的刷新超时时间内等待，然后更新可写配置目录中的持久化重置标记（`.extensions_config.json.skills-cache-reset.json`）。 This invalidates skill prompt caches in the handling Gateway process, waits up to the bounded refresh timeout, and then advances a durable reset marker (`.extensions_config.json.skills-cache-reset.json`) in the writable config directory. 挂载该目录的每个 Gateway 工作进程或 Pod 都会在下一次构建提示词时的约一秒内发现该标记，并重新扫描最新文件，因此一次调用即可覆盖共享该卷的所有副本；响应会报告 `scope: shared_config`，如果没有可用的扩展配置路径，则报告 `scope: process`。 Every Gateway worker or Pod mounting that directory notices the marker within about a second of its next prompt build and rescans the latest files, so one call reaches every replica sharing the volume; the response reports `scope: shared_config`, or `scope: process` when no extensions config path is available. 正在运行的任务不受影响。 Running tasks are unchanged. 通过 API 进行技能安装、编辑、删除、回滚以及启用/禁用切换时，都会发布同一标记，因此在一个副本上进行的更改无需重启即可在其他副本上生效。 Skill installs, edits, deletions, rollbacks and enable/disable toggles made through the API publish the same marker, so a change made on one replica takes effect on the others without a restart. 加载器层面的文件系统故障会返回通用服务器错误，并保留进程中最后一次成功加载的缓存，而不会发布空目录。 A loader-level filesystem failure returns a generic server error and preserves the last successfully loaded process cache rather than publishing an empty catalog. 此机制不涵盖使用独立文件系统的副本。 Replicas with independent filesystems are not covered. 直接写入挂载目录会绕过 DeerFlow 安装/编辑 API 所执行的验证、SkillScan 扫描和历史记录机制，因此只有运维人员控制的系统才应拥有写入权限。 Direct mount writes bypass the validation, SkillScan, and history applied by DeerFlow's install/edit APIs, so only operator-controlled systems should have write access.
技能安装和由智能体管理的技能编辑会先经过 **SkillScan**，这是一款原生确定性安全扫描器，运行于基于 LLM 的技能扫描器之前。 Skill installs and agent-managed skill edits run through **SkillScan**, a native deterministic safety scanner before the LLM-based skill scanner. 阶段 1 离线运行，不依赖 Semgrep/OpenGrep，会阻止私钥或 shell 执行等高置信度的 `CRITICAL` 级发现，并将警告级发现传递给 LLM 扫描器以进行上下文审查。 Phase 1 runs offline with no Semgrep/OpenGrep dependency, blocks high-confidence `CRITICAL` findings such as private keys or shell execution, and passes warning findings to the LLM scanner for contextual review. 对于代码文件（`scripts/` 下的任何文件、具有 `.py`、`.sh` 或 `.js` 等脚本后缀的文件，或以 `#!` 开头的无扩展名文件），如果其内容不是不含 NUL 的 UTF-8 文本，就会触发警告，并且仍会通过有损解码进行分析，因此单个异常字节无法使其逃过 `CRITICAL` 检查。 Code files (anything under `scripts/`, a script suffix such as `.py`, `.sh`, or `.js`, or an extensionless file starting with `#!`) that are not NUL-free UTF-8 text raise a warning and are still analyzed over a lossy decode, so a single stray byte cannot hide them from `CRITICAL` checks. 审核适配器会先对纯文本模型响应和 LangChain Responses API 文本块进行规范化，再解析所要求的 JSON 决策。 The moderation adapter normalizes both plain-text model responses and LangChain Responses API text blocks before parsing the required JSON decision. Python 实例客户端的数据外传检查遵循同一作用域内的最小证据链：绑定到已知客户端构造函数的简单名称、可选的名称到名称别名，以及该构造函数所支持的实际出站方法调用或上下文管理器使用。 Python instance-client exfiltration checks follow a minimal same-scope evidence chain: a simple name bound to a known client constructor, optional name-to-name aliases, and an actual outbound method or context-manager use supported by that constructor. 构造函数的根名称必须经证实来自导入；不会将仅仅看起来像规范名称的裸名称推断为模块。 Constructor roots must be proven imports; bare canonical-looking names are not inferred as modules. 嵌套作用域不继承客户端句柄，并且仅继承在外层作用域中从未被重新绑定的构造函数导入别名。 Nested scopes do not inherit client handles and inherit only constructor import aliases that are never rebound in the enclosing scope. 推导式、包含海象运算符的语句、注解、复杂绑定目标、不受支持的操作以及存在歧义的分支流程，都不会通过此信号产生发现；对于跳过的语法结构，会保守地使其可能绑定的每个名称失效，以免过时的客户端状态导致发现。 Comprehensions, walrus-bearing statements, annotations, complex binding targets, unsupported operations, and ambiguous branch flows produce no finding from this signal; skipped constructs conservatively invalidate every name they may bind so stale client state cannot create a finding. 此尽力而为的分析达到确定性的工作预算或递归限制时，不会丢弃已为该文件收集的发现。 A deterministic work budget or recursion limit reached by this best-effort analysis does not discard findings already collected for the file. 在 `config.yaml` 中设置 `skill_scan.enabled: false`，即可仅禁用确定性分析器；安全归档解压和 LLM 扫描器仍会运行。 Set `skill_scan.enabled: false` in `config.yaml` to disable only the deterministic analyzers; safe archive extraction and the LLM scanner still run.
Windows 脚本（`.bat`、`.cmd`、`.ps1`、`.psm1`、`.js`、`.jse`、`.vbs`、`.vbe`、`.wsf`）、HTML 应用程序（`.hta`）和脚本组件（`.sct`）即使位于 `scripts/` 之外，也会被视为代码，且不受文件名大小写影响。 Windows scripts (`.bat`, `.cmd`, `.ps1`, `.psm1`, `.js`, `.jse`, `.vbs`, `.vbe`, `.wsf`), HTML applications (`.hta`), and scriptlets (`.sct`) count as code even outside `scripts/`, regardless of filename case. 它们既会接受 SkillScan 分析，也会受到安装程序可执行代码策略的约束。 They receive both SkillScan analysis and the installer's executable-code policy.
SkillScan 会对通过管道传入常见 shell 的远程下载内容发出警告，涵盖使用 `env` 启动器、sudo、解释器路径和 shell 续行的情况。 SkillScan warns about remote downloads piped into common shells, including `env` launchers, sudo, interpreter paths, and shell line continuations. 它能够区分环境变量转储与 `env` 命令，包括涉及赋值和 GNU 字符串拆分转义的情况。 It distinguishes environment dumps from `env` commands, including assignments and GNU split-string escapes. Shell 解析和拆分展开均有界限；达到限制时，会保留已检测到的严重级发现。 Shell parsing and split expansion are bounded; reaching a limit preserves critical findings already detected. 通过管道传给 `jq` 和 `tee` 等非 shell 工具不会触发此警告。 Pipes to non-shell tools such as `jq` and `tee` do not trigger this warning.
SkillScan 对 HTTP/HTTPS 协议方案的拼写和 HTTP 主机名均不区分大小写，并将包含方括号括起的 IPv6 SkillScan treats HTTP/HTTPS scheme spellings and HTTP hostnames case-insensitively and recognizes bracketed IPv6
环回地址（`[::1]`）的 URL 识别为本地地址。 loopback (`[::1]`) URLs as local. 外部 IPv6 端点仍会触发网络相关发现， External IPv6 endpoints still trigger network findings,
并且，云元数据主机名检测不区分大小写。 and cloud-metadata hostname detection is case-insensitive.
SkillScan 会将技能示例中未加引号的整数模型用量计数器（如 `total_tokens` 和 `input_tokens`）识别为测量值。 SkillScan recognizes unquoted integer model-usage counters such as `total_tokens` and `input_tokens` in skill examples as measurements. 基准测试中的 token 统计数据和带正负号的差值也会被识别；凭据字段和其他字符串值仍需接受秘密信息检测。 Benchmark token statistics and signed deltas are also recognized; credential fields and other string values remain subject to secret detection.
对于 Python 凭据映射，SkillScan 会检查字面量值，同时将普通字典键视为标签。 For Python credential mappings, SkillScan checks literal values while treating ordinary dictionary keys as labels. 对于 `tokens = {"access_token": os.getenv("ACCESS_TOKEN")}` 这样的映射，不会报告存在硬编码凭据。 A mapping such as `tokens = {"access_token": os.getenv("ACCESS_TOKEN")}` does not report a hardcoded credential. 与已识别的云令牌或 API 令牌格式匹配的键仍会作为嵌入式凭据接受检查。 Keys matching a recognized cloud or API token format are still checked as embedded credentials.
SkillScan 会阻止嵌入的旧版和新版 `sk-` API 令牌，包括 OpenAI 项目密钥、服务账户密钥和管理员密钥，以及 Anthropic 密钥，即使它们出现在没有秘密值赋值语句的 Bearer 标头中也不例外。 SkillScan blocks embedded legacy and modern `sk-` API tokens, including OpenAI project, service-account, and admin keys and Anthropic keys, even in Bearer headers without a secret assignment. 令牌主体可以包含 `-` 和 `_`；检测结果中的证据会被完全遮蔽。 Token bodies may contain `-` and `_`; finding evidence is fully redacted. 占位符示例不会抑制对同一文件中后续凭据的检测。 Placeholder examples do not suppress detection of later credentials in the same file. 计划任务的通知摘要在发送到外部即时通信平台之前，也会遮蔽这些格式的密钥。 Scheduled-task notification summaries also redact these key formats before sending them to external IM platforms.
DeerFlow 还附带了 **skill-reviewer**，这是一项用于以只读方式审查技能质量的公开技能。 DeerFlow also ships with **skill-reviewer**, a public skill for read-only skill quality review. 它使用内置的 `review_skill_package` 工具来检查已安装的技能、本地软件包、归档文件或粘贴的 `SKILL.md` 内容，而无需激活目标技能、绑定其秘密信息、执行其脚本或安装该技能。 It uses the built-in `review_skill_package` tool to inspect installed skills, local packages, archives, or pasted `SKILL.md` content without activating the target skill, binding its secrets, executing its scripts, or installing it. 该工具向模型上下文返回经过标签中和处理的精简 JSON 载荷，并将完整的原始审查载荷保留在工具产物中，供程序化使用方使用。 The tool returns a compact, tag-neutralized JSON payload to the model context and keeps the full raw review payload in the tool artifact for programmatic consumers. 确定性审查核心复用 DeerFlow 的解析能力和 SkillScan 的事实数据，在 `contracts/skill_review/` 下输出带版本的 JSON 契约，并可通过后端 CLI 运行： The deterministic review core reuses DeerFlow parsing and SkillScan facts, emits versioned JSON contracts under `contracts/skill_review/`, and can be run from the backend CLI: 
cd backend
uv run python -m deerflow.skills.review.cli ../skills/public/data-analysis --format text --fail-on error --fail-on-incomplete
公开技能的 CI 豁免是 `.github/skill-review-waivers.v1.json` 中精确匹配且有到期时间的例外。 Public-skill CI waivers are exact, expiring exceptions in `.github/skill-review-waivers.v1.json`. 由于只有受信任的基准清单才能抑制检测结果，因此可以先合并一项仅修改清单的变更，在 `preapproved_file_sha256s` 中列出经过审查的未来完整文件的 SHA-256，从而安全地预先授权一个会修改文件的拉取请求；随后即可在另一个拉取请求中合并该文件变更。 Because only the trusted base manifest can suppress a finding, a file-changing pull request can be preauthorized safely by first merging a manifest-only change that lists the reviewed future full-file SHA-256 in `preapproved_file_sha256s`; the file change can then land in a later pull request.
工具也遵循同样的理念。 Tools follow the same philosophy. DeerFlow 自带一套核心工具，包括网页搜索、网页抓取、渲染后网页捕获、文件操作和 bash 执行，并支持通过 MCP 服务器和 Python 函数提供自定义工具。 DeerFlow comes with a core toolset — web search, web fetch, rendered web capture, file operations, bash execution — and supports custom tools via MCP servers and Python functions. 随附的 DDG、Brave、Tavily、SearXNG 和 Serper 搜索提供方接受可选的 `time_range` 参数，其值可为 `day`、`week`、`month` 或 `year`；省略该参数会保留现有的搜索行为。 The bundled DDG, Brave, Tavily, SearXNG, and Serper search providers accept an optional `time_range` of `day`, `week`, `month`, or `year`; omitting it preserves existing search behavior. 对于 DDG 按时间范围进行的搜索，DeerFlow 会排除忽略时间限制的 DDGS 后端。 For DDG recency searches, DeerFlow excludes DDGS backends that ignore time limits. 任何组件都可以替换。 Swap anything. 任何组件都可以添加。 Add anything.
对于 DDG 网页搜索和 DDG 图像搜索，`config.yaml` 中的 `max_results` 可以是正整数，也可以是环境变量引用，例如 `max_results: $DDG_MAX_RESULTS`，其中 `DDG_MAX_RESULTS=5`。 For DDG web search and DDG image search, `max_results` in `config.yaml` can be a positive integer or an environment-variable reference such as `max_results: $DDG_MAX_RESULTS` with `DDG_MAX_RESULTS=5`. 配置值的优先级高于工具调用的 `max_results` 参数。 The configured value takes precedence over the tool call's `max_results` argument. 无效值（例如 `abc`、空字符串或 `3.5`）、零和负数都会触发警告，并回退到默认的 5 条结果。 Invalid values (for example, `abc`, an empty string, or `3.5`), zero, and negative counts produce a warning and fall back to the default of 5 results. DDG、Brave 和 Serper 图像搜索共用同一套结果 URL 筛选机制，该机制会排除非 HTTP(S) URL、localhost、云元数据主机名和非公网地址字面量，包括组播地址和嵌入私有 IPv4 地址的 IPv6 表示形式。 DDG, Brave, and Serper image search share one result-URL screen that omits non-HTTP(S), localhost, cloud-metadata hostnames, and non-public address literals, including multicast and IPv6 spellings embedding private IPv4 addresses. 该筛选机制不进行 DNS 解析；下载器必须在建立连接时验证解析得到的地址。 The screen does not resolve DNS; downloaders must validate the resolved address when connecting. 如果某条结果既没有安全的完整图像 URL，也没有安全的缩略图，则会被排除。 A result with neither a safe full image URL nor a safe thumbnail is omitted.
图像结果筛选还会将 Unicode 主机名分隔符（`。`、`．`、 Image result screening also recognizes Unicode hostname separators (`。`, `．`,
`｡`）识别为点号，与 IDNA 客户端的规范化方式保持一致，然后再判定本地或私有 `｡`) as dots, matching IDNA client normalization before classifying local/private
主机。 hosts. 通过筛选的结果 URL 会保留其原始写法。 Accepted result URLs retain their original spelling.
DDG 图像搜索会将超时、速率限制和初始化失败连同原始查询一起报告为搜索错误。`No images found` 仅用于返回结果集为空的情况。 DDG image search reports timeouts, rate limits, and setup failures as search errors with the original query. `No images found` is reserved for a returned empty result set. SDK 的 `No results found.` 异常仍被视为搜索错误，因为它也可能掩盖尚未完成的后端请求；出于保守考虑，这也包括确实没有结果但无法确认已完成的搜索所引发的 SDK 异常。 The SDK's `No results found.` exception remains a search error because it can also hide an unfinished backend request; this conservatively includes SDK exceptions from genuinely empty searches whose completion cannot be confirmed.
当 Stdio MCP 服务器的入口点 Stdio MCP servers can set `cwd` in `extensions_config.json` when their entrypoint
或数据文件依赖特定工作目录时，可以在 `extensions_config.json` 中设置 `cwd`。 or data files depend on a specific working directory. 该设置适用于 The setting applies to
发现过程和工具调用；参见 [MCP 配置](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md#stdio-working-directory)。 both discovery and tool calls; see [MCP configuration](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md#stdio-working-directory).
省略该值，或将其设为 `null` 或空值，都会保留默认工作目录。 Omitted, `null`, or empty values keep the default working directories.
Tavily 的 `web_search` 还接受可选的 `include_domains` 和 `exclude_domains` Tavily `web_search` also accepts optional `include_domains` and `exclude_domains`
在其 `config.yaml` 工具条目中列出列表，以控制搜索来源。 lists in its `config.yaml` tool entry to control search sources. 非空的 Non-empty
`include_domains` 使用 Tavily 的 `filter` 模式，将结果限制在这些域名内。 `include_domains` uses Tavily's `filter` mode to restrict results to those domains.
这些是部署设置；模型仍然只提供 `query` 和可选的 These are deployment settings; the model still supplies only `query` and optional
`time_range`。 `time_range`. 省略过滤条件会保持现有的 SDK 请求不变；显式指定的 Omitted filters preserve the existing SDK request; an explicit

导出自定义技能Exporting Custom Skills
管理员可以通过**能力中心 → 技能 → 我的技能 → 查看详情 → 导出**，导出自己的自定义技能。 Administrators can export their own custom skills from **Capability Center → Skills → My skills → View details → Export**. 检查文件列表和声明的环境要求，然后选择**下载 .skill**。 Review the file list and declared environment requirements, then choose **Download .skill**. 归档文件包含当前已保存的技能，包括配套文件和空目录；已禁用的技能也可以导出。 The archive contains the currently saved skill, including supporting files and empty directories; disabled skills can also be exported. 如果技能在预览后发生变化，请在下载前刷新文件列表。 If the skill changes after preview, refresh the file list before downloading. 使用**安装 .skill** 将归档文件导入另一个 DeerFlow 实例；仍需处理现有名称冲突，并执行常规安装安全检查。 Import the archive on another DeerFlow instance with **Install .skill**; existing-name conflicts and normal installation security checks still apply.
不包含账户设置，以及技能文件夹之外的对话和历史记录。 Account settings, conversations and history outside the skill folder are excluded. 文件夹内的文件保持原样，包括作者放入其中的任何凭据；文件名提示仅供参考。 Files inside the folder are preserved unchanged, including any credentials an author placed there; filename notices are advisory. 在目标环境中配置依赖项和凭据。 Configure dependencies and credentials on the destination. 链接的文件夹或文件、硬链接、不受支持的可执行二进制文件、嵌套的 `SKILL.md` 文件以及不可移植的路径均无法导出。 Linked folders/files, hard links, unsupported executable binaries, nested `SKILL.md` files and nonportable paths cannot be exported. 导出功能支持具有相对于文件描述符操作且不跟随符号链接的文件系统 API 的主机（Linux/macOS）；对于不受支持的主机，会明确报错。 Export supports hosts with descriptor-relative no-follow filesystem APIs (Linux/macOS); unsupported hosts fail explicitly. 限制：4096 个 ZIP 条目，每个文件 64 MiB，内容总量及归档文件大小 100 MiB，前置元数据 1 MiB。 Limits: 4096 ZIP entries, 64 MiB per file, 100 MiB total content/archive and 1 MiB frontmatter. 不支持 YAML 别名和过于复杂的声明。 YAML aliases and excessively complex declarations are not supported. 在 POSIX 系统上导入时，会保留普通脚本的可执行语义，但不会恢复特殊权限。 Ordinary script executable semantics are preserved on POSIX import, without restoring special permissions. 参见[导出 API 契约](backend/docs/API.md#export-a-custom-skill)。 See [the export API contract](backend/docs/API.md#export-a-custom-skill).
Claude Code 集成Claude Code Integration
`claude-to-deerflow` 技能让你可以直接从 [Claude Code](https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code) 与正在运行的 DeerFlow 实例交互。 The `claude-to-deerflow` skill lets you interact with a running DeerFlow instance directly from [Claude Code](https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code). 发送研究任务、查看状态、管理会话线程——所有操作都无需离开终端。 Send research tasks, check status, manage threads — all without leaving the terminal.
**安装技能**： **Install the skill**: 
npx skills add https://github.com/bytedance/deer-flow --skill claude-to-deerflow
然后确保 DeerFlow 正在运行（默认地址为 `http://localhost:2026`），并在 Claude Code 中使用 `/claude-to-deerflow` 命令。 Then make sure DeerFlow is running (default at `http://localhost:2026`) and use the `/claude-to-deerflow` command in Claude Code.
**可执行的操作**： **What you can do**:
向 DeerFlow 发送消息并获取流式响应 Send messages to DeerFlow and get streaming responses
选择执行模式：flash（快速）、standard、pro（规划）、ultra（子智能体） Choose execution modes: flash (fast), standard, pro (planning), ultra (sub-agents)
检查 DeerFlow 的运行状况，列出模型、技能和智能体 Check DeerFlow health, list models/skills/agents
管理会话线程和对话历史记录 Manage threads and conversation history
上传文件以供分析 Upload files for analysis
**环境变量**（可选，用于自定义端点）： **Environment variables** (optional, for custom endpoints): 
DEERFLOW_URL=http://localhost:2026            # Unified proxy base URL
DEERFLOW_GATEWAY_URL=http://localhost:2026    # Gateway API
DEERFLOW_LANGGRAPH_URL=http://localhost:2026/api/langgraph  # LangGraph API
完整的 API 参考文档见 [`skills/public/claude-to-deerflow/SKILL.md`](skills/public/claude-to-deerflow/SKILL.md)。 See [`skills/public/claude-to-deerflow/SKILL.md`](skills/public/claude-to-deerflow/SKILL.md) for the full API reference.

每条消息仍最多可选择 1000 个文档。 Each message can still select up to 1000 documents. 当从单个数据集中选择超过 100 个文档时，DeerFlow 会按每批最多 100 个文档进行验证，同时保留完整的选择结果。 When more than 100 documents are selected from a single dataset, DeerFlow validates them in batches of at most 100 while preserving the complete selection. 如果任何一批包含无法访问或不可搜索的文档，检索请求将被拒绝。 If any batch contains an inaccessible or non-searchable document, retrieval is rejected.
高级部署可以通过 `config.yaml` 中的 `authorization.enabled` 启用可插拔授权。 Advanced deployments can enable pluggable authorization with `authorization.enabled` in `config.yaml`. 配置的 `AuthorizationProvider` 会在被禁止的工具到达模型或延迟加载工具目录之前将其过滤掉，随后在每次执行业务工具之前，通过现有的护栏中间件再次调用同一提供方进行检查。 A configured `AuthorizationProvider` filters denied tools before they reach the model or deferred-tool catalog, then the same provider is checked again before every business-tool execution through the existing guardrail middleware. Gateway 的 `threads:*` 和 `runs:*` 路由权限由同一提供方确定，同时现有的所有者检查和仅限管理员的管理访问控制仍然有效。 Gateway `threads:*` and `runs:*` route permissions are derived from the same provider, while existing owner checks and admin-only management gates remain in force. 每条启动 Agent 运行或使未来的 Agent 运行成为可能的 HTTP 路由都需要 `runs:create` 权限：这包括无状态的 `POST /api/runs/stream` 和 `POST /api/runs/wait` 端点，以及定时任务的创建、更新、恢复和手动触发等变更操作。 Every HTTP route that starts or enables a future Agent run requires `runs:create`: this includes the stateless `POST /api/runs/stream` and `POST /api/runs/wait` endpoints plus scheduled-task create, update, resume, and manual-trigger mutations. 定时任务的变更操作保留现有的 `threads:write` 权限要求；当请求体中提供了可选的线程 ID 时，无状态路由还会单独执行所有权检查。 Scheduled-task mutations retain their existing `threads:write` requirement, and the stateless routes separately enforce ownership when the optional thread ID is supplied in the request body. 生成的 `tool_search` 只有在作为当前构建中已过滤的延迟加载工具目录的访问入口时，才可以跳过第二次工具检查。 A generated `tool_search` may bypass the second tool check only when it fronts the current build's already-filtered deferred catalog. 模型访问也遵循同一提供方：Gateway 的 `models` 列表会按访问主体进行过滤，模型详情请求会强制检查 `model:use` 权限，运行时解析智能体的模型时也会再次检查；如果默认模型被拒绝访问，则回退到剩余候选模型中第一个同样通过 `model:use` 检查的模型。 Model access follows the same provider: the Gateway `models` list is filtered per principal, `model:use` is enforced on model detail requests and again when the runtime resolves the agent's model, and a denied default model falls back to the first remaining candidate that also passes `model:use`. 内置的 RBAC 提供方支持针对各角色配置 `tools`、`routes`、`models`、`skills` 和 `sandbox` 的允许/拒绝策略，并验证 `default_role` 是否指向已配置的角色；授权默认处于禁用状态。 The built-in RBAC provider supports per-role `tools`, `routes`, `models`, `skills`, and `sandbox` allow/deny policies and validates that `default_role` names a configured role; authorization is disabled by default. 通过管理 API（`GET /api/v1/admin/users`、`PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/{id}`；仅限管理员使用，并设有防止最后一名管理员被降级的保护机制）将提供程序的 `roles` 映射中声明的自定义角色分配给用户后，这些角色即可生效——无人持有的已声明角色不会授予任何权限。 Custom roles declared in the provider's `roles` mapping become reachable by assigning them to a user through the admin API (`GET /api/v1/admin/users`, `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/{id}`; admin-only, last-admin demotion protected) — a declared role nobody holds grants nothing. 请参阅 `config.example.yaml` 和[授权 RFC](docs/plans/2026-07-10-pluggable-authorization-rfc.md)。 See `config.example.yaml` and the [authorization RFC](docs/plans/2026-07-10-pluggable-authorization-rfc.md).
管理员可以撤销已停用账户的管理员角色，同时保持该账户的停用状态， Administrators can revoke a disabled account's admin role while keeping it disabled,
也可以在同一次账户更新中为其分配非管理员角色并重新启用该账户。 or assign a non-admin role and re-enable it in the same account update. 最后一名 The last
处于启用状态的管理员仍受保护，不能被降级或停用。 enabled administrator remains protected against demotion and disabling.
后续建议功能也会在调用所选模型之前检查 `model:use`，包括未提供名称时使用的默认模型。 Follow-up suggestions also check `model:use` before calling the selected model, including the default model when no name is supplied. 如果模型访问被拒绝，则返回 HTTP 403，不会调用 LLM；授权提供程序发生故障时，遵循配置的 `fail_closed` 策略。 A denied model returns HTTP 403 without an LLM call; authorization-provider failures follow the configured `fail_closed` policy.
对于具备视觉能力的智能体，`view_image` 以及随后将图像读入模型上下文的操作也需要 `sandbox:execute` 权限。 For vision-capable agents, `view_image` and the subsequent model-context image read also require `sandbox:execute`. 仅允许工具名称并不会授予图像文件访问权限；被拒绝执行沙箱操作的角色在权限变更后，无法重新读取先前记录的图像元数据。 Allowing the tool name alone does not grant image-file access; a role denied sandbox execution cannot reread previously recorded image metadata after its permissions change. 外部运行输入和线程状态更新不能设置 `viewed_images` 或 `thread_data`；从宿主机副本读取已保存的图像时，其路径必须解析为当前用户的线程及所记录的虚拟图像路径。 External run input and thread-state updates cannot set `viewed_images` or `thread_data`; when a saved image is read from a host copy, its path must resolve to the current user's thread and the recorded virtual image path.
每次模型请求最多包含四张最近查看的图像，以及 28 MiB 的 base64 图像数据。 Each model request includes at most four recently viewed images and 28 MiB of base64 image data. 因这些限制而被排除的图像会被报告为已省略；智能体可以再次调用 `view_image`，将其中某张图像重新纳入上下文。 Images excluded by these limits are reported as omitted; the agent can call `view_image` again to bring one back into context.
高级部署还可以通过在 `config.yaml` 或 `extensions_config.json` 的 `extensions.middlewares` 下声明 `AgentMiddleware` 类，扩展智能体运行时本身。 Advanced deployments can also extend the agent runtime itself by declaring `AgentMiddleware` classes under `extensions.middlewares` in `config.yaml` or `extensions_config.json`. 每个条目可以是一个 `module.path:ClassName` 字符串（使用无参数构造函数），也可以是一个 `{class, kwargs, apply_to}` 对象，其中的 `kwargs` 会传递给构造函数。 Each entry is a `module.path:ClassName` string (zero-argument constructor) or an object `{class, kwargs, apply_to}` whose `kwargs` are passed to the constructor. 可选的 `apply_to` 用于选择 `both`（默认值）、`lead` 或 `subagent`；旧式字符串仍适用于两条链。`kwargs` 的值必须属于 JSON 类型（对象、数组、字符串、数字、布尔值或 null）；YAML 日期和时间戳会被强制转换为 ISO 字符串，以与 JSON 保持一致。 Optional `apply_to` selects `both` (default), `lead`, or `subagent`; legacy strings still apply to both chains. `kwargs` values must be JSON types (object, array, string, number, boolean, or null); YAML dates and timestamps are coerced to ISO strings so they match JSON. DeerFlow 将所选的配置条目加载到主智能体和子智能体的处理流水线中，位置在各自内置的运行时中间件及循环/token 防护机制之后，但在最终响应/安全/澄清尾部组件之前，从而使企业分支能够添加领域防护规则、工具调用治理或可观测性钩子，而无须修改内置的中间件构建器。 DeerFlow loads the selected configured entries into the lead-agent and subagent pipelines after their built-in runtime middlewares and loop/token guards, but before the terminal-response/safety/clarification tail, so enterprise forks can add domain guardrails, tool-call governance, or observability hooks without patching the built-in middleware builders. 缺失的软件包、无效的类、损坏的模块以及构造函数错误，都会在创建智能体时引发明确报错。 Missing packages, invalid classes, broken modules, and constructor errors fail loudly at agent creation. 应将 `config.yaml` 和 `extensions_config.json` 视为由运维人员控制的可信文件：中间件路径会导致代码执行，与自定义工具、模型、沙箱、防护规则、MCP 服务器及 MCP 拦截器的声明一样。 Treat `config.yaml` and `extensions_config.json` as trusted operator-controlled files: middleware paths are code execution, just like custom tool, model, sandbox, guardrail, MCP server, and MCP interceptor declarations. Gateway 的技能/MCP 开关端点会保留此字段，但不会提供用于写入 `extensions.middlewares` 的 API 路径。 Gateway skill/MCP toggle endpoints preserve this field but do not expose an API write path for `extensions.middlewares`. 被排除在某条链之外的条目，不会在该链中被导入或实例化。 Entries excluded from a chain are not imported or constructed there. 无效的作用域值会导致配置验证失败，所选条目则保持其相对顺序。 Invalid scope values fail configuration validation, and selected entries retain their relative order. 打包扩展继续使用其现有的 `AgentScope` 契约。 Packaged extensions continue to use their existing `AgentScope` contract.
对于打包的、可配置的运行时集成，请使用 DeerFlow 的扩展管理器。 For packaged and configurable runtime integrations, use DeerFlow's extension manager.
它接受 Python 软件包需求说明、公共 HTTPS Git URL 或本地目录，并将 It accepts a Python package requirement, a public HTTPS Git URL, or a local directory, installs the
软件包安装到后端专用的 `extensions` 依赖组中，更新 package into the backend's dedicated `extensions` dependency group, updates
`backend/uv.lock`，并向仅在启动时加载的顶层 `plugins:` 列表添加一个已启用的条目 `backend/uv.lock`, and adds an enabled entry to the startup-only top-level `plugins:` list
，该列表位于 `config.yaml` 中： in `config.yaml`: 
# PyPI — pin a version for a reproducible deployment
make extension-install SOURCE="deerflow-extension-acme==1.2.3"

# Public HTTPS Git — pin an immutable commit
make extension-install \
  SOURCE="git+https://github.com/acme/deerflow-extension-acme.git@0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567"

# Local package — an absolute path avoids Make's backend-relative working directory
make extension-install SOURCE="$PWD/examples/deerflow-extension-example"

make extension-list
make extension-upgrade SOURCE="$PWD/examples/deerflow-extension-example"
make extension-disable NAME=acme
make extension-enable NAME=acme
make extension-remove NAME=acme

例如，在 `backend/` 目录下，检查 `demo` 是否已配置为启用且必需： For example, from `backend/`, check that `demo` is configured as enabled and required: 
inventory=$(uv run --frozen --no-group extensions deerflow extensions list --json) || exit 1
printf '%s\n' "$inventory" |
  jq -e 'any(.[]; .name == "demo" and .enabled == true and .required == true)'

Gateway 生成的后续建议现在会在解析 JSON 数组响应之前，对纯字符串模型输出和块式或列表式富内容进行规范化，避免提供方特有的内容封装导致建议被静默丢弃。 Gateway-generated follow-up suggestions now normalize both plain-string model output and block/list-style rich content before parsing the JSON array response, so provider-specific content wrappers do not silently drop suggestions. 后续建议和输入润色会跳过类型为非字符串的内容块，保留周围的文本，而不会导致请求失败 Follow-up suggestions and input polishing skip content blocks with non-string types, preserving the surrounding text instead of failing the request
可见内容检测（`has_visible_content`，由空响应、终止响应和模型长度中间件共用）和子智能体调度快照（`ParentContextSnapshot.from_state`）通过相等性比较而非集合成员资格检查来判断内容块类型，因此，当提供方输出的块具有不可哈希的类型（例如 `type: []`）时，该块会被跳过，同时保留周围的历史记录，而不会抛出 TypeError 并使其逸出中间件链 Visible-content detection (`has_visible_content`, shared by the empty-response, terminal-response and model-length middlewares) and the subagent dispatch snapshot (`ParentContextSnapshot.from_state`) probe content-block types by equality rather than set membership, so a provider-emitted block with an unhashable type such as `type: []` degrades to a skipped block — keeping the surrounding history — instead of raising TypeError out of the middleware chain
后端响应清理会在后续建议和润色后的草稿中保留无关的标签名，例如 `` 和 ``；只有名称恰好为 `` 的标签（后面可带属性）才会开启推理块。 Backend response cleanup preserves unrelated tag names such as `` and `` in follow-up suggestions and polished drafts; only the exact `` name (optionally followed by attributes) starts a reasoning block. 自闭合的 `` 和 `` 标签属于空推理块，会完整保留其后的回答。 Self-closing `` and `` tags are empty reasoning blocks and leave the following answer intact. 引号括起的属性内部的分隔符（例如 `"/>`）也不会影响其后回答的完整性。 Delimiters inside quoted attributes, such as `"/>`, also leave the following answer intact.
Web UI 输入框可以在发送前润色输入草稿。 The Web UI composer can polish draft input before sending. 改写通过使用 `input_polish` 模型配置的简短 Gateway LLM 请求执行，保留 `/data-analysis` 等斜杠技能前缀，并且仅在用户点击润色按钮后才替换本地草稿；它不会创建线程运行，也不会持久化消息。 The rewrite runs as a short Gateway LLM request using the `input_polish` model configuration, keeps slash skill prefixes such as `/data-analysis`, and only replaces the local draft after the user clicks the polish button; it does not create a thread run or persist a message.
输入润色、后续建议、后台项目文件摘要、 Input polishing, follow-up suggestions, background project-file summaries, the
LLM 技能扫描器、线程标题生成以及 `/goal` 评估器会构建 LLM skill scanner, thread title generation, and the `/goal` evaluator construct
各自的模型提供方实例，且构建过程在 Gateway 事件循环之外进行，因此本地凭据读取 their model providers off the Gateway event loop, so local credential reads do
不会阻塞无关请求。 not stall unrelated requests.
当智能体请求澄清时，Web UI 会显示结构化响应卡片，同时保持常规输入框可用。 When the agent asks for clarification, the Web UI shows the structured response card but keeps the normal composer available. 用户可以填写卡片，也可以发送自由形式的聊天消息来跳过卡片；该消息会关闭最近一项待处理的澄清请求，并成为智能体的下一条输入。 Users can complete the card or send a free-form chat message to bypass it; that message closes the latest pending clarification and becomes the agent's next input. 随附的回答文本保留在执行步骤面板之外；回应请求后，智能体继续运行时，对话中此前已完成的文本仍会保留。 Accompanying answer text stays outside the execution steps panel, and answering the request keeps previously completed text in the conversation while the agent continues.
在同一浏览器标签页内重新加载页面或切换对话后，Web UI 输入框中尚未发送的草稿仍会保留。 Unsent Web UI composer drafts survive page reloads and switching between conversations within the same browser tab. 草稿按用户、智能体和对话相互隔离，包含所选技能和显式对话引用，并在发送被接受后清除。 Drafts are isolated by user, agent, and conversation, include the selected skill and explicit conversation references, and are cleared once a send is accepted. 已附加到线程的项目文档在重新加载后也会保留；浏览器本地上传的文件和引用的消息摘录不会被持久化。 Project documents already attached to the thread also survive reloads; browser-local uploads and quoted message excerpts are not persisted.
如果附件上传报告 `success: false`，即使 HTTP 响应为 200，Web UI 也会停止发送。 If an attachment upload reports `success: false`, the Web UI stops the send even when the HTTP response is 200. 系统会显示上传错误，编辑器会保留草稿及附件，以便修正或重试。 The upload error is shown and the composer keeps its draft and attachments for correction or retry. 部分成功的上传中已被接受的文件会保留在服务器上；此操作不会回滚这些文件。 Files already accepted by a partially successful upload remain on the server; this does not roll them back.
文档转换会读取当前生效的 PDF 转换器设置，并在后台工作线程中解析文档，避免在 Gateway 事件循环中执行配置文件检查。 Document conversion reads the live PDF-converter setting and parses documents in a background worker, keeping configuration-file checks off the Gateway event loop.
Web UI 编辑器支持在光标处或通过 `@` 按钮插入 `@` 引用。 The Web UI composer supports `@` references at the cursor or through the `@` button. 可在分组选择器中搜索已启用的技能、当前项目中的文档以及其他对话；选中的引用会与草稿文本一起以内联形式显示，并可通过常规编辑操作删除。 Search enabled skills, documents in the current project, and other conversations in a grouped picker; selected references appear inline alongside the draft text and can be removed with normal editing. 一条消息中可以选择多个技能（最多 16 个不同的技能）；再次选择已勾选的技能会将其移除，每次选择都会依据当前用户注册表和智能体允许列表进行校验。 Multiple skills can be selected in one message (up to 16 unique skills); selecting a checked skill again removes it, with each selection checked against the current user registry and agent allowlist. 项目文件只有在摄取成功后才会作为附件添加；对话引用需要 `read_conversation` 能力，并仍受该能力每次运行的数量限制。 Project files are attached only after ingestion succeeds; conversation references require the `read_conversation` capability and retain its per-run limit. 如果能力发现失败，已保存的对话引用会保持完整，发送操作会等待重试成功后再继续。 If capability discovery fails, saved conversation references remain intact and sending waits for a successful retry. 润色功能在重新排列指令时会保留完整的引用标签。 Polishing preserves complete reference labels when instructions are reordered. 文件分组还提供本地上传功能。仍支持 `/goal`、`/compact` 和旧版 `/skill-name` 输入。 The file group also offers local uploads. `/goal`, `/compact`, and legacy `/skill-name` input remain supported.
选取内联引用后，可紧接着在其后继续输入。 After picking an inline reference, typing continues immediately after it. 移动光标后，光标会保持在所选位置，包括编辑引用旁的文本时。 Moving the cursor keeps the position you chose, including when editing text beside a reference.
当浏览器提供 Web Speech API 时，Web UI 编辑器还支持基于浏览器的语音听写。 The Web UI composer also supports browser-based voice dictation when the browser exposes the Web Speech API. 麦克风按钮仅将语音转写到本地草稿中；DeerFlow 只接收转写后的文本，音频处理则依据所在环境的策略，交由浏览器或操作系统的语音识别服务完成。 The microphone button transcribes speech into the local draft only; DeerFlow receives only the transcribed text, while audio handling is delegated to the browser or operating system speech-recognition service according to that environment's policy. 用户可以在发送前检查或编辑文本。 Users can review or edit the text before sending. 当浏览器裁剪转写片段两端的空白时，听写功能会保持词语之间的分隔，保留中文和日文的连续衔接，并将片段开头的标点符号和百分号直接接在前文之后，不添加空格。 Dictation keeps words separated when browsers trim transcript segments, preserves Chinese and Japanese joins, and attaches leading punctuation and percent signs without adding spaces.
在标准对话和自定义智能体对话中，Web UI 都会在编辑器下方显示本地化的 AI 生成内容免责声明，提醒用户核实重要的 The Web UI displays a localized AI-generated-content disclaimer below the composer in both standard and custom-agent conversations, reminding users to verify important
信息。 information.
首轮运行即使被中断，仍会持久化保存一个备用对话标题，因此停止流式响应后，刷新页面不会使该对话仍显示为“未命名”。 Interrupted first-turn runs still persist a fallback conversation title, so stopping a streaming response does not leave the thread as "Untitled" after refresh.
通过状态 API 更新子图检查点的标题时，根对话的标题保持不变。 Updating a subgraph checkpoint's title through the state API keeps the root conversation's title unchanged.
流式 Markdown 响应仅对新到达的词语应用动画；当下一个数据块扩展同一内容块时，已显示的文本不会淡出并重新播放动画。 Streaming Markdown responses animate only newly arrived words; text that is already visible is not faded out and replayed when the next chunk extends the same block.
在 Web UI 中，可以从已完成的助手轮次创建分支，形成新的主对话。 In the Web UI, completed assistant turns can be branched into a new main conversation. 自动继承的分支标题使用下一个未占用、与语言无关的数字后缀（`Title (2)`，随后另一个同级分支或该带编号对话的分支使用 `Title (3)`），使生成的同级标题保持各不相同，而无需持久化保存特定语言的标签；匹配此格式的显式指定或重命名后的同级标题也会占用其显示的后缀，即使它们不包含自动生成的序号元数据。 Automatically inherited branch titles use the next free language-neutral numeric suffix (`Title (2)`, then `Title (3)` for another sibling or a branch of the numbered conversation) so generated sibling titles remain distinct without persisting a locale-specific label; matching explicit or renamed sibling titles also reserve their displayed suffix even though they carry no generated sequence metadata. 通过 API 显式指定的标题会予以保留。 An explicit API title is preserved. 重命名分支会清除其自动生成的序号，因此下一次自动创建分支时，会以重命名后的标题为基础，从 `(2)` 开始编号。 Renaming a branch clears its generated sequence, so its next automatic branch starts from the renamed title at `(2)`. “最近聊天”还会将已加载的分支归组到已加载的父对话正下方，并以淡雅的树形连接线标示层级关系。 Recent chats also groups loaded branches directly beneath a loaded parent with subtle tree connectors. 父对话缺失、谱系格式错误或存在循环，以及置顶状态不同的分支，都会继续显示在顶层，而不会被隐藏或跨越置顶分界移动。 Missing parents, malformed or cyclic lineage, and branches in a different pinned state stay visible at the top level instead of being hidden or moved across the pinned boundary. 新对话从该轮次的检查点开始，并保留此前的重放检查点，因此可以立即重新生成分支中的响应。 The new thread starts from that turn's checkpoint and keeps the preceding replay checkpoint, so the branched response can be regenerated immediately. 最新响应在中断后也可以重新生成，即使其流式传输的部分文本从未保存到检查点中。 The latest response can also be regenerated after an interruption, even when its streamed partial text never reached a checkpoint. 在回答中途停止时，对话消息流中已通过流式传输显示的助手文本会被保留，因此刷新页面不再丢失已显示的进展；这些部分文本仍不属于权威的已完成检查点状态。 Stopping mid-answer retains the assistant text that was already streamed in the thread's message feed, so refreshing no longer discards visible progress; that partial text remains outside authoritative completed checkpoint state. 重新生成最新响应会保留对话的当前标题，包括在原始响应后手动重命名的标题。 Regenerating the latest response preserves the thread's current title, including a title you renamed manually after the original response. 对于缺少检查点父链接的旧版或导入历史记录，系统会使用按时间顺序进行有限范围查找的回退机制；如果不存在更早的重放检查点，仍可采用旧版的单检查点结构成功创建分支，但无法重新生成继承的响应。 Legacy or imported histories without checkpoint parent links use a bounded chronological fallback; if no earlier replay checkpoint exists, branching still succeeds with the legacy single-checkpoint shape, while regeneration remains unavailable for that inherited response. 现有的单检查点分支会保持不变，不会尝试进行不安全的检查点复制。 Existing single-checkpoint branches are left unchanged rather than attempting an unsafe checkpoint copy. 由于工作区文件不会保存到检查点中，只有从最新轮次创建分支时，系统才会尽力将当前工作区复制到分支中；从较早轮次创建分支时，仅保留恢复的消息历史，以确保分支不会继承对话后续阶段创建的文件。 Because workspace files are not checkpointed, the branch only receives a best-effort copy of the current workspace when you branch from the latest turn; branching from an older turn keeps just the restored message history so the branch never inherits files that were created in a later part of the conversation.
分支会保留源对话中有效的智能体选择。 Branches preserve the source conversation's valid agent selection. 旧版对话的分支若使用了无效的智能体 ID，则改用主智能体。 A branch of a legacy conversation with an invalid agent ID uses the lead agent instead.
Web UI 在每次运行中报告一次已完成任务的耗时。 The Web UI reports completed task time once per run. 这是总实际经过时间，包括模型推理、工具调用和等待时间，而非单个步骤的耗时或仅模型的思考时长。 This is total wall-clock time—including model reasoning, tool calls, and waiting—not a per-step or model-only thinking duration. 推理内容仍可通过其独立的展开区域查看。 Reasoning content remains available through its own separate disclosure.
在响应以流式方式输出时，仅包含推理的消息会保留在处理面板中，包括 Anthropic 思考块。 While a response streams, reasoning-only messages stay in the processing panel, including Anthropic thinking blocks. 一旦答案内容与推理一同到达，答案内容就会显示在助手消息气泡中。 Once answer content arrives alongside reasoning, it appears in an assistant bubble.
围栏代码块、缩进代码块或行内代码中的字面量 `` 标签会保留在答案及其复制文本中，而不会被移至推理展开区域。 Literal `` tags in fenced, indented, or inline code remain part of the answer and its copied text, rather than being moved into the reasoning disclosure. 这也包括在列表项所在行开启的代码围栏：代码之后的真正推理内容仍会被提取。 This includes fences opened on list-item lines: real reasoning after the code is still extracted. 流式输出期间，段落内尚未闭合的行内代码片段会得到保留，但遇到空行或起中断作用的标题、列表、分隔线或代码围栏时便会结束。 Unfinished inline code spans are preserved while streaming within a paragraph, but end at a blank line or an interrupting heading, list, thematic break or fence. 段落的缩进续行不会开启代码块。 Indented paragraph continuations do not start a code block.
在 Web UI 中，也可以通过消息工具栏编辑并重新运行最近一个已完成的用户轮次。 In the Web UI, the latest completed user turn can also be edited and rerun from the message toolbar. DeerFlow 会恢复到该用户消息之前的对话检查点，将编辑后的文本作为新的用户消息提交，并在重放开始进行或成功后隐藏被替代的轮次。 DeerFlow restores the conversation checkpoint before that user message, submits the edited text as a new user message, and hides the superseded turn once the replay is in progress or succeeds. 这仅重放对话状态：文件、记忆更新以及外部工具的副作用均不会被撤销。 This is a conversation-state replay only: files, memory updates, and external tool side effects are not undone.
Web UI 的聊天链接会先对自定义线程标识符进行百分号编码，再将其放入路由路径段，因此 `#` 和 `?` 等 URL 保留字符不会改变所打开的对话。 Web UI chat links percent-encode custom thread identifiers before placing them in route segments, so reserved URL characters such as `#` and `?` do not change which conversation is opened.
聊天归档Chat Archive
从侧边栏删除聊天时，需要在显示其标题的确认提示中进行确认。 Deleting a chat from the sidebar requires confirmation showing its title. 删除操作会移除对话及其文件，且无法撤销。 Deletion removes the conversation and its files and cannot be undone.
使用最近聊天的侧边栏菜单中的**归档聊天**，可隐藏已完成的工作，同时保留其消息、文件和原始链接。 Use **Archive chat** in a recent chat's sidebar menu to hide completed work while keeping its messages, files, and original link. 成功提示消息提供**撤销**选项。 The success message offers **Undo**. 打开**聊天 → 已归档**，可查找已归档的对话并逐一恢复；已打开的归档对话也会在顶部显示恢复按钮。 Open **Chats → Archived** to find archived conversations and restore them individually; an open archived conversation also shows a restore button in its header. 搜索会按标题筛选已加载的对话，可通过**加载更多**查看更早的条目。 Search filters the titles of loaded conversations, with **Load more** for older entries.
归档和恢复操作会保留聊天的活动时间和置顶状态。 Archive and restore preserve the chat's activity time and pinned state. 归档不会停止正在运行的任务或暂停其计划任务，新活动也不会自动恢复该聊天。 Archiving does not stop a running task or pause its schedules, and new activity does not automatically restore it. 如果打算移除对话及其文件，请使用现有的删除操作。 Use the existing Delete action when you intend to remove a conversation and its files.
会话目标Session Goals
使用 `/goal ` 为当前线程附加一个有效的完成条件。 Use `/goal ` to attach one active completion condition to the current thread. 目标是作用域限定于线程的状态，而非技能激活，因此它会跨轮次持续生效，直到 DeerFlow 判定其已满足，或你将其清除。 The goal is thread-scoped state, not a skill activation, so it stays active across turns until DeerFlow determines it has been satisfied or you clear it. 智能体在每次模型调用时都能看到当前有效的目标，因此，即使上下文压缩移除了陈述该目标的消息，它仍会继续朝该目标推进。 The agent sees the active goal on every model call, so it keeps working toward it after context compaction removes the message that stated it.
支持的命令： Supported commands: 
/goal finish the implementation and make all tests pass
/goal              # show the active goal
/goal clear        # clear it
每次由 Gateway 支持的运行结束后，DeerFlow 都会使用一个不进行思考的评估模型，依据当前有效的目标评估可见对话，包括助手的工具调用及经过缩短的工具结果。 After each Gateway-backed run, DeerFlow evaluates the visible conversation, including the assistant's tool calls and shortened tool results, against the active goal with a non-thinking evaluator model. 仅有成功的工具结果并不意味着目标已满足；如果助手不得不猜测缺失或含糊的信息，评估器会报告 `needs_user_input`。 A successful tool result alone does not satisfy a goal, and when the assistant had to guess missing or ambiguous information the evaluator reports `needs_user_input`. 评估器必须返回一个带类型的阻碍项（`missing_evidence`、`needs_user_input`、`run_failed`、`external_wait` 或 `goal_not_met_yet`），并附上可见证据。 The evaluator must return a typed blocker (`missing_evidence`, `needs_user_input`, `run_failed`, `external_wait`, or `goal_not_met_yet`) plus visible evidence. 只有当最新的助手轮次已持久化保存为检查点、阻碍项为 `goal_not_met_yet`、线程在评估期间未发生变化，且无进展熔断机制尚未触发时，DeerFlow 才会注入隐藏的继续执行请求。 DeerFlow only injects a hidden continuation when the latest assistant turn is durably checkpointed, the blocker is `goal_not_met_yet`, the thread did not change during evaluation, and the no-progress breaker has not fired. 安全上限默认为 8 次隐藏的继续执行；对于重复且完全相同的无进展评估，会在 2 次尝试后停止。`/goal clear` 和用户撰写的任何新输入都优先于已排队的继续执行请求。 The safety cap defaults to 8 hidden continuations, and repeated identical non-progress evaluations stop after 2 attempts. `/goal clear` and any user-authored new input win over queued continuations. 当评估器认为目标已满足时，DeerFlow 会在运行成功完成收尾并发布更新后的线程状态后清除该目标。 When the evaluator considers the goal satisfied, DeerFlow clears it after the run successfully finalizes and publishes the updated thread state. 同一检查点会将完成结果记录为 `goal_outcome`，该记录会一直保留到下一次目标变更。 The same checkpoint records the completion as `goal_outcome`, which stays until the next goal change. 如果必需的产物交付或其接收回执的持久化失败，运行会报告错误，目标则保持有效，以便稍后重试；这不会启动另一次隐藏的继续执行。 If required artifact delivery or its receipt persistence fails, the run reports an error and the goal remains active for a later retry; this does not start another hidden continuation.
评估器会在固定的证据预算内保留近期工作和最新的用户请求，并利用剩余空间保留较长用户输入中的更多内容。 The evaluator keeps recent work and the latest user request within a fixed evidence budget, using spare room to retain more of long user input.
如果正常完成的评估器响应所采用的判定格式无效，DeerFlow 会使用同一份证据再评估一次，除非你取消操作或更改线程。 If a normally completed evaluator response has an invalid verdict format, DeerFlow evaluates the same evidence once more unless you cancel or change the thread. 两次调用都会计入次数；第二次失败后会停止自动继续执行，而不会重复执行任务或其工具调用。 Both calls are counted; a second failure stops automatic continuation without repeating the task or its tools.
对于使用工具的轮次，成功的操作结果决定无进展计数：仅更改回复措辞或沙箱工具的 UI 说明不会重置该计数，而新观察到的工作进展可以使其继续运行。 For tool-using turns, successful operation results drive the no-progress count: changing only reply wording or a sandbox tool's UI explanation does not reset it, while newly observed work can keep it going. 纯文本轮次仍采用基于回复的检查。 Text-only turns keep their reply-based check. 目标尚未达成时，在触发 token 或循环检测的硬性停止条件后，或运行达到步数上限时，自动继续仍会停止，但目标会保持活动状态，以供用户在后续轮次中继续处理。 An unmet goal still stops automatic continuation after a token or loop-detection hard stop, or when the run reaches its step limit, and stays active for a later user turn.
重新生成较早的回复时，会保留聊天当前设置的目标及其已使用的继续执行预算。 Regenerating an earlier response keeps the goal currently set for the chat and its used continuation budget. 这不会恢复已清除或已达成的目标。 It does not restore a goal that was cleared or achieved. 重放较早回答的历史检查点时，会移除该回答的完成记录。 The earlier answer's completion record is removed when its historical checkpoint is replayed.
Web UI 会在输入框上方显示当前活动目标，说明自动继续停止的原因，并在目标达成后显示“目标已达成”通知。 The Web UI shows the active goal above the composer, says why auto-continue stopped, and shows a "Goal met" notice after a goal is met. 设置了目标时，无法编辑最后一条消息。 Editing the last message is off while a goal is set. TUI 和受支持的即时通信（IM）渠道也提供相同命令。 The same command is available from the TUI and supported IM channels. 在 IM 中，`/goal` 还会说明目标是否正在进行、自动继续停止的原因，或目标已达成（此状态保留至下一次目标变更），所用语言为绑定所有者的 Web UI 语言（否则使用 `channel_connections.notification_locale`）。 In IM, `/goal` also says whether the goal is in progress, why auto-continue stopped, or that it was met (until the next goal change), in the binding owner's web UI language (else `channel_connections.notification_locale`). 在 Web UI 和受支持的 IM 渠道中，设置 `/goal ` 还会以该条件为任务启动一次运行；状态和清除命令仅管理目标状态。 In the Web UI and supported IM channels, setting `/goal ` also starts a run with the condition as the task; status and clear commands only manage goal state. 当线程中有运行正在进行时，设置或清除目标的操作会被拒绝，包括由其他 Gateway 工作进程负责的运行，以防目标检查点偏离活动运行的检查点谱系而形成分支。 Setting or clearing a goal is rejected while that thread has a run in flight, including a run owned by another Gateway worker, so the goal checkpoint cannot branch away from an active run's checkpoint lineage. IM 回复会说明拒绝原因，并请你等待任务完成；目标保持不变，被拒绝的命令不会自动重试。 IM replies explain this rejection and ask you to wait for the task to finish; the goal stays unchanged and the rejected command is not retried automatically.
当你的角色缺少 `runs:create` 权限时，Web UI 会在准备线程或保存目标之前拒绝新任务或 `/goal `，并保留草稿以供重试。 When your role lacks `runs:create`, the Web UI rejects a new task or `/goal ` before preparing the thread or saving the goal, and keeps your draft for retry. 目标状态查询、目标清除和 `/compact` 仍分别受其各自端点权限的约束。 Goal status, goal clearing, and `/compact` remain governed by their own endpoint permissions.
手动上下文压缩Manual Context Compaction
自动压缩和手动压缩都会按时间顺序处理所有选定的历史记录 Automatic and manual compaction process all selected history in chronological
分块，然后再替换这些历史记录。`summarization.trim_tokens_to_summarize` 限制每一步的原始 chunks before replacing it. `summarization.trim_tokens_to_summarize` bounds raw
输入量；生成的摘要最多使用该额度的一半，以便为源内容留出空间。 input per step; generated summaries use at most half to leave room for source.
超长摘要会保留带标记的头部和尾部文本；原始历史记录绝不会被截断。 Oversized summaries retain marked head/tail text; raw history is never clipped.

启用 `task_continuity.enabled` 后，`history_search` 会搜索当前任务的活动 With `task_continuity.enabled`, `history_search` searches the current task's active

Web UI 在合并历史记录与实时更新时，会保留已持久化消息的顺序。 The Web UI preserves persisted message order when merging history with live updates. 在已加载的历史记录中，围绕某个已持久化结果的流式步骤会保持在一起，包括在该结果之后到达的步骤。 Streaming steps around a persisted result inside the loaded history stay together, including steps that arrive after the result. 当历史记录尚未刷新且 UI 尚未渲染这些步骤时，压缩期间捕获的步骤也仍会显示在其对应的已持久化结果之前。 Steps captured during compaction also remain visible before their persisted result when history has not refreshed and the UI has not rendered them yet.
压缩会保留当前用户请求，并对较早的助手/工具活动进行摘要。 Compaction keeps the current user request and summarizes older assistant/tool activity. 当保留该请求使摘要窗口中仅剩助手消息和工具消息时，输入裁剪会优先保留其中最新的内容。 When rescuing that request leaves an assistant/tool-only summary window, input trimming favors its most recent content. 对于用户消息锚点位于裁剪预算之外的混合历史记录，压缩会保留现有的末条消息回退机制。`summarization.trim_tokens_to_summarize`（默认为 4000）控制原始摘要输入的裁剪；转义和提示词格式化会产生超出该预算的额外开销。 For mixed histories whose user-message anchor falls outside the trimming budget, compaction retains the existing final-message fallback. `summarization.trim_tokens_to_summarize` (4000 by default) controls trimming of the raw summary input; escaping and prompt formatting add overhead beyond that budget. 将此选项设为 `null` 会禁用摘要模型的输入裁剪；只有当模型能够接受待压缩的完整历史记录时，才应选择此设置。 Setting this option to `null` disables input trimming for the summary model; choose that only when the model can accept the full history being compacted.
如果再次出现相同的已渲染摘要提示词和候选模型，且第一个候选模型此前曾原样返回现有摘要，DeerFlow 就会复用该摘要。 If the same rendered summary prompt and model candidates recur, DeerFlow reuses the existing summary when the first candidate previously returned it unchanged. 原样返回的回退响应不会被缓存，因此，复用机制不会阻止在调用失败后重试主模型。 An unchanged fallback response is not cached, so reuse cannot prevent the primary model from being retried after an invocation failure. 缓存失败时会回退到正常生成流程。 Cache failures fall back to normal generation. 有关摘要结果和复用计数器，请参阅[摘要遥测](backend/docs/summarization.md#reuse-and-telemetry)。 See [summarization telemetry](backend/docs/summarization.md#reuse-and-telemetry) for the summary result and reuse counters.
在 Web UI 的消息输入框中使用 `/compact`，可对当前线程中较早的上下文进行摘要。 Use `/compact` in the Web UI composer to summarize older context for the current thread. DeerFlow 会保持完整聊天记录可见，但后续模型调用会使用压缩后的摘要和近期消息。 DeerFlow keeps the full chat visible, but future model calls use the compacted summary plus recent messages. 当历史记录不足以进行压缩时，该命令会被忽略；当线程中有正在执行的运行时，该命令会被阻止，即使该运行由另一个 Gateway 工作进程负责也不例外。 The command is ignored when there is not enough history to compact, and it is blocked while the thread has a run in flight, including when that run is owned by another Gateway worker. 如果多工作进程环境中的预留失去租约，DeerFlow 会在替代运行继续执行之前取消检查点写入器，并在清理完成后返回可重试的冲突。 If a multi-worker reservation loses its lease, DeerFlow cancels the checkpoint writer before the replacing run proceeds and returns a retryable conflict after cleanup. 线程标题编辑通过同一状态写入边界串行处理；当存在活动运行时，会显示冲突，同时保持重命名对话框打开。 Thread-title edits are serialized through the same state-write boundary and show a conflict without closing the rename dialog when a run is active.
当执行处于待处理状态，或检查点不可用、因而无法确认线程是否空闲时，手动压缩也会返回 HTTP 409。 Manual compaction also returns HTTP 409 when execution is pending or the checkpoint is unavailable, so it cannot confirm the thread is idle.
当所选模型配置了正值的 `context_window` 时，聊天标题栏还会显示上下文窗口用量指示器。 The chat header also shows a context-window gauge when the selected model has a positive `context_window` configured. 它会估算最新已物化检查点中消息的词元数，并在重新获取数据期间继续显示同一线程先前的百分比，不受累计词元用量设置的影响。 It estimates the latest materialized checkpoint's message tokens and keeps the previous same-thread percentage visible while data refetches, independently of the cumulative token-usage setting.
当 `token_budget.enabled` 和 `token_usage.enabled` 均启用时，每次主智能体运行的预算都会计入已完成的子智能体用量，包括最后一批。 When `token_budget.enabled` and `token_usage.enabled` are both enabled, each lead-agent run budget includes completed subagent usage, including the final batch. 隐藏的目标续行共享该预算；新的用户运行会重新开始计算。 Hidden goal continuations share the budget; a new user run starts fresh.
子智能体Sub-Agents
Web 聊天支持直接指定模型，例如“让 MODEL 处理此事”“本轮使用 MODEL”和“从现在起使用 MODEL”；也支持对应的中文表达。 Web chats accept direct model choices such as “ask MODEL to handle this”, “use MODEL for this turn”, and “from now on use MODEL”; Chinese equivalents are supported too. 为子智能体选择模型不会改变主模型，单轮选择不会改变默认设置，而会话级选择会使用现有的浏览器本地线程覆盖设置，不会更改账户偏好。 A child choice leaves the main model unchanged, a one-turn choice leaves defaults unchanged, and a conversation choice uses the existing browser-local thread override without changing account preferences. 模型选择会根据可用配置和可选的 `models[].aliases` 进行解析；名称或作用范围存在歧义时，会在执行前显示选择卡片，而委派仍要求启用子智能体（Ultra 模式）。 Choices resolve against available configurations and optional `models[].aliases`; ambiguous names or scopes show a selection card before execution, while delegation still requires enabled subagents (Ultra mode).
当子智能体以 `return_direct=True` 的工具结束执行时，包括由扩展中间件提供的工具，其输出会按工具调用顺序返回。 When a sub-agent ends with `return_direct=True` tools, including tools contributed by extension middleware, their outputs are returned in tool-call order. 工具失败会将任务标记为失败，同时保留该批次的输出。 A failed tool marks the task as failed while preserving the batch outputs.
流关闭后，被取消的子智能体会继续占用其执行槽位，直到 After its stream closes, a cancelled sub-agent keeps its execution slot until
沙箱清理和扩展任务停止通知按照其现有的 sandbox cleanup and extension task-stop notifications finish under their existing
错误和超时策略完成。 error and timeout policies. 重复取消不会放弃此清理过程。 Repeated cancellation does not abandon this cleanup.
任务停止会将被中断的执行报告为已中止，同时保留任何在取消前已发布的已完成、 Task-stop reports an interrupted execution as aborted, preserving any completed,
失败或超时结果。 failed or timed-out outcome already published before cancellation.

智能体库。**导出智能体** 会下载一个带有版本信息的 Agents gallery. **Export Agent** downloads a versioned

选择一个本地名称。 choose a local name. 无效的包会显示验证消息，以便你修正 Invalid packages show validation messages so you can correct
该包并重试。 the package and retry. 导入操作绝不会覆盖现有智能体。 Imports never overwrite an existing agent. 包中包含 Packages carry
模型、工具/MCP、技能、知识、子智能体、推理和记忆策略 model, tool/MCP, skill, knowledge, sub-agent, reasoning, and memory-policy
设置，但有意排除记忆内容、对话、凭据， settings, but deliberately exclude memory contents, conversations, credentials,
以及归运营者所有的 GitHub 绑定。 and operator-owned GitHub bindings.
子智能体是一种优化手段，而不是应对复杂请求的默认方式。 Sub-agents are an optimization, not the default response to a complex request.
当“停止”操作在委派任务返回回复之前将其中断后，用户的下一 After Stop interrupts a delegated task before it returns a reply, the next user
轮交互会在智能体的持久上下文中将先前的任务标记为已取消，以便它 turn marks that earlier task as cancelled in the agent's durable context so it
可以重试。 can retry. 现有回复会予以保留。 Existing replies are preserved. 缺少状态元数据的旧回复 Older replies without status metadata
可能仍显示为进行中；不会根据其文本推断其结果。 may still appear in progress; their outcome is not inferred from their text.
持久化委派台账根据运行和提供方工具调用 The durable delegation ledger distinguishes calls by run and provider tool-call
ID 来区分调用，因此后续用户轮次可以复用某个 ID，而不会替换先前的工作或 ID, so a later user turn can reuse an ID without replacing earlier work or
丢失该轮次中按运行分别统计的委派次数。 losing that turn's per-run delegation count.
当委派在实际并行带来的延迟缩短、专业能力或上下文隔离方面具有明确的净收益时，主代理可以即时创建子代理，每个子代理都有各自限定范围的上下文、工具和终止条件。 The lead agent can spawn sub-agents on the fly — each with its own scoped context, tools, and termination conditions — when delegation has clear net benefit from real parallel latency, specialist capability, or context isolation. 主代理会避免并行分派范围相互依赖或副作用相互重叠的任务；当专业能力或上下文隔离带来的收益明显占优时，仍可在一个子代理中执行有界的顺序任务链。 It keeps interdependent scopes and overlapping side effects out of parallel dispatch; a bounded sequential chain can still run in one sub-agent when specialist or context-isolation benefit clearly wins. 主代理使用能够发挥作用的最少数量的子代理，并重新评估后续批次，而不会仅因任务规模大或包含多个步骤就进行扇出分派。 The lead uses the fewest useful sub-agents and re-evaluates later batches instead of fanning out solely because a task is large or multi-step. 子代理返回结构化结果，主代理对其进行验证并综合为连贯的输出。 Sub-agents report back structured results, and the lead agent verifies and synthesizes them into a coherent output. 确定性的工具回执既涵盖直接工具消息，也涵盖更新状态的 `Command` 结果，例如委派的 `task` 响应；当回执台账达到其上下文预算上限时，它会保留最新的操作及其原始回执 ID。 Deterministic tool receipts cover both direct tool messages and state-updating `Command` results such as delegated `task` responses; when the receipt ledger reaches its context budget, it retains the newest actions and their original receipt IDs. 操作人员可以通过 `verification.receipts_enabled: false` 禁用这一溯源层。 Operators can disable this provenance layer with `verification.receipts_enabled: false`. 子代理配置的技能从与主代理相同的用户范围目录中解析，因此用户自有的自定义技能仍然可用，同时不会暴露其他用户的版本。 Their configured skills are resolved from the same user-scoped catalog as the lead agent, so user-owned custom skills remain available without exposing another user's version. 子代理内部的 AI 消息和工具消息仅限于委派图内，不会进入父级聊天流。 Their internal AI and tool messages stay scoped to the delegated graph instead of entering the parent chat stream. 重新加载的线程历史也强制执行相同的边界：通过回调捕获的子代理 AI 响应仍可在运行事件诊断信息中查看，但不包含在父级对话记录中，而父级 `task` 结果仍附在其子任务卡片上。 Reloaded thread history enforces the same boundary: callback-captured sub-agent AI responses remain available in run-event diagnostics but are excluded from the parent transcript, while the parent `task` result remains attached to its subtask card. 启用摘要功能时，长时间运行的子代理会压缩较早的历史记录，并在继续执行前将摘要作为受保护、隐藏的持久上下文重新注入，从而使近期的助手和工具活动始终以任务为依据。 Long-running sub-agents compact older history when summarization is enabled and re-inject the summary as guarded, hidden durable context before continuing, so recent assistant/tool activity remains grounded in the task. 子代理的系统指令，包括角色和报告约定，会在压缩后保留；如果将被摘要的内容仅有这些指令和当前请求，则跳过压缩。 Their system instructions, including the role and report contract, survive compaction; if only those instructions and the current request would be summarized, compaction is skipped. 提供方或模型的请求失败会被报告为失败的子代理任务，而非成功结果，以便主代理和 Web UI 正确作出响应。 Provider/model request failures are reported as failed sub-agent tasks rather than successful results, so the lead agent and Web UI can react to them correctly. 并发的父级运行也会获得独立的服务端子代理执行 ID，因此，即使提供方复用了工具调用 ID，也无法导致某次运行轮询、取消或清理另一次运行的后台任务。 Concurrent parent runs also receive independent server-side sub-agent execution IDs, so a provider that reuses a tool-call ID cannot make one run poll, cancel, or clean up another run's background task. 折叠的子代理卡片会显示实际使用的模型；当提供方返回用量元数据时，还会显示累计 token 总数，该数值在每次子代理 LLM 调用完成后更新，并在重新加载后保留。 Collapsed sub-agent cards show the effective model and, when the provider returns usage metadata, a cumulative token total that updates after each completed sub-agent LLM call and persists after a reload. 启用 token 用量跟踪时，已完成的子代理用量会根据该次运行最终工具消息中的元数据归属到发起分派的步骤，而不是依赖进程全局的提供方 ID 缓存。 When token usage tracking is enabled, completed sub-agent usage is attributed back to the dispatching step from that run's terminal tool-message metadata rather than a process-global provider-ID cache.
当展开的子代理卡片加载持久化的步骤时，延迟到达的历史响应 When expanded sub-agent cards load persisted steps, delayed history responses
会补齐时间线中缺失的条目，而不会用截断的预览替换已经接收到的完整步骤 fill missing timeline entries without replacing complete steps already received
这些完整步骤来自实时流。 from the live stream with truncated previews.
对于无法识别的结构化任务状态，子代理卡片会将其保持为进行中。 Sub-agent cards keep unrecognized structured task statuses in progress. 随附的 Accompanying
结果文本不会将未知状态变为成功或失败。 result text does not turn an unknown status into success or failure.
被取消或超时的后台子代理执行会保留提供方报告的已完成模型调用的 token 用量，其中包括在下一次进度更新之前收到的响应。 Cancelled or timed-out background sub-agent executions retain provider-reported token usage from completed model calls, including responses received before their next progress update. 向父级运行交付最终用量时，不会重复计算先前的进度快照。 Final usage delivery to the parent run does not count earlier progress snapshots twice.
对于 `tests_passed:go test ./...`，标记为 `[no test files]` 或 `[no tests to run]` 的包不会否定另一个包的测试通过汇总。 For `tests_passed:go test ./...`, packages marked `[no test files]` or `[no tests to run]` do not veto a passing summary from another package. 仅包含零测试包汇总的运行仍为 `UNVERIFIED`；失败仍具有优先权。 Runs with only zero-test package summaries remain `UNVERIFIED`; failures still take precedence. 在 `-v` 模式下，如果某条记录中各测试用例对应的行全为 `--- SKIP`，该记录也仍为 `UNVERIFIED`：Go 以 0 退出并打印相同的 `ok` 提示，因此没有任何测试实际运行并通过。 Under `-v`, a record whose per-case lines are all `--- SKIP` also remains `UNVERIFIED`: Go exits 0 and prints the same `ok` banner, so nothing ran to pass. 非详细输出无法区分全部跳过的包与测试通过的包，因此仍按记录内容予以信任。 Non-verbose output cannot separate an all-skipped package from a passing one and stays trusted as recorded. 这检查的是记录的执行证据，而非声明的正确性。 This checks recorded execution evidence, not claim correctness.
