普通 `task` 也会收到调度该任务的运行实例当前上传内容的防御性快照。 An ordinary `task` also receives a defensive snapshot of the dispatching run's current uploads. 这使符合条件的子代理能够使用 `list_uploaded_files` 查找先前轮次的文件，同时不会将本轮附件作为历史上传返回。 This lets eligible sub-agents use `list_uploaded_files` to find earlier-turn files without returning same-turn attachments as historical. 延迟启动或恢复运行的 `batch_task` 工作进程会保持此工具禁用，因为它们没有有效的本轮上传边界。 Delayed or recovered `batch_task` workers leave this tool disabled because they have no valid turn-local upload boundary.
通过嵌入式 Python 上传时，保存的文档及其 Markdown 转换结果使用同一份已捕获的内容，即使调用方的源内容在上传期间发生变化也是如此。 Embedded Python uploads use the same captured content for the saved document and its Markdown conversion, even if the caller's source changes during upload. 转换后的 Markdown 在文件名冲突时会获得一个带数字后缀的未占用文件名，从而保留用户编写的 Markdown 和先前上传的配套文件。 Converted Markdown receives a free filename with a numeric suffix on collision, preserving user-authored Markdown and companions from earlier uploads. 上传响应会报告实际的配套文件名。 The upload response reports the actual companion filename.

原生子代理发生硬超时时，会在执行截止时刻发布 `TIMED_OUT`，同时清理工作继续在监督下进行。 Native subagent hard timeouts publish `TIMED_OUT` at the execution deadline while cleanup continues under supervision. 持久化批处理工作进程最多等待 60 秒以完成清理，然后记录已发布的结果并应用配置的重试策略。 Durable batch workers wait up to 60 seconds for cleanup before recording the published outcome and applying the configured retry policy. 尚未结束的执行会一直占用容量和沙箱租约，直至拆除完成；应确保工作具有幂等性，因为重试可能与延迟进行的清理重叠。 Unfinished executions retain capacity and sandbox leases until teardown finishes; keep work idempotent because retries may overlap late cleanup.
持久化 `batch_task` 工作进程使用同一份由应用持有的插件快照来组装工具并执行任务。 Durable `batch_task` workers use one app-owned plugin snapshot for tool assembly and execution. Gateway 重启后，恢复的任务会采用新工作进程的插件快照；插件对象绝不会存储在持久化任务记录中。 Recovered tasks adopt the new worker's plugin snapshot after a Gateway restart; plugin objects are never stored in durable task records.
普通 `task` 委派与显式的持久化 `batch_task` 执行共享启动时确定的 `subagent_runtime` 进程容量。 Ordinary `task` delegation and explicit durable `batch_task` execution share the startup-scoped `subagent_runtime` process capacity. 批处理模式将大量独立条目存储在 SQL 数据库中，分别设置总量、活跃条目数和运行条目数限制，并支持重启恢复、大小受限的结果以及线程范围内的 Web UI 面板。 Batch mode keeps large independent item sets in SQL with separate total, live, and running limits, restart recovery, bounded results, and a thread-scoped Web UI panel. 该面板按需分页显示大小受限的预览，并打开所选条目已存储的报告和已捕获的证据以供审查。 The panel pages through bounded previews on demand and opens a selected item’s stored report and captured evidence for review. 引用会打开匹配的已保存摘录，无需联系提供方；已打开的结果会持续刷新，直到条目完成，并在条目列表检测到后续重试时刷新。 Citations open the matching saved excerpt without contacting the provider, and an open result refreshes until the item finishes and when the item list observes a later retry. 已关闭的结果对话框不会获取完整详情。 Closed result dialogs do not fetch full details. 批量存储的结果仍可通过限定于所有者范围的 JSONL 导出获取，而内部执行上下文和授权上下文绝不会出现在面向所有者的响应中。 Bulk stored results remain available through the owner-scoped JSONL export, while internal execution and authorization context never enters owner-facing responses. 如果批处理工作进程随后停止或被禁用，包含已持久化批次的线程仍保留只读条目检查和 JSONL 导出功能；执行控件将保持禁用，直到工作进程重新运行。 If the batch worker is later stopped or disabled, threads with persisted batches retain read-only item inspection and JSONL export; execution controls remain disabled until the worker is running again. 有关限制和恢复语义，请参阅 `config.example.yaml` 和[实现约定](docs/plans/2026-08-24-subagent-batch-capacity-implementation.md)。 See `config.example.yaml` and [the implementation contract](docs/plans/2026-08-24-subagent-batch-capacity-implementation.md) for limits and recovery semantics.
在持久化批处理工具可用时，所有者可以在后续轮次中使用 `read_batch_result(batch_id, position=0, offset=0, max_chars=4000)` 显式查看选定的结果。 While durable batch tools are available, a later owner turn can explicitly inspect selected results with `read_batch_result(batch_id, position=0, offset=0, max_chars=4000)`. 该读取工具仅限于当前用户和线程使用，在显式绑定的 SDK 图中也同样如此。 The reader is restricted to the current user and thread, including in explicitly bound SDK graphs. 它返回一个 JSON 文档的单个窗口，其中包含已存储的报告、执行/错误状态以及单独的验收标准/判定结果，且不会启动工作或等待工作完成。 It returns one window of a JSON document containing the stored report, execution/error state and separate acceptance criteria/verdict, without starting or waiting for work. 解析前先拼接各个 `content` 窗口：传入 `next_offset`，并将返回的 `revision` 作为 `expected_revision` 传入，直至 `next_offset` 为 null。 Concatenate `content` windows before parsing: pass `next_offset` and the returned `revision` as `expected_revision` until `next_offset` is null. 条目发生变化时会返回 `restart_required`；应丢弃之前的窗口，并从零开始。 A changed item returns `restart_required`; discard prior windows and start at zero. 对于在 `batch_status.total_items` 范围内选定的下一项，使用 `next_position`。 Use `next_position` for a selected next item within `batch_status.total_items`. 窗口最大为 8,192 个字符；元数据共享该窗口预算，JSON 响应的封装还会增加开销。`result_truncated` 表示存储阶段已对报告实施截断；继续读取无法恢复已丢弃的内容。 The maximum window is 8,192 characters; metadata shares the window budget, and JSON response framing adds overhead. `result_truncated` means storage already capped the report; continuation cannot recover discarded content. 某项执行成功并不意味着任务已通过验收，未经检查的标准仍为 UNVERIFIED。 A succeeded item is not an accepted task, and unchecked criteria remain UNVERIFIED. 不添加自动结果摄取、等待循环或 UI 更改。 No automatic result ingestion, waiting loop or UI change is added.
完整的转义后响应也在 10,000 个字符以内，并且符合所绑定宿主为 `tool_output` 外部化及回退设置的正数限额，因此正常的预算分配能够保留用于继续读取的 JSON。 The complete escaped response also fits within 10,000 characters and the bound host's positive `tool_output` externalization/fallback limits, so normal budgeting preserves continuation JSON. 较小的限额可能缩短窗口；`budget_too_small` 表示操作人员必须提高预算，而不是重试长度为零的页面。 Smaller limits can shorten a window; `budget_too_small` means the operator must raise the budget instead of retrying a zero-length page. 如果限额小到连该错误封装都无法容纳，就无法提供可用的结构化响应。 Limits too small even for that error envelope cannot provide a usable structured response. 显式构建且带有自定义输出预算中间件的 SDK 图应通过 `SubagentRuntime` 提供同一个 `AppConfig`；默认图使用默认的读取器限额。 Explicit SDK graphs with a custom output-budget middleware should supply the same `AppConfig` through `SubagentRuntime`; default graphs use default reader limits.
离线验收：`cd backend && PYTHONPATH=. uv run --locked pytest tests/test_batch_result_reader.py -k 'real_submission_worker or sdk_factory_graph' -q`。 Offline acceptance: `cd backend && PYTHONPATH=. uv run --locked pytest tests/test_batch_result_reader.py -k 'real_submission_worker or sdk_factory_graph' -q`. 这会测试真实提交、工作进程的确定性完成、数据库重新打开以及通过已注册图进行的消费；外部模型执行由伪模型／执行器替代。 This exercises real submission, deterministic worker completion, database reopen and registered graph consumption; external model execution is replaced by a fake model/executor.
持久化的知识研究结果还会导出可为空的 `result_artifact.knowledge_sources` 快照。 Durable knowledge-research results also export nullable `result_artifact.knowledge_sources` snapshots. 每个保留的来源都将已存储报告中的引用 ID 与 `knowledge_search` 或委派的 `task` 捕获的原始摘录配对，并附带其 RAGFlow 数据集／文档／分块定位信息及页码。 Each retained source pairs a citation ID in the stored report with the exact excerpt captured by `knowledge_search` or a delegated `task`, plus its RAGFlow dataset/document/chunk locator and pages. 现有的经过身份验证的 `GET /api/threads/{thread_id}/subagent-batches/{batch_id}/results.jsonl` 仍是入口：在该行的 `sources` 中按 `id` 查找报告的 `#knowledge-` 链接，即可在 Gateway 重启或提供方变更后查看证据。 The existing authenticated `GET /api/threads/{thread_id}/subagent-batches/{batch_id}/results.jsonl` remains the entry point: find a report's `#knowledge-` link in that row's `sources` by `id` to inspect the evidence after a Gateway restart or provider change. 精简的条目查询不包含这些快照；可点击的引用位于选定结果的对话框中，批次预览中不提供。 Compact item queries do not include these snapshots; clickable citations are available in the selected result dialog, not batch previews.
浏览器审阅最多保留 100 个来源，每段摘录最多 16,000 个字符，标识符和名称的长度也受到限制。 Browser review keeps at most 100 sources, with 16,000 characters per excerpt and bounded identifiers/names. 因这些读取限额而被排除的来源会增加所显示的遗漏数量；持久化快照和 JSONL 导出仍保留其原始内容。 Sources excluded by these read limits increase the displayed omission count; the persisted snapshot and JSONL export retain their original contents.
快照保留完整摘录，而不是从提供方新获取的内容。 Snapshots preserve whole excerpts, not freshly fetched provider content. 它们沿用 100 个来源／1,000,000 个文本字符的转发限额；除报告现有的预算外，序列化快照还具有独立的字符预算，其值等于 `subagent_batches.max_result_chars`。`knowledge_sources.omitted_count` 报告因转发、快照投影检查或快照预算而遗漏的被引用记录数量；在候选选择之前被过滤的格式错误或不受支持的生产方产物不计入其中。 They inherit the 100-source/1,000,000-text-character forwarding limits; the serialized snapshot has a separate character budget equal to `subagent_batches.max_result_chars`, in addition to the report's existing budget. `knowledge_sources.omitted_count` reports referenced records omitted by forwarding, snapshot projection checks or snapshot budgeting; malformed or unsupported producer artifacts filtered before candidate selection are not counted. 报告截断会将选择范围限制为已存储文本中的完整引用。 Report truncation limits selection to complete citations in the stored text. 旧结果／纯文本结果没有快照（`null`）；不会重建历史证据。 Old/plain results have no snapshot (`null`); no historical evidence is reconstructed. Gateway 启动时通过迁移 `0033_batch_result_artifact` 添加一个可为空的 JSON 列。 Gateway startup adds a nullable JSON column through migration `0033_batch_result_artifact`. 迁移前请备份数据库；降级会删除证据快照，同时保留报告文本。 Back up the database before migrating; downgrading drops evidence snapshots while preserving report text. 已取消、失败和过期的尝试不会发布证据，显式重试会清除先前的快照。 Cancelled, failed and stale attempts do not publish evidence, and explicit retries clear previous snapshots.
显式启用的嵌套 `task` 委派会在其捕获的产物中传递被遗漏的不透明来源 ID。 Explicitly enabled nested `task` delegation propagates omitted opaque source IDs in its captured artifact. 每份外层报告都会筛选这些 ID 并去重，而独立捕获的证据可以恢复被遗漏的来源。 Each outer report filters and deduplicates those IDs, and independently captured evidence can restore an omitted source. 持久化快照仅发布最终计数。 The durable snapshot publishes only the final count. 缺少遗漏 ID 的旧版任务产物无法重建先前在转发过程中丢失的内容；正文中无法识别的引用不会被视为已捕获的证据。 Legacy task artifacts without omission IDs cannot reconstruct earlier forwarding losses; unknown prose citations are not treated as captured evidence.
如果某项在耗尽重试预算后，其最后一次租约到期，则在所有条目均进入终止状态后，批次也进入终止状态：没有任何条目成功时为 `failed`，至少有一项成功时为 `completed`。 If an item's final lease expires after exhausting the retry budget, the batch reaches a terminal state once all items are terminal: `failed` when none succeeded, or `completed` when at least one succeeded. 在这两种情况下，该项均保持 `failed` 状态，以便部分结果仍然可见。 The item remains `failed` in both cases so partial results stay visible.
已暂停的批次也会在工作进程退出后协调处理已过期的尝试。 Paused batches also reconcile expired attempts after a worker exits. 可重试的条目会保持排队状态，在您显式恢复批次之前不会启动新工作；重试次数已耗尽的尝试会被标记为失败，并可单独重试。 Retryable items remain queued without starting new work until you explicitly resume the batch; exhausted attempts become failed and can be retried individually.
直接集成 `create_deerflow_agent(...)` 的应用可以显式管理同一边界，而不依赖 Gateway 启动。 Direct `create_deerflow_agent(...)` integrations can own the same boundary explicitly instead of relying on Gateway startup. 构建一个 `SubagentRuntime`，并让该应用中的所有图共享它；这样，其 `max_running`、常规的每次运行总量、绑定的 `task` 工具以及可选的持久化批次工具就会使用同一个由调用方持有的快照和执行控制器。 Construct one `SubagentRuntime` and share it across every graph in that application; its `max_running`, ordinary per-run total, bound `task` tool, and optional durable-batch tools then use the same caller-owned snapshot and execution controller. 带有批次存储库的运行时拥有一个工作进程，必须在构建图之前启动，并在应用关闭期间停止： A runtime with a batch repository owns a worker and must be started before graph construction and stopped during application shutdown: 
from deerflow.agents import RuntimeFeatures, create_deerflow_agent
from deerflow.subagents import SubagentRuntime

runtime = SubagentRuntime.from_app_config(app_config, batch_repository=batch_repository)
async with runtime:
    graph = create_deerflow_agent(
        model,
        features=RuntimeFeatures(subagent=True),
        subagent_runtime=runtime,
    )
    # Serve or invoke graph while the durable worker is running.
`SubagentRuntime.stop()` 会等待其拥有的批次服务完成，然后才传播调用方的取消请求，包括重复的取消请求。 `SubagentRuntime.stop()` waits for its owned batch service to finish before propagating caller cancellation, including repeated cancellation. 这一排空过程没有超时限制；存储库操作和子级清理必须能够终止。 This drain has no timeout; repository operations and child cleanup must terminate. 如果服务关闭也失败或被取消，则保留调用方的首次取消，并将服务故障作为其原因。 If service shutdown also fails or is cancelled, the first caller cancellation is preserved with the service failure as its cause.
Gateway 运行的收尾过程在调用方取消时也会保留所有权：它会先完成终态持久化、流结束和资源清理，再传播中断。 Gateway run finalization also retains ownership through caller cancellation: it finishes terminal persistence, stream completion, and resource cleanup before propagating the interruption. 后端 I/O 必须终止，或自行实施超时限制。 Backend I/O must terminate or enforce its own timeout. Gateway 关闭时，也会等待那些已提交终态但仍在收尾的工作进程；关闭排空的时间预算耗尽时会记录日志，但不会取消它们尚未完成的写入。 Gateway shutdown includes workers still finalizing after their terminal status commits; an expired shutdown drain budget is logged and does not cancel their outstanding writes.
在 AIO 和 POSIX LocalSandbox 上，被取消、被替换、失败或被强制停止的执行会在释放沙箱租约之前停止其所属的 bash 进程，包括普通的 `nohup` 子进程。 Cancelled, replaced, failed or hard-stopped executions stop their owned bash processes before releasing the sandbox lease on AIO and POSIX LocalSandbox, including ordinary `nohup` children. 此前成功轮次启动的后台服务会继续运行。 Background services from successful earlier turns remain running. 清理过程会跟踪继承的执行标记和进程组；对于刻意逃逸此所有权范围的脚本，它并不构成安全边界。 Cleanup tracks inherited execution markers and process groups; it is not a security boundary against scripts deliberately escaping that ownership. Windows LocalSandbox 和第三方提供程序保留其现有行为。 Windows LocalSandbox and third-party providers retain their existing behavior.
即使诊断日志保留了检查点检查的回溯信息，终态清理也会释放运行日志及其消息序列回调。 Terminal cleanup releases the run journal and its message-sequence callback even when diagnostic logs retain a checkpoint-inspection traceback.
该工厂仍然不会加载 YAML 或创建 SQL 基础设施：配置快照、存储库和生命周期均由调用方提供。 The factory still does not load YAML or create SQL infrastructure: the caller supplies the config snapshot, repository, and lifecycle. 由于它接受由调用方管理的 `system_prompt`，直接集成方也负责模型可见的、关于这些限制的任何表述；无论如何，默认中间件都会强制执行运行时限制。 Because it accepts a caller-owned `system_prompt`, direct integrations also own any model-visible wording about those limits; the default middleware enforces the runtime limits regardless. 该工厂不会挂载 Gateway 按所有者限定作用域的 HTTP 路由或 Web UI，因此直接使用它的应用如果需要这些界面，就必须自行提供结果 API/UI。 The factory does not mount the Gateway owner-scoped HTTP routes or Web UI, so direct applications must expose their own result API/UI if they need those surfaces. 如果仅用于常规委派，`SubagentRuntime(...)` 无需异步启动。 For ordinary delegation only, `SubagentRuntime(...)` needs no asynchronous startup.
管理员可以在 **设置 → 子智能体** 中添加、编辑、禁用和删除可复用的工作智能体定义。 Administrators can add, edit, disable, and delete reusable worker definitions from **Settings → Subagents**. 内置定义和 `config.yaml` 中的定义仍会作为只读条目显示在此处。 Built-in and `config.yaml` definitions remain visible there as read-only entries. 默认的主智能体可以使用所有已启用的运行时子智能体；而通过页面创建的每个自定义智能体则可以允许使用全部、不允许使用任何子智能体，或仅允许使用选定的子智能体。 The default Lead Agent can use every enabled runtime sub-agent; each page-created Custom Agent can instead allow all, none, or a selected set. 这一选择会同时在模型可见的目录和服务端的 `task` 工具中得到强制执行。 That selection is enforced both in the model-visible directory and by the server-side `task` tool. 在此版本中，托管定义在整个部署范围内生效，并遵循 `agent_storage.backend`：本地部署使用原子文件，多实例部署使用共享应用数据库。 Managed definitions are deployment-wide in this version and follow `agent_storage.backend`: atomic files for a local deployment or the shared application database for multiple instances.
原生工作智能体支持通过 YAML 配置和托管子智能体 API，为每个子智能体设置 `thinking_enabled` 和 `reasoning_effort`。 Native workers support per-subagent `thinking_enabled` and `reasoning_effort` through YAML configuration and the managed-subagent API. 未配置的工作智能体沿用现有的关闭思考的请求；所选模型的强制思考策略、支持的推理力度值及其别名仍然适用。 Unconfigured workers keep the existing thinking-off request; the selected model's required-thinking policy, supported effort values and aliases still apply. 这些设置不会继承主智能体的思考偏好。 These settings do not inherit the lead agent's thinking preferences.
例如，当节省的实际耗时超过重复探索和综合整理的成本时，独立的只读研究可以并发进行；而涉及共享文件并依赖顺序测试反馈的存储库重构则仍由主智能体负责。 For example, independent read-only research can run concurrently when the wall-clock savings outweigh duplicated discovery and synthesis cost, while a repository refactor with shared files and sequential test feedback remains with the lead agent. 当 `max_concurrent_subagents` 为 `1` 时，并行和多批次路由指导会被禁用；只有在能够带来显著的专业能力收益或上下文隔离收益时，才仍可使用委派。 When `max_concurrent_subagents` is `1`, parallel and multi-batch routing guidance is disabled; delegation remains available only for material specialist or context-isolation benefit.

现有的释放同步机制会保存工作区和输出文件，但不会同步文件 The existing release sync saves workspace/output files but does not mirror file
删除操作，因此，远程已删除文件的已保存副本可能会在重新创建时再次出现。 deletions, so saved copies of remotely deleted files can reappear on recreation.
每轮 E2B 挂载上传最多接受 512 MiB 和 2,000 个文件。 Each E2B mount upload pass accepts at most 512 MiB and 2,000 files. 该轮上传 The pass

或 E2B SDK 调用。 or E2B SDK calls.
E2B VM 会一直占用其槽位，直到 E2B 确认销毁。 An E2B VM keeps its slot until E2B confirms destruction. 此规则涵盖 This rule covers
创建和回收操作。 create and reclaim operations. 发现机制可以找到来自另一个 Gateway 的 VM。 Discovery can find a VM from another Gateway.
关闭时会关闭不具有所有权的发现客户端，而不销毁其 VM。 Shutdown closes an unowned discovery client without destroying its VM.
释放时，当 VM 进入预热池后，便不再将其计入转换中的数量。 Release stops counting a transition when the VM enters the warm pool.
关闭过程中的竞态会在终止操作发生暂时性故障后重试远程清理。 Shutdown races retry remote cleanup after a transient kill failure.
重置会销毁所跟踪的活跃 E2B VM 和预热 E2B VM。 Reset destroys tracked active and warm E2B VMs. 旧的提供程序实例 The old provider instance
无法接受新的获取请求。 cannot accept new acquisitions.
DeerFlow 不只是*说说*要做什么。 DeerFlow doesn't just *talk* about doing things. 它有自己的计算机。 It has its own computer.
每个任务都有自己的执行环境，并拥有完整的文件系统视图——包括技能、工作区、上传内容和输出内容。 Each task gets its own execution environment with a full filesystem view — skills, workspace, uploads, outputs. 智能体可以读取、写入和编辑文件。 The agent reads, writes, and edits files. 它可以查看图像，并在安全配置下执行 shell 命令。 It can view images and, when configured safely, execute shell commands.
先读后写门控机制将每个读取标记与对应的 `read_file` 调用结果绑定，其中也包括返回多消息 `Command` 更新的自定义工具。 The read-before-write gate ties each read mark to that `read_file` call's result, including custom tools returning multi-message `Command` updates. 无关的结果既不能在读取失败后授权写入，也不能掩盖成功的读取。 An unrelated result cannot authorize a write after a failed read or hide a successful read.
当结果分析达到 JSON 解析器的深度限制时，工具（包括 `read_file`）返回的深度嵌套 JSON 会保留其原始内容。 Deeply nested JSON returned by tools, including `read_file`, keeps its original content when result analysis reaches the JSON parser's depth limit. 当嵌套深度导致无法分析时，明确的工具失败仍被视为错误，其类别为未知。 Explicit tool failures remain errors with an unknown category when depth prevents analysis.
嵌套深度超过 JSON 解析器递归限制的工具调用参数和澄清选项会被视为不可用的不透明值，而不会导致本次运行中止。 Tool-call arguments and clarification options nested past the JSON parser's recursion limit are treated as unusable opaque values instead of aborting the run.
在 Linux 和 macOS 上，本地沙箱的 `write_file` 替换操作和 `str_replace` 会先暂存完整文本，再替换目标文件，因此写入、刷新或发布错误都会保留原文件。 On Linux and macOS, local sandbox `write_file` replacements and `str_replace` stage complete text before replacing the destination, so write, flush or publication errors retain the previous file. 系统会检查现有的写入权限；在写入暂存内容之前，会转移所有权、权限位、ACL 和扩展属性。 Existing write access is checked; ownership, permission bits, ACLs and extended attributes are transferred before writing staging content. 元数据转移失败会中止保存。 Failure to transfer metadata aborts the save. 成功的修订会获得新的修改时间，即使保存的内容为空也如此；源文件描述符清理错误仅作为警告，不会改变保存结果。 Successful revisions get a fresh modification time, including empty saves; source-descriptor cleanup errors are warnings and do not change the save result. 具有 set-id 位、macOS 文件标志或与内容绑定的 Linux 特权属性的文件会被拒绝处理，并保持不变。 Files with set-id bits, macOS file flags or content-bound Linux privilege attributes are refused unchanged. 替换操作需要父目录的写入权限，并会发布一个新的 inode；其他硬链接仍保留先前的版本。 Replacement requires parent-directory write access and publishes a new inode; other hard links retain the previous version. 符号链接遵循现有的路径验证规则。 Symlinks follow the existing path-validation rules. 追加写入、其他主机平台（包括 Windows）、shell 写入和远程提供程序均保持现有行为。 Append writes, other host platforms (including Windows), shell writes and remote providers keep their existing behavior. 这并不保证并发写入或断电后的恢复。 This is not a concurrent-writer or power-loss recovery guarantee.
循环警告允许智能体在给出最终答复之前保存已收集的结果。 Loop warnings let agents save collected results before their final answer. 如果保存被先读后写门控机制阻止，智能体可以读取目标文件，然后重试保存一次。 If the save is blocked by the read-before-write gate, the agent may read the target file and retry the save once. 陷入循环或已停止的工具必须由可用的替代工具取代；如果不存在获准的保存路径，或者重试失败，最终答复应包含已收集的结果，并说明文件未保存。 A looping or stopped tool must be replaced by an available alternative; if no permitted save path exists or the retry fails, the final answer includes the collected results and reports that the file was not saved. 当 `present_files` 可用时，警告还会要求智能体在答复之前展示其已在 `/mnt/user-data/outputs` 中创建的文件，以确保交付已完成的输出。 When `present_files` is available, the warning also asks the agent to present the files it already created in `/mnt/user-data/outputs` before answering, so finished outputs are delivered. 恢复调用仍计入循环次数限制，硬性停止会阻止进一步执行工具。 Recovery calls still count toward the loop limits, and a hard stop prevents further tool execution.
限定行范围的 `read_file` 调用在所有沙箱提供商上都采用相同的行计数方式：只有换行符才标志着一行结束。 Ranged `read_file` calls count lines the same way on every sandbox provider: a line ends only at a newline. 因此，在 E2B、BoxLite、Tenki 和 OpenSandbox 上，包含单独回车符（例如已保存的进度日志）、换页符或 Unicode 行分隔符的文件，返回的行与本地沙箱相同，而截断读取所建议的 `start_line` 指向下一个尚未读取的行。 On E2B, BoxLite, Tenki, and OpenSandbox, a file with bare carriage returns (such as a saved progress log), form feeds, or Unicode line separators therefore returns the same lines as on the local sandbox, and the `start_line` a truncated read suggests points at the next unread line.
对同一文件的并发读写在同步和异步工具调用之间共用一道互斥门控。 Concurrent reads and writes to the same file share a gate across synchronous and asynchronous tool calls. 等待该门控的异步调用方不会占用完成当前读写操作所需的工作线程。 Async callers waiting for that gate do not occupy worker threads needed to finish the current read or write. 取消正在等待的调用不会中断当前操作；已经开始检查文件的调用仍会等待该工作完成，然后才释放其门控。 Cancelling a waiting call leaves the current operation running; a call that already started file inspection still waits for that work to finish before releasing its gate.
内置的 `grep` 工具既可搜索单个文本文件，也可搜索某个目录下所有匹配的文本文件，因此智能体可以直接搜索已上传的文档，而不必先将搜索请求扩大到整个上传目录。 The built-in `grep` tool searches either one text file or all matching text files below a directory, so an agent can search an uploaded document directly without first broadening the request to the entire uploads directory.
在本地沙箱中，`grep(literal=True)` 还可在现有文件大小限制内搜索压缩文本或单行 JSON 中的长行。 On the local sandbox, `grep(literal=True)` also searches long lines in minified text or single-line JSON, within the existing file-size limit. 不区分大小写的字面匹配在保留 Unicode 大小写处理行为的同时，避免对重复前缀进行重新扫描。 Case-insensitive literal matching avoids repeated-prefix rescans while preserving Unicode case behavior. 正则表达式搜索保留了针对长行的安全防护。 Regex searches retain their long-line safety guard.
在所有沙箱提供商上，返回的预览都仅显示每个匹配行的开头部分（默认为 200 个字符），因此长行中靠后位置的匹配内容可能不会出现在预览中。 Across sandbox providers, returned previews show only the beginning of each matched line (200 characters by default), so a match later in a long line may not appear in the preview. 使用所报告的路径和行号查看源内容。 Use the reported path and line number to inspect the source. `truncated` 标志涉及匹配数量限制，而非预览长度。 The `truncated` flag concerns match-count limits, not preview length.
E2B 的 `glob` 过滤器会保留文件名模式中的空格、引号和美元符号，同时保持通配符匹配和相对于根目录的目录范围限定方式不变。 E2B's `glob` filter preserves spaces, quotes, and dollar signs in filename patterns, while wildcard matching and root-relative directory scoping remain unchanged.
E2B 的 `grep` 在报告匹配路径和行号时，也会保留文件名和目录名中的冒号。 E2B `grep` also preserves colons in file and directory names when reporting matching paths and line numbers.
在 E2B、BoxLite、Tenki 和 OpenSandbox 上，`glob` 和 `grep` 会保留返回路径中的非 LF 分隔字符，例如垂直制表符和 Unicode 行分隔符；`grep` 还会保留匹配文本中的这些字符。 `glob` and `grep` preserve non-LF separator characters, such as vertical tabs and Unicode line separators, inside returned paths on E2B, BoxLite, Tenki, and OpenSandbox; `grep` also preserves them inside matched text.
远程 `ls` 会先排除被忽略的后代条目，再应用其 500 个条目的列表限制，因此依赖项和构建目录树不会挤占可见文件的名额。 Remote `ls` excludes ignored descendants before applying its 500-entry listing limit, so dependency and build trees do not crowd out visible files. 显式列出被忽略的目录时，仍会列出其内容；常规深度限制和输出限制依然有效。 Explicitly listing an ignored directory still lists its contents; normal depth and output limits remain in effect.
AIO 目录列表操作会丢弃已不存在的 shell 会话，以便下一个请求能够恢复。 AIO directory listings discard missing shell sessions so the next request can recover.
连接断开后，目录列表操作和持久 shell 命令会报告一个 After a dropped connection, directory listings and persistent shell commands report an
未知的结果，而不会重新执行该操作；后续调用会使用新会话。 unknown outcome without replaying the operation; later calls use a fresh session.
当前上传内容的上下文能够容忍格式错误的附件元数据：无效的文件名 Current-upload context tolerates malformed attachment metadata: invalid filename
引用以及无法检查的文件会被逐一跳过，而有效的 references and files that cannot be checked are skipped individually, while valid
同一消息中的附件仍然可用。 attachments in the same message remain available. 不可用或超出显示范围的 Unusable or display-overflowing
文件大小会沿用现有的零大小显示回退方式，而不会丢弃该文件。 file sizes use the existing zero-size display fallback without dropping the file.
探测错误会以调试级别记录到日志中。 Probe errors are logged at debug level. 对于能够安全呈现的、大小为 1 TB 或以上的文件 Sizes of 1 TB or more that can be rendered
其大小会安全地显示为 `>= 1 TB`，以保持提示词简洁；存储的字节数保持 safely display as `>= 1 TB` to keep the prompt compact; stored byte counts remain
不变。 unchanged.
启用 `uploads.auto_convert_documents: true` 后，文档解析会 When `uploads.auto_convert_documents: true` is enabled, document parsing runs
对所有大小的文件都在 Gateway 事件循环之外运行，包括小型压缩 outside the Gateway event loop for every file size, including small compressed
Office 文件。 Office files. 取消上传时，会等待其转换工作线程完成，然后才 Cancelling an upload waits for its conversion worker before

已上传 Markdown 文档的大纲会识别 ATX 标题语法，通过线性后缀扫描清理结尾标记，并跳过 HTML 注释块以及围栏式和缩进式代码示例，从而使话题标签和代码注释不会 Uploaded Markdown outlines recognize ATX heading syntax, clean closing markers with a linear suffix scan, and skip HTML comment blocks and fenced and indented code examples, so hashtags and code comments do not
在智能体的标题预览中挤占真正的文档章节。 crowd out real document sections from the agent's heading preview. 缩进的粗体示例也会被排除；仍支持最多带有三个前导空格的 PDF 风格粗体标题。 Indented bold examples are also excluded; PDF-style bold headings with up to three leading spaces remain supported.
会清理包裹整个标题的粗体标记以及相邻的 PDF 粗体片段；独立的粗体 Whole-title bold wrappers and adjacent PDF bold spans are cleaned; separate bold
由普通文本连接的片段会保留其标记，而不是仅丢失最外层的一对标记。 spans joined by ordinary text retain their markers instead of losing only the outer pair.
加粗被拆分的数值表格行，包括带括号的年份、带正负号的数值以及 Split-bold numeric table rows, including parenthesized years, signed values, and

是数值列，就会被排除，因此不会占用大纲的标题配额。 is a numeric column, so they do not consume the outline's heading budget.

个字符，并附有截断标记。 characters per file, with truncation markers. 上传的完整文件仍然可用 Full uploaded files remain available

在 Windows 上，`st_ctime_ns` 可能表示创建时间；对任一 On Windows, `st_ctime_ns` may represent creation time; a same-size rewrite of either

保守的元数据验证，并不保证内容相同或具备防篡改能力。 conservative metadata validation, not a guarantee of content equality or tamper resistance.
沙箱图像下载在读取过程中强制执行 20 MiB 的视觉输入大小限制，包括为模型上下文重新读取图像时。 Sandbox image downloads enforce the 20 MiB vision limit while reading, including model-context rereads. 超大数据流会在遇到第一个导致超限的数据块时停止。 Oversized streams stop at the first over-limit chunk. 自定义沙箱提供方必须接受 `download_file(..., max_bytes=...)` 关键字参数并执行其限制；较旧的实现会导致图像读取失败，而不会回退到不受限制的下载。 Custom sandbox providers must accept and enforce the `download_file(..., max_bytes=...)` keyword; older implementations fail the image read instead of falling back to an unrestricted download.
为视觉模型调用加载的图像字节是临时的：模型使用这些数据后，DeerFlow 会移除隐藏的 base64 消息，避免后续检查点不断重复保存该载荷。 Image bytes loaded for a vision-model call are transient: DeerFlow removes the hidden base64 message after the model consumes it so later checkpoints do not keep duplicating that payload.
每次运行结束后，DeerFlow 都会为该次运行所属的 `workspace` 和 `outputs` 目录记录工作区变更摘要。 After each run, DeerFlow records a workspace change summary for the run-owned `workspace` and `outputs` directories. Web UI 会在助手轮次上显示一个紧凑的“文件已更改”徽标；打开后可查看新建、修改和删除的文件，并在可以安全显示时展示文本差异。 The Web UI shows a compact "files changed" badge on the assistant turn; opening it reveals created, modified, and deleted files with text diffs when safe to display. 上传文件会被排除，因为它们是用户输入，而非智能体生成的变更；DeerFlow 所属的 `.mcp/` 命名空间下的 stdio MCP 临时文件和调试文件也会被排除，因为它们属于进程内部状态（与 `.git/` 和 `node_modules/` 一样，任何层级中名为 `.mcp` 的目录都会被排除）。 Uploads are excluded because they are user inputs, not agent-generated changes, and stdio MCP temporary/debug files under the DeerFlow-owned `.mcp/` namespace are excluded because they are process-internal state (like `.git/` and `node_modules/`, any directory named `.mcp` is excluded at any depth). 对于大型文件、二进制文件或看起来包含敏感信息的文件，仅显示元数据。 Large, binary, or sensitive-looking files are shown as metadata only.
如果某个文件在计算哈希值时消失，扫描会跳过该文件并继续 If a file disappears while being hashed, the scan omits that file and continues
记录其他文件。 recording the others. 因此，并发删除临时文件不会 A concurrent scratch-file removal therefore does not
导致整个快照被丢弃，也不会妨碍检测其他生成的输出。 discard the whole snapshot or suppress detection of other produced outputs.
这仍然是尽力而为的扫描，而非原子性的文件系统快照。 This remains a best-effort scan, not an atomic filesystem snapshot.
通过 `present_files` 展示的文件仍属于该会话线程的产物状态，Web UI 会在页面刷新后恢复产物面板和所选文档。 Files presented through `present_files` remain part of the thread's artifact state, and the Web UI restores the artifact panel and selected document after a page refresh. 较长的产物列表可在桌面端面板和移动端抽屉中滚动，确保每个文件都可访问。 Long artifact lists scroll within the panel on desktop and in the mobile drawer, keeping every file reachable. 当一条已完成的回复成功展示 2 至 50 个文件时，其最后一张文件卡片还会提供一次 ZIP 下载。 When a completed response successfully presents between 2 and 50 files, its final file card also offers one ZIP download. 归档所包含的文件由终端交付回执确定，而非由浏览器提供的路径确定；ZIP 包含文件的当前版本，这些版本可能自回复生成后已发生变化。 Archive membership comes from the terminal delivery receipt rather than browser-supplied paths, and the ZIP contains the current file versions, which may have changed since the response. 运行结束时，当前选中的正式产物会刷新一次，使编辑结果无需手动重新加载即可显示。 The currently selected formal artifact is refreshed once when the run finishes so edits become visible without a manual reload. 在线程空闲时，也可以在 Unix 和 Windows 上通过面板编辑 `/mnt/user-data/outputs` 下现有的 UTF-8 文本产物并显式保存；保存时使用内容修订版本，以防覆盖智能体所做的更改。 Existing UTF-8 text artifacts under `/mnt/user-data/outputs` can also be edited and explicitly saved from the panel on Unix and Windows while the thread is idle; saves use content revisions to prevent overwriting agent changes. 延迟完成的后台读取无法替换编辑器中已成功保存的修订版本；取消预览读取和完整文件读取时，也会中止相应的 HTTP 请求。 A delayed background read cannot replace a successfully saved revision in the editor; cancelled preview and full-file reads also abort their HTTP requests. 源代码预览还会根据文件名识别没有扩展名的 `Dockerfile` 和 `Makefile` 产物。 Source previews also recognize extensionless `Dockerfile` and `Makefile` artifacts by their file names. 未知文件类型（包括名为 `constructor` 和 `__proto__` 等的文件）仍以下载作为回退方式。 Unknown file types, including names such as `constructor` and `__proto__`, retain the download fallback.
未保存的产物编辑内容会在切换聊天、工作区页面或访问 Unsaved artifact edits survive switching chats, workspace pages, or visiting the
首页并在同一浏览器标签页中返回后保留。 home page and returning in the same browser tab. 草稿会保留在内存中，归属于 Drafts stay in memory for the
当前账户，直到保存或丢弃；切换账户或退出登录会清除草稿。 current account until saved or discarded; changing accounts or signing out clears them.

当输出文件被原子替换时，产物的内容修订版本会更新， Artifact content revisions refresh when an output file is atomically replaced,

对于超过 2 MiB 编辑限制的常规文件，使用文件标识和变更元数据 Regular files over the 2 MiB editing limit use file identity and change metadata
进行范围请求验证，而无需计算整个文件的哈希值。 for range validators without hashing the whole file. 对于条件字节范围请求， Conditional byte ranges for
常规文件要求 ETag 匹配；采用日期形式的 `If-Range` 请求会收到当前完整文件。 regular files require a matching ETag; date-form `If-Range` requests receive the full current file.
保存时还会将对现有文件的读取限制为 2 MiB 加上一个检测字节，因此， Saving also bounds the existing-file read to 2 MiB plus one detection byte, so a
在大小检查后增长或被替换的文件会被拒绝，而无需将 file that grows or is replaced after the size check is rejected without loading
整个超大文件加载到内存中。 the entire oversized file into memory.

如果采样恰好将 CRLF 行结束符截为两半，预览会保留此前的完整行，并省略不完整的最后一条记录，即使其最后一个字段带有引号也是如此。 If the sample cuts a CRLF line ending in half, the preview keeps the earlier complete rows and omits the incomplete final record, including when its last field is quoted.

`report%20final.md` 和 `report final.md` 仍是不同的文件。 `report%20final.md` and `report final.md` remain distinct files. Markdown 链接 Markdown links
继续接受经过 URL 编码的路径。 continue to accept URL-encoded paths.
`write_file` 使用指引反映当前模型的输出 token 限制，包括自定义智能体和思考模式的覆盖设置。 The `write_file` guidance reflects the active model's output token limit, including custom-agent and thinking-mode overrides. 对于较长的文档，会引导智能体使用 `append=True` 分节写入；对于限制未知的模型，不提供数值形式的预算提示。 For longer documents, the agent is guided to write sections with `append=True`; models without a known limit receive no numeric budget hint.
使用 `AioSandboxProvider` 时，shell 命令在隔离的容器内执行。 With `AioSandboxProvider`, shell execution runs inside isolated containers. 使用 `LocalSandboxProvider` 时，文件工具仍映射到宿主机上各线程专属的目录，但宿主机 `bash` 默认禁用，因为它无法构成安全的隔离边界。 With `LocalSandboxProvider`, file tools still map to per-thread directories on the host, but host `bash` is disabled by default because it is not a secure isolation boundary. 仅对完全可信的本地工作流重新启用宿主机 bash。 Re-enable host bash only for fully trusted local workflows. 宿主机 bash 命令具有按实际经过时间计算的超时限制，长期运行的进程应在后台启动，并将输出重定向至工作区日志。 Host bash commands have a wall-clock timeout, and long-lived processes should be started in the background with output redirected to a workspace log. 如果本地命令在创建子进程后启动失败，DeerFlow 会尝试终止并回收该子进程，然后再向上传递启动错误。 If local command startup fails after spawning a child, DeerFlow attempts to kill and reap that child before propagating the startup error. 在 Windows 上，Git Bash/MSYS 参数转换的排除范围仅限于安全的非根虚拟路径前缀，因此宿主机原生 CLI 启动器仍保持正常的 MSYS 兼容性。 On Windows, Git Bash/MSYS argument-conversion exclusions are limited to safe non-root virtual path prefixes, so host-native CLI launchers retain their normal MSYS compatibility. 当本地沙箱回退到 PowerShell 时，会以 UTF-8 捕获输出，使中日韩文本不依赖 Gateway 宿主机的区域设置。 When the local sandbox falls back to PowerShell, it captures output as UTF-8 so CJK text does not depend on the Gateway host locale.

智能体式浏览器控制Agentic Browser Control
浏览器截图和 Browserless 捕获结果会以原子方式发布完整内容。 Browser screenshots and Browserless captures publish complete content atomically.
新文件使用主机的常规创建权限（通常为 `0644`），而 New files use the host's normal creation permissions (typically `0644`), and
替换文件时会保留现有权限，因此沙箱代码可以读取捕获结果， replacements preserve existing permissions, so sandbox code can read captures
前提是这些权限允许读取。 when those permissions allow it.
如果保存失败，即使临时文件的 If saving fails, the original error is preserved even when temporary-file
清理也失败，原始错误仍会保留；先前的输出保持完好。 cleanup also fails; the previous output remains intact.
清理失败会连同临时文件路径一起记录到日志中，以便排查问题。 Cleanup failures are logged with the temporary-file path for troubleshooting.
日志处理器的错误不能取代保存错误，也不能导致跳过其余清理操作。 Logging-handler errors cannot replace the save error or skip remaining cleanup.
Browserless 使用空占位文件预留捕获文件名，以防止并发 Browserless reserves capture names with empty placeholders to prevent concurrent
捕获操作相互覆盖。 captures from overwriting one another. 发布失败会释放占位文件； Failed publication releases the placeholder;

发布错误或中断。 publication error or interruption.

则改用下一个可用名称，例如 `report-1.png`。 moves to the next free one, such as `report-1.png`.

顺序出现，并支持带缩进或无缩进的 YAML 列表。 order within a tool entry, with either indented or indentless YAML lists.
读取页面并不等同于*使用*页面。 Reading a page is not the same as *using* one. 除了只读的 `web_fetch` 和 `web_capture` 工具外，DeerFlow 还提供一个可选的智能体浏览器工具组，为每段对话维持一个实时浏览器会话，使智能体能够实际操作页面——导航、读取交互元素、点击、输入、提交表单，以及在大量使用 JavaScript 的网站上完成多步骤流程。 Alongside the read-only `web_fetch` and `web_capture` tools, DeerFlow ships an optional agentic browser tool group that keeps a live, per-conversation browser session so the agent can actually operate a page — navigate, read the interactive elements, click, type, submit forms, and follow multi-step flows on JavaScript-heavy sites.
每次操作都会返回页面交互元素的最新快照，每个元素均通过一个稳定的 `[ref]` 编号定位，因此智能体会根据刚刚观察到的内容执行操作，而不是猜测选择器。 Each action returns a fresh snapshot of the page's interactive elements, each addressed by a stable `[ref]` number, so the agent acts on what it just observed instead of guessing selectors. 默认情况下，出站 URL 会接受 SSRF 筛查，浏览器的 TCP 连接则通过本地代理建立，该代理将每个连接固定到经过筛查的地址，因此，即使 DNS 响应在检查后发生变化，也无法将这些连接重定向到私有主机（不涵盖 WebRTC UDP）。 Outbound URLs are SSRF-screened by default, and the browser's TCP connections go through a local proxy that pins each one to the screened addresses, so a DNS answer that changes after the check cannot redirect them to a private host (WebRTC UDP is not covered). 它由 Playwright 驱动，并作为可选附加依赖提供，以保持核心安装精简： It is powered by Playwright and shipped as an optional extra so the core install stays lean: 
cd backend
uv sync --extra browser
uv run playwright install chromium
然后取消 `config.yaml` 中 `group: browser` 工具条目的注释（`browser_navigate`、`browser_snapshot`、`browser_click`、`browser_type`、`browser_get_text`、`browser_back`、`browser_screenshot`、`browser_close`）。`make dev` / Docker 启动时会检测已启用的 `browser_navigate` 工具，并在同步依赖时保留 `browser` 附加依赖。 Then uncomment the `group: browser` tool entries in `config.yaml` (`browser_navigate`, `browser_snapshot`, `browser_click`, `browser_type`, `browser_get_text`, `browser_back`, `browser_screenshot`, `browser_close`). `make dev` / Docker startup detects an enabled `browser_navigate` tool and preserves the `browser` extra on dependency syncs. 如果已配置浏览器控制但缺少 Playwright，Gateway 将无法启动；除非后端确实能够提供浏览器功能，否则 `/api/features` 会隐藏 Browser UI。 The Gateway fails startup if browser control is configured but Playwright is missing, and `/api/features` hides the Browser UI unless the backend can actually serve it. 除本地可信调试外，其他场景均应保持 `headless: true` 和 `allow_private_addresses: false`。 Keep `headless: true` and `allow_private_addresses: false` for anything but local, trusted debugging. 通过 `cdp_url` 连接到现有 Chrome 时，无法强制实施 DeerFlow 的子资源/重定向 SSRF 防护，因此默认拒绝连接，除非通过 `allow_unguarded_cdp: true` 明确确认接受该风险；仅应将其用于可信的本地浏览器。 Attaching to an existing Chrome with `cdp_url` cannot enforce DeerFlow's subresource/redirect SSRF guard and therefore fails closed unless `allow_unguarded_cdp: true` explicitly acknowledges that risk; use it only with a trusted local browser. 浏览器会话仅存在于当前进程内；启用此工具组时，应将 Gateway 保持为单个工作进程，因为常规的 uvicorn 工作进程分发机制不提供线程亲和性。 Browser sessions are process-local; keep the Gateway to a single worker process while this tool group is enabled because ordinary uvicorn worker dispatch does not provide thread affinity. 这意味着需要设置 `GATEWAY_WORKERS=1`；对于完全未向 uvicorn 传递工作进程数量的启动方式（`backend/Dockerfile`、`scripts/serve.sh`），还需将 `WEB_CONCURRENCY` 保持未设置或设为 `1`，因为 uvicorn 会从该变量获取进程数量。 That means `GATEWAY_WORKERS=1`, and on the launches that pass uvicorn no worker count at all (`backend/Dockerfile`, `scripts/serve.sh`) also `WEB_CONCURRENCY` unset or `1`, since uvicorn takes the process count from it.
当浏览器控制可用，且智能体未限制 `tool_groups` 或其中包含 `browser` 组时，现有的非模拟 Custom Agent 聊天也会显示相同的 Browser Live 控件。 Existing, non-mock Custom Agent chats expose the same Browser Live controls when browser control is available and the agent either leaves `tool_groups` unrestricted or includes the `browser` group. 如果显式设置的工具组允许列表不包含 `browser`，这些控件将保持隐藏。 An explicit tool-group allowlist without `browser` keeps those controls hidden.
关闭登录弹出窗口后，浏览器控制权会返回同一会话中仍然打开的标签页，同时保留 Cookie 和站点存储。 Closing a login popup returns browser control to a surviving tab in the same session, preserving cookies and site storage. 如果所有标签页均已关闭，下一次浏览器操作会在同一会话中打开一个标签页。 If every tab closes, the next browser action opens a tab in that same session. 在由 DeerFlow 管理的浏览器中，恢复的标签页会被置于前台；通过 CDP 连接的 Chrome 则会保持用户当前的标签页焦点。 Recovered tabs are brought to the front in DeerFlow-managed browsers; CDP-attached Chrome keeps the user's current tab focus.
工作区的 Browser Live 客户端通过协商采用二进制 JPEG WebSocket 帧， The workspace Browser Live client negotiates binary JPEG WebSocket frames,
每次显示刷新仅保留最新的待处理帧，并撤销被替换的 keeps only the newest pending frame per display refresh, and revokes replaced
对象 URL。 object URLs. Gateway 控制消息仍使用 JSON，而未 Gateway control messages remain JSON, and clients that do not
请求二进制能力的客户端将继续使用旧版 JSON/base64 帧协议。 request the binary capability retain the legacy JSON/base64 frame protocol.
建立 Live 连接时，握手时限为 10 秒。 Opening a Live connection has a 10-second handshake deadline. 停滞的 A stalled
握手会进入现有的有上限退避机制，最多重连 six 次； handshake enters the existing capped backoff, with up to six reconnects;
连接成功后会重置该重试额度。 successful connections reset that retry budget. 该面板会区分连接 The panel distinguishes connection
超时与其他连接故障，并显示恢复指导，包括 timeouts from other connection failures and shows recovery guidance, including
当重试次数耗尽时。 when retries are exhausted. 关闭 Live 后再重新开启，即可重置重试额度。 Turn Live off and on to start a fresh retry budget.
上下文工程Context Engineering
**表格工具输出概要**：CSV/TSV 的行数按逻辑记录计算，因此带引号字段内的换行符不会增加行数。 **Table Tool-Output Synopses**: CSV/TSV row counts reflect logical records, so newlines inside quoted fields do not add rows. 如果解析失败或超出字段数量限制，概要会将记录数报告为无法确定。 If parsing fails or exceeds the field limit, the synopsis reports an undetermined count. 超出输入大小限制的输出仍会跳过结构化解析。 Outputs beyond the input-size limit still skip structured parsing.
**隔离的子智能体上下文**：每个子智能体都在各自独立隔离的上下文中运行。 **Isolated Sub-Agent Context**: Each sub-agent runs in its own isolated context. 这意味着子智能体无法查看主智能体或其他子智能体的上下文。 This means that the sub-agent will not be able to see the context of the main agent or other sub-agents. 这一点很重要，可以确保子智能体专注于当前任务，而不会受到主智能体或其他子智能体上下文的干扰。 This is important to ensure that the sub-agent is able to focus on the task at hand and not be distracted by the context of the main agent or other sub-agents.
**摘要**：在会话中，DeerFlow 会积极管理上下文——对已完成的子任务进行摘要，将中间结果转存至文件系统，并压缩不再与当前任务直接相关的内容。 **Summarization**: Within a session, DeerFlow manages context aggressively — summarizing completed sub-tasks, offloading intermediate results to the filesystem, compressing what's no longer immediately relevant. 这使它能够在执行耗时较长、包含多个步骤的任务时保持敏锐，而不超出上下文窗口的容量。 This lets it stay sharp across long, multi-step tasks without blowing the context window.
**工具输出预览额度**：已转存到外部的结果，其结构化概要有字符数限制。 **Tool Output Preview Budget**: Structured synopses of externalized results have a character budget. 过长的 JSON 键和路径会使用 `...` 缩短，超出大小限制的概要正文会包含截断提示。 Long JSON keys and paths are shortened with `...`, and oversized synopsis bodies include a truncation notice. 原始的头部和尾部样本、完整文件引用以及 `read_file` 使用指导都会保留；如需准确的键或完整输出，请读取已保存的文件。 Raw head/tail samples, the full file reference, and `read_file` guidance are preserved; read the saved file for exact keys or complete output.
**严格的工具调用恢复**：当提供方或中间件中断工具调用循环时，DeerFlow 现在会移除被强制停止的助手消息中提供方级别的原始工具调用元数据，并在下一次调用模型之前，为尚未收到结果的调用注入占位工具结果。 **Strict Tool-Call Recovery**: When a provider or middleware interrupts a tool-call loop, DeerFlow now strips provider-level raw tool-call metadata on forced-stop assistant messages and injects placeholder tool results for dangling calls before the next model invocation. 这可以防止严格验证 `tool_call_id` 序列的 OpenAI 兼容推理模型因历史记录格式错误而失败。 This keeps OpenAI-compatible reasoning models that strictly validate `tool_call_id` sequences from failing with malformed history errors.
**提供方安全停止**：启用 `safety_finish_reason.enabled` 后，原生 OpenAI Responses 中 `status=incomplete` 且 `incomplete_details.reason=content_filter` 的响应会抑制工具调用，包括格式错误的调用，并从后续请求中移除这些调用的原生帧。 **Provider Safety Stops**: With `safety_finish_reason.enabled`, native OpenAI Responses `status=incomplete` with `incomplete_details.reason=content_filter` suppresses tool calls, including malformed calls, and removes their native frames from subsequent requests. 对于被过滤后为空的响应，系统会提供说明；对于不含工具调用的可见部分回答，则予以保留。 Empty filtered responses receive an explanation; visible partial answers without tool calls are preserved.
**可见的工具运行完成状态**：对于交互式轮次，如果工具执行后的最终响应为空，DeerFlow 会重试一次，随后显示可见的错误，而不会无声地报告运行成功。 **Visible Tool-Run Completion**: For interactive turns, DeerFlow retries an empty post-tool final response once, then surfaces a visible error instead of reporting a silent successful run.
**运行历史记录**：仅包含图像的用户输入在每次运行中只记录一次，即使智能体进行了多次模型调用也是如此。 **Run History**: Image-only user input is recorded once per run, even when the agent makes multiple model calls. 其媒体内容会保留在历史记录中，无需文本摘要。 Its media content remains in history without requiring a text summary.
Web 历史记录会将各个工具结果保留在其原始位置，当 Web history keeps separate tool results in their original positions when a
提供方跨轮次或跨运行重复使用工具调用 ID 时。 provider reuses a tool-call ID across turns or runs. 同一结果的副本 Copies of the same result
通过其消息 ID 进行匹配。 are matched by their message ID. 没有消息 ID 的旧版结果会被保留 Legacy results without message IDs are kept
为独立条目，因为仅凭其提供方 ID 无法识别一次调用。 separately because their provider ID alone cannot identify an invocation.
在对应的助手消息之前送达的结果仍能正确关联，只要该调用是唯一的 A result delivered before its assistant still resolves when that call is unique
在该轮次中。 in the turn. 从当前同一次调用重建的结果只保留一条历史记录。 Rebuilt results from the same current invocation keep one history entry.

大多数智能体在对话结束的那一刻就会忘记一切。 Most agents forget everything the moment a conversation ends. DeerFlow 则会记住。 DeerFlow remembers.

DeerFlow 会跨会话构建持久记忆，记录你的个人资料、偏好和积累的知识。 Across sessions, DeerFlow builds a persistent memory of your profile, preferences, and accumulated knowledge. 你使用得越多，它就越了解你——你的写作风格、技术栈以及经常使用的工作流程。 The more you use it, the better it knows you — your writing style, your technical stack, your recurring workflows. 记忆存储在本地，始终由你掌控。 Memory is stored locally and stays under your control.

即使包含可见文本，也会被排除在记忆提取之外。 excluded from memory extraction even when they contain visible text.

对于格式错误、无法解码或嵌套过深的 mem0 JSON 响应、非对象类型的 Malformed, undecodable, or excessively nested mem0 JSON responses, non-object
封装结构以及无效的结果容器，均遵循所配置的读取失败 envelopes, and invalid result containers follow the configured read failure
策略：`fail_open` 返回空的记忆召回数据，而 `fail_closed` 则引发 policy: `fail_open` returns empty recall data, while `fail_closed` raises a

提供需要主动启用的 `honcho` 后端，可用于自托管或托管的 Honcho（v3 An opt-in `honcho` backend is available for self-hosted or hosted Honcho (v3
API）。 API). 它会构建用户模型记忆——长期偏好和跨会话的 It builds user-model memory — long-term preferences and a cross-session
工作表征——这一过程在 Honcho 服务器端完成，因此该后端不会在本地进行 LLM working representation — on Honcho's server side, so the backend makes no LLM
调用。 calls locally. 每个用户都拥有一个根据 `user_id` 派生的独立工作区； Each user gets an isolated workspace derived from `user_id`; a
缺少用户 id 时会采取关闭式失败处理，而不会回退到共享工作区。 missing user id fails closed instead of falling back to a shared workspace.
框架隐藏的用户消息不会被写入 Honcho；宿主的 Framework-hidden user messages are excluded from Honcho writes; the host's
人工输入过滤器会保留隐藏消息中承载的有效用户澄清答复。 human-input filter preserves valid user clarification answers carried in hidden messages.
如果该过滤器引发异常，则仅跳过受影响的隐藏消息； If that filter raises an exception, only the affected hidden message is skipped;
该批次中的其他消息仍可被记录。 other messages in the batch can still be captured.
此后端不支持事实的创建、读取、更新和删除（CRUD），也不支持在设置页面编辑事实。 Fact CRUD and Settings-page fact editing are not available for this backend. 请参阅 See
[Honcho 后端指南](backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/honcho/README.md)。 the [Honcho backend guide](backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/honcho/README.md).
记忆更新现在会在应用时跳过重复的事实条目，因此重复的偏好和上下文不会在跨会话过程中无限累积。 Memory updates now skip duplicate fact entries at apply time, so repeated preferences and context do not accumulate endlessly across sessions.
旧版记忆文件在读取或导入时会进行规范化处理，包括其中可恢复的事实元数据，从而使旧的本地数据在模式演进过程中仍可使用。 Legacy memory files are normalized when read or imported, including recoverable fact metadata, so older local data remains usable as the schema evolves. 前端和后端的规范化处理会在置信度缺失或无效时使用 `0.5`，将空白来源默认设为 `unknown`，并去除事实内容首尾的空白。 Frontend and backend normalization use confidence `0.5` when missing or invalid, default blank sources to `unknown`, and trim fact content. 如果存储的置信度无法转换为有限浮点数，例如手动编辑的文件中出现了无界 JSON 整数，也会回退为 `0.5`，而不会导致记忆读取、导入、提示构建或相关性排序失败。 A stored confidence that cannot become a finite float, such as an unbounded JSON integer in a hand-edited file, also falls back to `0.5` instead of failing a memory read, import, prompt build or relevance ranking.
在默认的 DeerMem `middleware` 模式下，自动提取现在会先按作用域、持久性和权威性对每条候选事实进行分类，再由确定性写入门控决定是否接受。 In the default DeerMem `middleware` mode, automatic extraction now classifies every proposed fact by scope, durability, and authority before a deterministic write gate accepts it. 系统仅存储持久、描述性的用户级事实；当前会话线程或项目的约束以及一次性操作权限仍保留在对话状态中。 Only durable, descriptive user-level facts are stored; current-thread or project constraints and one-time action permissions stay in conversation state. 用户全局摘要必须同时满足用户作用域和描述性权威性要求，因矛盾而执行的移除受作用域门控约束，而依赖替代项的移除仅在其替代项确实通过验证并成功存储后才会执行。 User-global summaries require both user scope and descriptive authority, contradiction removals are scope-gated, and a replacement-dependent removal is applied only when its replacement actually survives validation and storage. 这些分类标签仅作为提取阶段的元数据，不增加额外的 LLM 调用，也不会写入事实文件。 These classification labels are extraction-only metadata, add no extra LLM call, and are not written into the fact files. `memory.mode: tool` 中的显式 CRUD 工具仍是一条独立的、由模型主导的路径。 The explicit CRUD tools in `memory.mode: tool` remain a separate, model-directed path. 通过 `memory.backend_config.prompts_dir` 覆盖内置 DeerMem 提示的部署，必须将新的分类字段添加到自定义模板中（即 `memory_update` 的事实、摘要和移除格式，以及 `consolidation` 的合并事实模式）：写入门控采用失败即拒绝的策略，因此未迁移的模板会阻止所有由提取驱动的事实、摘要和移除写入，且仅通过 `rejected_by_scope_gate` 指标和高拒绝率警告体现出来。 Deployments that override the bundled DeerMem prompts via `memory.backend_config.prompts_dir` must add the new classification fields to their custom templates (the `memory_update` fact/summary/removal formats and the `consolidation` consolidated-fact schema): the write gate fails closed, so an un-migrated template stops every extraction-driven fact, summary, and removal write, surfacing only through the `rejected_by_scope_gate` metrics and the high-rejection-rate warning.
当某个事实作用域内的事实数量达到 `max_facts` 时，DeerMem 默认仍使用以往仅依据置信度的淘汰顺序。 When a fact scope reaches `max_facts`, DeerMem still uses the historical confidence-only eviction order by default. 运维人员可以选择启用 `memory.backend_config.fact_eviction_policy: hybrid-v1`，该策略综合考虑有界置信度（65%）、显式确认的新近程度（25%）以及查询驱动的访问热度（10%）。 Operators can opt in to `memory.backend_config.fact_eviction_policy: hybrid-v1`, which combines bounded confidence (65%), explicit-confirmation freshness (25%), and query-driven access heat (10%). 仅在启用 hybrid-v1 或影子模式时，才会收集混合信号元数据。 Hybrid signal metadata is collected only while hybrid-v1 or shadow mode is enabled. 显式确认由现有的记忆更新 LLM 调用以 `factsToReinforce` 返回，且仅在确定性消息处理也检测到用户强化信号时才会被接受；它还会重置该事实的陈旧性审查计时。 Explicit confirmation is returned as `factsToReinforce` by the existing memory-update LLM call and is accepted only when deterministic message processing also detects a user reinforcement signal; it also resets the fact's staleness-review clock. 此确定性门控作用于批次层面：它只能确定，当前提取批次经过筛选后的最后六条消息中，有一条人类消息匹配了强化模式。 This deterministic gate is batch-level: it establishes only that a human message among the last six filtered messages in the current extraction batch matched a reinforcement pattern. 由 LLM 选出的 `factsToReinforce` ID 提供事实绑定；DeerMem 不会独立验证信号与事实之间的对应关系。 The LLM-selected `factsToReinforce` ID supplies the fact binding; DeerMem does not independently verify a signal-to-fact correspondence. 重复提取或自动注入绝不会构成对事实的确认。 Repeated extraction or automatic injection never confirms a fact. 自定义 `memory_update` 提示应添加可选的 `factsToReinforce` 数组，以参与确认新近程度的计算。 Custom `memory_update` prompts should add the optional `factsToReinforce` array to participate in confirmation freshness. 访问热度存储在独立的、随时间衰减的附属文件中，并且仅在 `memory_search` 确实返回该事实时才会增加，因此读取不会重写作为权威记录的 Markdown 文件或其 `updatedAt`。 Access heat is stored in a separate decaying sidecar and increases only when `memory_search` actually returns the fact, so reads do not rewrite canonical Markdown or its `updatedAt`. 混合模式还会为纠正条目预留数量受限的最低配额（上限的 10%，最多 10 个；未使用的配额重新参与常规竞争）。 Hybrid mode also reserves a bounded minimum of correction slots (10% of the cap, at most 10; unused slots return to normal competition). 因容量限制而进行的删除仍是物理删除，但一个规模受限、仅包含元数据的审计记录会记录事实 ID、类别、策略评分和原因，而不复制事实内容。`fact_eviction_shadow_enabled: true` 会在默认策略运行的同时评估 hybrid-v1，而不改变实际保留结果。 Capacity deletion remains physical, but a bounded metadata-only audit records fact IDs, categories, policy scores, and reasons without copying fact content. `fact_eviction_shadow_enabled: true` evaluates hybrid-v1 alongside the default policy without changing actual retention. 此功能不会增加 LLM 调用，并且可通过选择 `confidence` 回滚。 This feature adds no LLM invocation and can be rolled back by selecting `confidence`.
基于文件的记忆现在将全局用户上下文与智能体事实分开存储。 File-backed memory now separates global user context from agent facts. 每个用户都有一个 `memory.json`，其中仅包含与项目无关的 `user` 和 `history` 摘要；每条事实都以作为权威记录的 Markdown 文件形式存储在 `agents/{agent_name}/facts/` 下。 Each user has one `memory.json` containing only the project-independent `user` and `history` summaries; every fact is a canonical Markdown file below `agents/{agent_name}/facts/`. 现有的主智能体中间件、API、Settings、导入/导出以及嵌入式客户端调用若省略 `agent_name`，都会在 DeerMem 内部解析到保留的 `__default__` 存储桶。 Existing lead-agent middleware, API, Settings, import/export, and embedded-client calls that omit `agent_name` resolve inside DeerMem to the reserved `__default__` bucket. 该存储桶名称不符合有效的自定义智能体名称语法，因此真正名为 `lead-agent` 的自定义智能体拥有独立的事实库，而且删除自定义智能体时，无法删除不含 `config.yaml`、仅用于记忆存储的目录。 That bucket is outside the valid custom-agent name grammar, so a real custom agent named `lead-agent` has a separate fact repository and deleting a custom agent cannot delete a memory-only directory without `config.yaml`. 公开的智能体标识符不区分大小写，并统一规范为小写。 Public agent identifiers are case-insensitive and canonicalized to lowercase. 运行时/API 读取方仍会收到所选智能体或默认智能体的兼容性 `facts` 数组，因此前端不会从 `memory.json` 读取智能体事实；结构化的 Markdown `source` 元数据会在 MemoryManager 边界处映射为原有的字符串字段。 Runtime/API readers still receive a compatibility `facts` array for the selected/default agent, so the frontend does not read agent facts from `memory.json`; structured Markdown `source` metadata is projected to the historical string field at the MemoryManager boundary. 未限定作用域的“全部清除”（Clear All）会先迁移尚未读取的旧版各智能体 JSON 中的事实，但不接纳其中即将被清除的摘要，随后从所有智能体存储桶中删除共享摘要和事实，同时保留智能体配置文件，从而防止后续读取时恢复此前跳过的旧版事实；显式限定为某个智能体的清除操作只删除该智能体的事实。 An unscoped Clear All first migrates facts from unread legacy per-agent JSON without adopting its soon-to-be-cleared summaries, then removes shared summaries and facts from every agent bucket while preserving agent configuration files, so a later read cannot resurrect skipped legacy facts; an explicitly agent-scoped clear removes only that agent's facts. 首次正常读取时，嵌入用户 JSON 中的旧事实会自动迁移到 `__default__`；如果先前隐式使用的 `lead-agent` 存储桶所对应的目录并非真正的自定义智能体目录，写入该存储桶的事实也会一并迁移。 On first normal read, old facts embedded in the user JSON are migrated automatically to `__default__`; facts written to the earlier implicit `lead-agent` bucket are also moved when that directory is not a real custom agent. 迁移和正常写入都只会在持久化存储锁释放后，才通知已配置的检索适配器。 Migration and normal writes notify the configured retrieval adapter only after durable storage locks are released. DeerMem 默认使用支持作用域的 SQLite FTS5/BM25 适配器，仅在 `.retrieval/` 下存储可重建的派生索引数据（若设置了 `memory.backend_config.retrieval_index_path`，则存储在该路径下；相对路径以 `storage_path` 为基准解析），并在 Gateway 启动期间于后台重建索引，或在首次限定作用域的搜索时按需重建。 DeerMem uses a scope-aware SQLite FTS5/BM25 adapter by default, stores only rebuildable derived index data under `.retrieval/` (or under `memory.backend_config.retrieval_index_path` when set; a relative value resolves against `storage_path`), and rebuilds it in the background during Gateway startup or lazily on the first scoped search. 损坏的派生索引会自动重新创建。 A corrupt derived index is recreated automatically. 当多个 Gateway 实例共享同一个 `storage_path` 时，应将 `retrieval_index_path` 指向实例本地磁盘：SQLite 的 WAL 模式不支持网络文件系统；其他实例写入后，每个实例都会在下一次搜索时重新同步相应用户的索引；如果已声明为多实例部署（`deployment.multi_instance: true` / `DEER_FLOW_MULTI_INSTANCE=1`），却仍将索引放在记忆根目录下，系统会在启动时记录警告。 When several Gateway instances share one `storage_path`, point `retrieval_index_path` at instance-local disk: SQLite's WAL mode is unsupported on network filesystems, each instance re-syncs a user's index on its next search after a peer writes, and a declared multi-instance deployment (`deployment.multi_instance: true` / `DEER_FLOW_MULTI_INSTANCE=1`) that leaves the index under the memory root logs a warning at startup. Helm chart 为其挂载了一个 Pod 本地的 `emptyDir`。 The Helm chart mounts a Pod-local `emptyDir` for it. 将 `memory.backend_config.retrieval_adapter` 设为空字符串，即可禁用该适配器，并使用本地子串匹配作为回退方案。 Set `memory.backend_config.retrieval_adapter` to an empty string to disable it and use the local substring fallback. 中文分词是可选功能；安装后端的 `memory-zh` 扩展依赖（`uv sync --extra memory-zh`），即可使用 jieba 辅助的子短语搜索。 Chinese tokenization is optional; install the backend `memory-zh` extra (`uv sync --extra memory-zh`) for jieba-assisted sub-phrase search. 日志化写入、共享用户锁以及乐观用户记忆修订机制可防止更新在无提示的情况下丢失。 Journaled writes, a shared user lock, and optimistic user-memory revisions prevent silent lost updates.
设置 `memory.backend_config.retrieval_relevance_enabled: true`，即可启用确定性的相关性/置信度排序和查询感知注入。 Set `memory.backend_config.retrieval_relevance_enabled: true` to opt into deterministic relevance/confidence ranking and query-aware injection. 这会**在搜索时绕过 `retrieval_adapter`**，包括默认的 FTS5/BM25 适配器和自定义适配器；索引仍按配置运行。`retrieval_relevance_weight` 控制混合权重，`retrieval_diversity_weight` 用于启用近重复惩罚（默认值为 0）。 This **bypasses `retrieval_adapter` for search**, including the default FTS5/BM25 and custom adapters; indexing remains configured. `retrieval_relevance_weight` controls the blend and `retrieval_diversity_weight` enables near-duplicate penalties (default 0). 评分时，每条查询或事实最多只使用前 4096 个字符和 128 个词元。 Scoring uses at most the first 4096 characters and 128 tokens per query/fact. 搜索仅对最多 `top_k` 条结果进行多样化处理；注入则分别对保证注入的事实和常规事实进行多样化处理，直至达到各自的词元预算。 Search diversifies only up to `top_k`; injection diversifies guaranteed and regular facts independently until their token budgets are reached. 保持此功能禁用，即可保留现有的检索和注入行为。 Leave the feature disabled to retain the existing retrieval and injection behavior.
词汇相关性衡量的是经 IDF 加权的不同查询词的覆盖程度，因此 Lexical relevance measures IDF-weighted coverage of distinct query terms, so
重复的部分匹配无法仅靠使评分达到饱和，就与完整匹配获得相同的 repeated partial matches cannot tie a complete match merely by saturating the

对于相同的用户/智能体候选作用域，搜索和自动注入使用相同的 IDF 权重 Search and automatic injection use the same IDF weighting for the same

记忆注入遵循所配置的运行模式。 Memory injection follows the configured operation mode. 在 `middleware` 模式下，DeerMem 会注入用户全局摘要和所选智能体的事实。 In `middleware` mode, DeerMem injects the user-global summaries and the selected agent's facts. 自定义智能体的引导对话也使用该智能体的事实存储桶，因此设置细节不会泄露到默认智能体的记忆中。 Custom-agent bootstrap conversations use that agent's fact bucket as well, so setup details do not leak into the default agent's memory. 在 `tool` 模式下，自动生成的 `` 块仅包含全局 `user` 和 `history` 摘要；智能体事实通过 `memory_search` 显式检索，从而避免自动注入与工具返回的事实上下文重复。 In `tool` mode, the automatic `` block contains only the global `user` and `history` summaries; agent facts are retrieved explicitly through `memory_search`, avoiding duplicate automatic and tool-returned fact context. 在任一模式下，设置 `memory.injection_enabled: false` 仍会禁用整个块。 Setting `memory.injection_enabled: false` still disables the entire block in either mode. 当事实 ID 已知时，`memory_get(fact_id)` 会直接返回当前用户和智能体作用域内该 ID 对应的确切事实，无须按内容搜索。 When a fact ID is already known, `memory_get(fact_id)` returns that exact fact from the current user and agent scope without searching by content. ID 不存在或超出作用域时，会返回未找到错误；后端若不具备所需的读取能力或智能体作用域支持能力，则返回不支持错误。 Missing or out-of-scope IDs return a not-found error; backends without the required read or agent-scope capability return an unsupported error.
单个自定义智能体可以选择禁用记忆，而无须更改全局设置。 An individual Custom Agent can opt out of memory without changing the global setting. 在该智能体的 `users/{user_id}/agents/{name}/config.yaml` 中添加 `memory_enabled: false`。 Add `memory_enabled: false` to that agent's `users/{user_id}/agents/{name}/config.yaml`. 该智能体仍会收到当前日期提醒，但 DeerFlow 不会为其注入召回的记忆、将被动更新或摘要驱动的记忆更新（包括手动执行 `/compact`）加入队列、提供记忆工具，或添加记忆工具使用说明。 The agent still receives the current-date reminder, but DeerFlow does not inject recalled memory, queue passive or summarization-driven memory updates (including manual `/compact`), expose memory tools, or add memory-tool instructions for that agent. 如果某个现有智能体被关闭，则在下一次模型调用之前，其先前注入的记忆块会从检查点状态中移除，而其日期提醒和对话会保留。 If an existing agent is switched off, its previously injected memory block is removed from checkpoint state before the next model call while its date reminder and conversation remain. 省略该字段（或将其设为 `true`）会保留现有的全局 `memory` 行为。 Omitting the field (or setting it to `true`) preserves the existing global `memory` behavior.
针对单条事实的存储库操作确实采用增量方式：更新插入/删除操作仅对指定的事实文件执行读取、日志记录、写入和重新索引，并返回明确标示为不完整的增量，而非依赖缓存伪装出来的完整文档。 Single-fact repository operations are genuinely incremental: an upsert/delete reads, journals, writes, and re-indexes only the addressed fact files, and returns an explicit incomplete delta rather than a cache-dependent fake full document. 摘要变更集会将提供的 `user`/`history` 子键合并到已持久化的相应部分中，使局部更新无法擦除未提供的同级键；完整导入则会先将这两个部分规范化为完整的兼容性模式，再应用替换值。 Summary change sets merge the supplied `user`/`history` child keys over the persisted sections so a partial update cannot erase omitted siblings; full imports normalize both sections to the complete compatibility schema before applying replacement values. 对于格式错误的事实列表或空白/非文本内容，导入操作会在更改已存储的记忆之前返回 HTTP 400 并予以拒绝；可恢复的旧版元数据仍会获得默认值。 Imports reject malformed fact lists or blank/non-text content with HTTP 400 before changing stored memory; recoverable legacy metadata still receives defaults. 显式提供的空事实列表仍被视为有意清空。 An explicit empty fact list remains an intentional clear. 管理器/API 的兼容性方法仅在其公开响应契约要求时，才会构建一份新的完整文档。 Manager/API compatibility methods materialize a fresh full document only when their public response contract requires one. 事实级单点操作分别使用预期的用户记忆修订版本和事实修订版本，并且在所有指定事实的前置条件仍然成立时，可以显式执行变基。 Fact-level point operations use separate expected user-memory and fact revisions and may explicitly rebase when every addressed fact precondition still holds. 限定范围的清空、带数量上限的创建、整合和裁剪等基于快照的操作，绝不会重放过时的删除/裁剪集合：发生清单冲突时，会重新加载完整文档并重新计算操作，且重试次数有限。 Snapshot-derived operations such as scoped clear, capped create, consolidation, and trimming never replay stale delete/trim sets: a manifest conflict reloads the complete document and recomputes the operation, with a bounded retry. 事实路径使用 `SHA-256(fact_id)` 的前两个十六进制字符，使生成的 `fact_*` ID 分布到各个分片中。 Fact paths use the first two hexadecimal characters of `SHA-256(fact_id)` so generated `fact_*` IDs distribute across shards. 缓存令牌结合了共享 JSON 的纳秒级修改时间（mtime）、大小和已持久化的修订版本；这样无需扫描事实文件，就能防止因修改时间精度较低且文件大小不变的写入而返回过时数据。 The cache token combines the shared JSON's nanosecond mtime, size, and persisted revision; this prevents coarse-mtime same-size writes from returning stale data without scanning fact files. 直接在系统之外编辑 Markdown 后，需要显式重新加载。 Direct out-of-band Markdown edits require an explicit reload. 存储特有的冲突和数据损坏会在 MemoryManager 边界处进行转换；Gateway 将冲突作为 HTTP 409 返回，并将稳定且不含敏感信息的数据损坏错误作为 HTTP 500 返回。 Storage-specific conflicts and corruption are translated at the MemoryManager boundary; the Gateway returns conflict as HTTP 409 and a stable, non-sensitive corruption error as HTTP 500. 完整文档的 `save()` 仍作为兼容性 API 保留，并在写入前计算差异；格式错误或缺失的 `facts` 不再能够静默擦除智能体的 Markdown 文件。 Full-document `save()` remains a compatibility API and computes a diff before writing; malformed or missing `facts` can no longer silently erase an agent's Markdown files. 旧版迁移会在删除智能体的 `memory.json` 之前保留非空的 `user`/`history`；如果摘要发生冲突，则会保留旧版文件并明确报错，而非选择其中一方。 Legacy migration preserves non-empty `user`/`history` before deleting an agent `memory.json`; conflicting summaries keep the legacy file and fail loudly instead of choosing a winner.
用户首次正常读取记忆时，`memory.json` 中的旧版事实会自动迁移到保留的 `__default__` Markdown 存储桶中。 Legacy facts in `memory.json` migrate automatically into the reserved `__default__` Markdown bucket on the user's first normal memory read. 希望在开始处理流量之前审计或完成迁移的运维人员，可以在 `backend/` 下运行可选的幂等 CLI： Operators who prefer to audit or complete the migration before serving traffic can run the optional idempotent CLI from `backend/`: 
PYTHONPATH=. python scripts/migrate_memory_markdown.py --all-users --dry-run
PYTHONPATH=. python scripts/migrate_memory_markdown.py --all-users
# A custom DeerMem root or original non-directory-safe identity can be explicit:
PYTHONPATH=. python scripts/migrate_memory_markdown.py --storage-path /path/to/deerflow-home --user-id 'test@example.com'
对于运行中的应用程序，从 v1 到 v2 的存储迁移是单向的：此 PR 之前的代码无法读取 Markdown 事实。 The v1-to-v2 storage migration is one-way for a running application: pre-PR code does not read Markdown facts. 在升级持久化部署之前，请停止 DeerFlow，并为配置的记忆存储根目录创建文件系统快照或完整备份。 Before upgrading a persistent deployment, stop DeerFlow and take a filesystem snapshot or full backup of the configured memory storage root. 迁移还会在写入 v2 数据之前，将每个将被破坏性修改的 JSON 源文件以 `{manifest_filename}.v1.bak` 的形式持久保存在原路径旁；如果已有备份不匹配或备份写入失败，迁移会停止，且不会修改 v1 源文件。 The migration also durably retains each destructive JSON source beside the original path as `{manifest_filename}.v1.bak` before writing v2 data; an existing mismatched backup or a backup-write failure stops migration without modifying the v1 source. 此本地备份保留了迁移前的数据，但不能替代完整快照，也不包含升级后创建的事实。 This local backup preserves pre-migration data but is not a substitute for a full snapshot and does not contain facts created after the upgrade.
`--user-id` 可以重复指定。`--all-users` 会发现所选存储根目录下现有的目录安全存储桶；对于曾传入包含 `@` 等字符的原始 ID 的独立集成，应通过 `--user-id` 使用原始值。 `--user-id` may be repeated. `--all-users` discovers the existing directory-safe buckets below the selected storage root; standalone integrations that passed raw IDs containing characters such as `@` should use the original value with `--user-id`. 某个用户的迁移失败会被报告，且不会掩盖其余审计结果；只要有任何用户失败，命令就会以非零状态码退出。 A failed user's migration is reported without hiding the rest of the audit, and the command exits non-zero when any user fails. 首次读取时自动迁移的路径仍保持启用，因此启动时不要求运行此 CLI。 The automatic first-read path remains enabled, so running this CLI is not required for startup.
推荐模型Recommended Models
DeerFlow 不依赖特定模型——它适用于任何实现 OpenAI 兼容 API 的 LLM。 DeerFlow is model-agnostic — it works with any LLM that implements the OpenAI-compatible API. 不过，使用支持以下能力的模型时，其表现最佳： That said, it performs best with models that support:
**长上下文窗口**（100k+ tokens），用于深度研究和多步骤任务 **Long context windows** (100k+ tokens) for deep research and multi-step tasks
**推理能力**，用于自适应规划和复杂任务分解 **Reasoning capabilities** for adaptive planning and complex decomposition
**多模态输入**，用于图像理解和视频理解 **Multimodal inputs** for image understanding and video comprehension
**强大的工具使用能力**，用于可靠的函数调用和结构化输出 **Strong tool-use** for reliable function calling and structured outputs
MindIE XML 工具调用参数支持带符号、小数和指数形式的 JSON MindIE XML tool-call arguments support signed, fractional, and exponent JSON
数值。 numbers. 如果某个数值发生上溢、下溢至零，或超出 Python 的整数 If a number overflows, underflows to zero, or exceeds Python's integer
位数限制，则完整参数会保持为字符串，包括嵌套的 JSON 列表 digit limit, the complete argument stays a string, including nested JSON lists
和对象，以免解析时静默改变其值。 and objects, so parsing does not silently change its value. Python 字面量集合 Python-literal sets
以及包含非有限浮点数的元组，也会将完整参数保留为 and tuples containing non-finite floats also retain the complete argument as
文本，即使它们嵌套在其他容器中也是如此。 text, including when nested in other containers. 字典的键和值 Dictionary keys and values
也会被递归检查。 are checked recursively as well.

`DeerFlowClient.stream()` 在每个 `values` 事件中都包含 `summary_text`。 `DeerFlowClient.stream()` includes `summary_text` in each `values` event. 这是当前压缩后的上下文摘要；若不存在，则为 `None`。 This is the current compacted context summary, or `None` when absent. 使用方无需读取检查点内部数据即可记录变化；重复的快照可能携带相同的摘要，而初始快照可能已经包含来自先前轮次的摘要。 Consumers can record changes without reading checkpoint internals; repeated snapshots may carry the same summary, and an initial snapshot may already contain one from an earlier turn.
DeerFlow 可以作为嵌入式 Python 库使用，无需运行完整的 HTTP 服务。 DeerFlow can be used as an embedded Python library without running the full HTTP services. `DeerFlowClient` 提供对所有智能体和 Gateway 功能的直接进程内访问，并返回与 HTTP Gateway API 相同的响应模式。 The `DeerFlowClient` provides direct in-process access to all agent and Gateway capabilities, returning the same response schemas as the HTTP Gateway API. HTTP Gateway 还提供 `DELETE /api/threads/{thread_id}`，用于在 LangGraph 线程本身被删除后，移除由 DeerFlow 管理的本地线程数据： The HTTP Gateway also exposes `DELETE /api/threads/{thread_id}` to remove DeerFlow-managed local thread data after the LangGraph thread itself has been deleted:

对于存储在数据库中的运行事件，删除某次运行会保留其所属线程的序列水位标记。 For database-backed run events, deleting a run preserves its thread's sequence watermark. 删除线程时，一旦没有剩余事件，就会移除该水位标记，从而允许重新创建的线程从序列号 1 开始。 Thread deletion removes that watermark once no events remain, allowing a recreated thread to restart at sequence 1. 按所有者范围执行删除时，如果仍有其他所有者的事件，则会保留该水位标记。 Owner-scoped deletion preserves the watermark when another owner's events remain.
单进程 JSONL 事件存储也会保留线程序列水位标记， Single-process JSONL event storage also retains the thread sequence watermark
使其在删除运行和重启后仍然存在，因此使用 `after_seq` 的客户端不会遗漏后续的 across run deletion and restarts, so clients using `after_seq` do not miss later
消息。 messages. 备份此后端时，请将 `runs/.seq-watermark` 与运行文件一同保留 Keep `runs/.seq-watermark` with the run files when backing up this
后端。 backend. 新建或替换的水位标记继承被删除运行文件的权限位。 New or replaced watermarks inherit the deleted run file's permission bits.
删除整个线程会移除水位标记并重置分配。 Deleting the complete thread removes the watermark and resets allocation.

from deerflow.client import DeerFlowClient

client = DeerFlowClient()

# Chat
response = client.chat("Analyze this paper for me", thread_id="my-thread")

# Streaming (LangGraph SSE protocol: values, messages-tuple, end)
for event in client.stream("hello"):
    if event.type == "messages-tuple" and event.data.get("type") == "ai":
        print(event.data["content"])
    elif event.type == "messages-tuple" and event.data.get("type") == "tool" and "artifact" in event.data:
        # Structured tool artifacts (for example, ask_clarification cards)
        # are preserved when the ToolMessage provides one.
        print(event.data["artifact"])

# Configuration & management — returns Gateway-aligned dicts
models = client.list_models()        # {"models": [...]}
skills = client.list_skills()        # {"skills": [...]}
client.update_skill("web-search", enabled=True)
client.upload_files("thread-1", ["./report.pdf"])  # {"success": True, "files": [...]}
client.set_goal("thread-1", "finish the implementation and make all tests pass")
client.get_goal("thread-1")       # {"goal": {...}} or {"goal": None}
client.clear_goal("thread-1")
继续某个线程时，`values` 仍包含完整的对话状态，而 `messages-tuple` 和 `end.usage` 仅涵盖当前轮次。 When continuing a thread, `values` still contains the full conversation state, while `messages-tuple` and `end.usage` cover only the current turn.
嵌入式客户端的 `end.usage` 会累加流式数据块中的用量增量， The embedded client's `end.usage` sums usage deltas across streamed chunks,
其中也包括原生 Gemini 等在每次 including providers such as native Gemini that report usage more than once per
回复中多次报告用量的提供方。 reply. 已完成消息的用量会进行核对调整，而不会再次计入这些 The completed message's usage is reconciled without counting those
数据块；即使已完成消息中的计数较低，也不会扣除数据块已经 chunks again; a lower completed-message count does not subtract tokens already
报告的 token 数。 reported by chunks. 仍然支持提供方在数据块中给出的带正负号的修正值，也 Signed provider corrections in chunks remain supported, as
支持仅在完成时报告用量的提供方。 do providers that report usage only at completion.
并发调用 `upload_files()` 时，对于每次调用开始时尚不存在的文件名，会通过 Concurrent `upload_files()` calls preserve filenames absent at the start of each
分配数字后缀来保留文件，并返回实际保存的名称。 call by assigning numeric suffixes and returning the actual saved names. 同样的 The same
规则也适用于新生成的 Markdown 配套文件。 rule applies to new Markdown companions. 对于已经存在的完全相同的名称，仍保留 Exact names already present retain
替换行为。 replacement behavior. 可转换的文件通过一份私有的源文件快照进行处理，贯穿 Convertible files use one private source capture through
所有文件名重试和转换操作，同时保留原始权限和 all filename retries and conversion, preserving the original permissions and
时间戳。 timestamps. 检测到的暂存文件替换会被拒绝；暂存清理 Detected staging-file substitution is rejected; staging cleanup
失败会被记录到日志中，且不会掩盖上传结果。 failures are logged without masking the upload outcome.
HTTP Gateway 接受 `values`、`messages-tuple`、`updates`、`debug`、`tasks`、`checkpoints` 和 `custom` 流模式。 The HTTP Gateway accepts `values`, `messages-tuple`, `updates`, `debug`, `tasks`, `checkpoints`, and `custom` stream modes. 对于不受支持的模式（如 `messages` 和 `events`）、不受支持的非默认运行选项（如 webhook、延迟执行或 `multitask_strategy="enqueue"`），以及未声明的 SDK 选项（如检查点持久性覆盖设置），系统会在执行前返回 `422`，而不会静默忽略或降级处理。 Unsupported modes such as `messages` and `events`, unsupported non-default run options such as webhooks, delayed execution, or `multitask_strategy="enqueue"`, and undeclared SDK options such as checkpoint durability overrides return `422` before execution instead of being silently ignored or downgraded.
所有返回字典的方法都会在 CI 中依据 Gateway Pydantic 响应模型进行验证（`TestGatewayConformance`），以确保嵌入式客户端与 HTTP API 模式保持同步。 All dict-returning methods are validated against Gateway Pydantic response models in CI (`TestGatewayConformance`), ensuring the embedded client stays in sync with the HTTP API schemas. 完整的 API 文档请参见 `backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py`。 See `backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py` for full API documentation.

每个项目都存储自由格式的指令——背景、约定以及 Each project stores free-form instructions — background, conventions, and
适用于项目中每个对话的约束——这些内容可在 constraints that apply to every conversation in the project — editable on the

项目内的线程上启动时，Gateway 会一次性固定项目的当前状态，并将 a member thread, the Gateway pins the project's current state once and renders
指令呈现为一个有界的、作用域限于当前请求的 `` 块，仅供该次运行 the instructions as a bounded, request-scoped `` block for that run
使用：该块绝不会进入系统提示词或持久化历史记录，并且每次 only: the block never enters the system prompt or persisted history, and every
新运行都会读取最新保存的指令。 new run sees the latest saved instructions. 指令的大小上限为 Instructions are capped at
`projects.instructions_max_bytes` 个 UTF-8 字节（默认值为 8192，范围为 256–262144）； `projects.instructions_max_bytes` UTF-8 bytes (default 8192, range 256–262144);
多字节字符按其 UTF-8 字节长度计数。 multi-byte characters count as their UTF-8 byte length. 超出大小限制的指令 Oversized instructions
会在写入时被拒绝，并返回 `422`，绝不会被静默截断。 are rejected with a `422` at write time and are never silently truncated.

每个项目都有一个文档架，用于存放整个项目共享的文件，管理入口 Each project has a document shelf for files the whole project shares, managed
位于项目页面的“文档”部分： from the project page's Documents section:
**上传**文件（通过按钮或拖放，每个请求上传一个文件）。 **Upload** a file (button or drag-drop, one file per request). 文档架大小 Shelf size
限制复用 `uploads.max_file_size`（默认 50 MiB）；重新上传相同 limits reuse `uploads.max_file_size` (default 50 MiB); re-uploading identical
内容时会返回现有条目，而不会创建重复条目。 content returns the existing entry instead of creating a duplicate.
超出大小限制的上传返回 `413`；经过去重的上传返回 `200`。 Oversized uploads return `413`; deduplicated uploads return `200`. 暂存 Staging
清理失败会被记录到日志中，但不会替换上述结果，也不会替换项目不存在、 cleanup failures are logged without replacing these outcomes or a missing,
归属其他用户或已归档时的 `404` 响应。 foreign, or archived project's `404` response.

操作完成后丢弃其未完成的文件；等待后续上传数据的过程仍可取消。 operation finishes; waiting for further upload data remains cancellable.
**列出**条目及其名称、大小、修改时间和来源（上传或 **List** entries with name, size, modified time, and provenance (uploaded vs.
从对话中保存），并可预览或下载任意条目。 saved from a conversation), and preview or download any entry.

新的文档架文件名，包括上传时显式指定的 `name` 和转存时的 `shelf_name`， New shelf names, including explicit upload `name` and promotion `shelf_name`,
均遵循常规上传文件名验证规则。 follow ordinary upload filename validation. 包含 NUL、Windows Names containing NUL, Windows
保留设备名（如 `CON.txt`）或以点结尾的名称会被拒绝，并返回 reserved device names (such as `CON.txt`), or trailing dots are rejected with
`400`，此时尚未暂存任何字节。 `400` before bytes are staged.
在原生 Windows 环境中，文档架使用扩展长度的文件系统路径， On native Windows, the document shelf uses extended-length filesystem paths
因此，对于层级较深的工作区目录和较长的文件名，无需启用系统级长路径设置，即可上传、下载、 so deep workspace directories and long filenames can be uploaded, downloaded,
恢复和彻底清除文件。 restored, and purged without enabling the system-wide long-path setting.
文件系统对单个路径组成部分的限制仍然适用。 Filesystem limits on individual path components still apply.
项目内会话线程中的运行还会收到一个大小受限的 `` 索引，该索引在每次 Runs on member threads also receive a bounded `` index rendered per

`list_project_documents` 和 `read_project_document` 工具读取文档。 documents with the `list_project_documents` and `read_project_document` tools.
文档字符计数使用容量受限且线程安全的缓存；并发读取 Document character counts use a bounded, thread-safe cache; concurrent reads
不会因该缓存锁而串行执行完整文件扫描。 do not serialize full file scans behind its cache lock.
文档架转换过程中的清理失败会被记录到日志中，但不会替换读取结果 Shelf conversion cleanup failures are logged without replacing the read result
或其原始错误。 or its original error. 已取消的读取会等待其暂存清理完成； Cancelled reads wait for their staging cleanup to finish;
无法删除的文件仍可由现有的保留期清理机制处理。 files that cannot be removed remain eligible for the existing retention sweep.
归档读取语义Archive read semantics
归档项目会冻结写入操作，但保留读取功能。 Archiving a project freezes writes but keeps reads. 已归档项目中的线程 Threads in an archived
仍会运行，并继续接收该项目的指令和文件架索引， project still run and still receive the project's instructions and shelf index,
而且文件架仍然完全可读：列表查看、预览/下载、 and the shelf remains fully readable: listing, preview/download, the
对话文件浏览器以及附加到线程功能均可继续使用。 conversation-files browser, and attach-to-thread all keep working. 上传、 Uploads,
保存到项目以及将文件架中的单个文件移至回收站，都要求项目处于活动 save-to-project, and moving individual shelf files to trash require an active
状态，且回收站中的文档无法恢复到已归档的项目中。 project, and a trashed document cannot be restored into an archived one.
仍然允许删除已归档的项目，这会将其整个文件架移至 Deleting an archived project remains allowed and moves its whole shelf to
回收站。 trash.
