计划任务Scheduled Tasks
DeerFlow 现已在工作区中内置计划任务功能的最小可行产品（MVP）。 DeerFlow now includes a first-class scheduled-task MVP in the workspace.
编辑一次性任务的标题或提示词时，会保留其原定执行时间，包括秒数，以及夏令时结束、时钟回拨时所选定的那一次时间。 Editing a one-time task's title or prompt preserves its original execution time, including seconds and the selected occurrence during a daylight-saving clock rollback. 更改其日期、时间或时区会重新计算执行时间。 Changing its date, time, or timezone recalculates the execution time. 编辑过程中切换任务时，会加载所选任务自身的标题、提示词和调度设置。 Switching tasks while editing loads the selected task's own title, prompt, and schedule.
对于执行时间未更改的一次性任务，即使距离执行时间已不足 `scheduler.min_once_delay_seconds`，仍可保存。 An unchanged one-time execution time can still be saved within `scheduler.min_once_delay_seconds` of its run. 新的执行时间以及重新激活的已完成任务必须满足该最小延迟要求。 New execution times and reactivated finished tasks must satisfy that minimum delay.
当前最小可行产品（MVP）的功能： Current MVP capabilities:
在 `/workspace/scheduled-tasks` 管理任务 Manage tasks at `/workspace/scheduled-tasks`
一次性任务表单不接受因夏令时切换而被跳过的本地时间；创建或保存任务前，请选择其他时间。 One-time task forms reject local times skipped by daylight-saving transitions; select another time before creating or saving the task.
现有的一次性任务会显示调度器的执行时间，即使对话或 API 保存的是因夏令时切换而被跳过的本地时间。 Existing one-time tasks display the scheduler's execution time even when a conversation or API saved a local time skipped by daylight saving. 未明确指定时区偏移量的本地时间采用重复时段中第一次出现的时间。 Local times without an explicit offset use the first occurrence of a repeated hour.
选择每个定时任务是复用线程及其对话历史，还是每次运行都创建新线程 Choose whether each scheduled task reuses a thread and its conversation history or creates a fresh thread per run
将每个任务固定分配给 `lead_agent`（默认）或任务所有者已有的自定义智能体；未知名称会被拒绝 Pin each task to `lead_agent` (default) or a custom agent the owner already has; unknown names are rejected
在定时任务的 PATCH 请求中发送 `assistant_id: null` 会将任务重置为使用 `lead_agent`；省略 `assistant_id` 则保留当前智能体，即使该自定义智能体此后已被删除 Sending `assistant_id: null` in a scheduled-task PATCH resets the task to `lead_agent`; omitting `assistant_id` preserves the current agent, including when that custom agent has since been deleted
将现有任务复制到创建表单中，作为可编辑的草稿，不复制其运行历史 Duplicate an existing task into the create form as an editable draft without copying its run history
支持 `once`、`cron` 和 `interval` 调度 Support `once`, `cron`, and `interval` schedules
编辑或复制间隔任务时，会保留其已保存的执行周期，直到明确更改间隔；这也包括操作员的调度器配置所允许的短于一分钟的间隔 Editing or duplicating an interval task preserves its saved cadence until the interval is explicitly changed, including sub-minute intervals allowed by the operator's scheduler configuration
将后台定时执行作为非交互式 DeerFlow 运行执行（其中不提供 `ask_clarification`） Run background scheduled executions as non-interactive DeerFlow runs (`ask_clarification` is not exposed there)
当复用的线程繁忙或全局执行配额已用尽时，将到期的执行持久化为 `queued`，并在有可用容量时启动；排队的执行实例在 Gateway 重启后仍会保留，并在超过 `scheduler.queue_timeout_seconds` 后失败 Persist a due execution as `queued` when its reused thread or the global execution budget is busy, then launch it when capacity is available; queued occurrences survive Gateway restarts and fail after `scheduler.queue_timeout_seconds`
重试因资源繁忙而等待的定时任务时，如果先前的一轮恢复仍在进行，该任务会保留其执行槽位。 Retrying a busy scheduled task keeps its execution slot while an earlier recovery pass is still in progress.
如果某次定时运行的完成钩子失败，调度器会在下一次轮询时根据其已持久化的终态运行记录完成收尾，而不会使其一直保持 `running` 直到重启。 A scheduled run whose completion hook fails is finalized from its durable terminal run on the next scheduler poll instead of staying `running` until a restart.
在任务所有者之间公平分配执行槽位：同一所有者在任一时刻最多只能有 `scheduler.max_concurrent_runs_per_user` 次定时运行处于启动中或运行中（默认值为 2，始终不超过 `max_concurrent_runs`；`0` 表示关闭每个所有者的数量上限），等待队列按所有者轮流处理，因此某个所有者的任务积压不会阻碍其他所有者的运行。 Share execution slots fairly between task owners: one owner may have at most `scheduler.max_concurrent_runs_per_user` scheduled runs starting or running at a time (default 2, never more than `max_concurrent_runs`; `0` turns the per-owner cap off), and the waiting queue is drained owner by owner, so one owner's backlog never holds back another owner's run. 等待时间超过 `scheduler.queue_timeout_seconds` 的运行会被跳过，其历史记录显示为“已跳过：等待空闲槽位的时间过长” A run that waits longer than `scheduler.queue_timeout_seconds` is skipped, and its history reads "Skipped: it waited too long for a free slot"
当执行实例处于 `queued`、`launching` 或 `running` 状态时，冻结任务定义，以防已持久化的执行实例在无提示的情况下采用不同的提示词、线程或调度；暂停或删除任务会取消现有的等待执行实例，而对于处于 `launching`/`running` 状态的工作，必须等待其完成后再重试这些修改操作；显式手动触发的执行仍可等待并运行，无须恢复已暂停的调度 Freeze a task's definition while an occurrence is `queued`, `launching`, or `running`, so a durable occurrence cannot silently pick up a different prompt, thread, or schedule; transitioning a task to paused or deleting it cancels an existing waiting occurrence, while `launching`/`running` work must finish before those mutations are retried and an explicit manual trigger may still wait and run without resuming a paused schedule
暂停、恢复、触发任务，查看历史记录，以及删除任务 Pause, resume, trigger, inspect history, and delete tasks
搜索任务标题或提示词，并结合状态／类型筛选条件及当前线程范围进行筛选。 Search task titles or prompts, combined with status/type filters and the current thread scope.
通过正常的 DeerFlow 运行生命周期执行定时工作 Execute scheduled work through the normal DeerFlow run lifecycle
当 `channel_connections.enabled: true` 时，通过发件箱和投递工作进程，将定时任务更新发送至任务所有者已连接的即时通信身份，适用于支持主动推送的应用，目前包括企业微信、飞书/Lark、微信和 QQ 私聊；“设置 → 通知”可按已连接的应用分别控制主动更新，独立于浏览器通知和普通聊天回复。 When `channel_connections.enabled: true`, send scheduled task updates to the task owner's connected IM identities (outbox + delivery worker) on apps that support proactive push, which today is WeCom, Feishu/Lark, WeChat and QQ private chat; Settings → Notifications controls proactive updates per connected app, independently of browser notifications and ordinary chat replies. 每个执行实例针对每个目标最多将一条消息加入队列：消息可涉及运行完成、失败或未达成目标，连续三次未达成目标后自动暂停，智能体暂停任务（其停止条件已满足），或任务结束（所有 `max_runs` 均已完成，或已到达 `end_at`）；当多种情况同时适用时，优先发送暂停或结束通知，并仍会说明最后一次运行的情况。 Each occurrence queues at most one message per target: a finished, failed or goal-missed run, the automatic pause after three missed goals, a pause by the agent (its stop condition was met) or the task finishing (all `max_runs` done, `end_at` reached); when several apply, the pause or finish wins and still says how the last run went. 一次性任务仅发送其运行结果。 A one-time task sends only its run's outcome. 消息入队与结果记录在同一数据库事务中完成，因此，在崩溃或租约丢失后完成收尾的运行不会针对同一目标重复将通知加入队列。 The message is queued in the same database transaction that records the outcome, so runs finalized after a crash or a lost lease do not enqueue a duplicate notice for the same target. 消息内容完整，可独立理解，并使用你的 Web 界面语言（否则使用 `channel_connections.notification_locale`）：包含任务标题、发生的情况、智能体已回复时的一行结果，以及“打开 DeerFlow → 定时任务以查看详情。”，不含 ID 或链接。 It reads on its own, in your web UI language (else `channel_connections.notification_locale`): the task title, what happened, a one-line result when the agent replied, and "Open DeerFlow → Scheduled tasks for details.", with no IDs and no links. 普通的手动“立即运行”试运行和被中断的运行不发送通知；未产生已完成运行便结束的执行实例（启动错误、排队超时、因重启而中断）也不发送通知。 Plain manual "run now" trials and interrupted runs stay silent, and so do occurrences that end without a finished run (launch error, queue timeout, interrupted by a restart). 渠道或传输故障会使投递暂时搁置，最长约一天，且不耗尽重试次数；平台拒绝则会触发重试，持续约 15 分钟后最终标记为 `failed`。 Channel/transport outages park deliveries without exhausting retries, for up to about a day; platform rejections retry for roughly 15 minutes before settling as `failed`. 如果你在投递等待期间断开某个身份的连接，系统绝不会再向其推送：对应记录会被标记为 `failed` 并从待投递队列中移除。 An identity you disconnect while a delivery is waiting is never pushed to: the row is dropped as `failed`.
按每页 50 条浏览执行历史；查看较早的页面时会暂停自动刷新，并提供明确的返回最新运行记录的操作。 Browse execution history in pages of 50; older pages pause automatic refresh, with an explicit return to the latest runs. 仅在成功读取后显示运行次数；加载中或读取失败不会被报告为零次运行。 Counts appear only after a successful read; loading and failed reads are not reported as zero runs.
派生的运行摘要会在限制文本长度之前，移除开头以 `` 标示的内联推理内容，避免冗长的思考内容在预览或定时通知中取代实际答案。 Derived run summaries omit leading inline `` reasoning before limiting the text, so a long thought does not replace the actual answer in previews or scheduled notices. 原始响应历史和答案中的字面标签保持不变；此前存储的运行摘要不会被重写。 Original response history and literal tags in the answer are unchanged; previously stored run summaries are not rewritten.
**通过 API 筛选执行历史** **Filter execution history through the API**
为了检查失败情况而无需下载每一次成功执行的记录，拥有 `threads:read` 权限的已认证客户端可以针对其拥有的任务请求 `GET /api/scheduled-tasks/{task_id}/runs?status=failed&limit=50&offset=0`。 To inspect failures without downloading every successful occurrence, authenticated clients with `threads:read` can request `GET /api/scheduled-tasks/{task_id}/runs?status=failed&limit=50&offset=0` for an owned task. 可选的 `status` 接受 `queued`、`launching`、`running`、`success`、`failed`、`skipped`、`interrupted` 或 `unmet`；这些是单次执行的状态，因此 `completed` 等任务状态无效（422）。 The optional `status` accepts `queued`, `launching`, `running`, `success`, `failed`, `skipped`, `interrupted`, or `unmet`; these are occurrence statuses, so task statuses such as `completed` are invalid (422).
筛选在分页之前进行。`limit`（1–200，默认 50）和 `offset`（非负，默认 0）作用于匹配的记录，记录先按创建时间、再按 ID 排序，两者均为降序。 Filtering happens before pagination. `limit` (1–200, default 50) and `offset` (nonnegative, default 0) apply to matching records, ordered by creation time then ID, both descending. 省略 `status` 会保留现有的混合历史记录数组响应；没有匹配项时返回 `[]`。 Omitting `status` preserves the existing mixed-history array response; no matches return `[]`. 该 API 不会改变任务执行行为，工作区的历史记录界面仍不进行筛选。 The API does not change task execution, and the workspace history UI remains unfiltered.
当前最小可行产品（MVP）的限制： Current MVP limits:
不支持纯文本通知作业 No text-only notification jobs
不支持频道或 GitHub 派发目标（上述结果推送不属于派发目标） No channel or GitHub dispatch targets (result push above is not a dispatch target)
通过 `config.yaml -> scheduler.enabled` 启用后台轮询。 Enable background polling with `config.yaml -> scheduler.enabled`. 手动触发使用相同的定时任务资源和执行路径。 Manual trigger uses the same scheduled-task resource and execution path.
在任务表单中，将每小时、每天、每周或每月的计划切换为**自定义 cron** 时，会以其当前的时间设置为起点，包括所选的星期几或每月的日期。 In the task form, switching an hourly, daily, weekly, or monthly schedule to **Custom cron** starts from its current timing, including selected weekdays or day of the month. 编辑预设后切换回自定义 cron 时，会使用这些编辑后的设置，而不是较早的表达式。 Switching back to Custom cron after preset edits uses those edits rather than an older expression.

生命周期、安全上限和停止条件Lifecycle, safety caps and stop conditions
任务页面和 REST API（`POST` / `PATCH /api/scheduled-tasks`）接受与对话相同的单次运行目标（`goal_objective`）、安全上限（`max_runs`、`end_at`）和停止条件（`stop_condition`）。 The tasks page and the REST API (`POST` / `PATCH /api/scheduled-tasks`) accept the same per-run goal (`goal_objective`), safety cap (`max_runs`, `end_at`) and stop condition (`stop_condition`) as a conversation. 在 PATCH 中发送 `null` 可清除这四个字段中的任意一个；不带 UTC 偏移量的 `end_at` 表示任务所在时区的本地钟表时间。 Sending `null` in a PATCH clears any of these four; an `end_at` without a UTC offset is wall-clock time in the task's timezone.
新增或更改的一次性 `run_at` 值，以及提交的不带偏移量的 `end_at` 值，必须是所选时区中实际存在的时间。 New or changed one-time `run_at` values and submitted `end_at` values without an offset must exist in the chosen timezone. 对于这些字段，因夏令时切换而被跳过的时间会被拒绝；请选择有效的本地时间，或提供带有 UTC 偏移量的明确时刻。 For these fields, a time skipped by a daylight-saving transition is rejected; choose a valid local time or supply an explicit instant with a UTC offset. 对于重复出现的本地时间，默认采用较早出现的那一次，除非通过偏移量选择较晚的那一次。 Repeated local times keep the earlier occurrence unless an offset selects the later one. 周期性 cron 调度保留现有的运行时行为：若某次固定时间的执行落在被跳过的本地时间内，则会在当天顺延后的时间触发。 Recurring cron schedules keep their existing runtime behavior: a fixed-time occurrence in a skipped local time fires at the shifted time that day.
停止条件是用户设定的“当……时停止”规则。 A stop condition is the user's "stop when …" rule. 它存储在独立字段中（迁移 `0031`），绝不会存入任务指令中。 It is stored in its own field (migration `0031`), never inside the task instructions. 只有在一次运行开始时，DeerFlow 才会将其附加到该次运行的消息中，并要求该次运行在规则满足时调用 `stop_scheduled_task`。 Only when a run starts does DeerFlow append it to that run's message and ask the run to call `stop_scheduled_task` when the rule holds. 当 `scheduler.tool_enabled` 开启时，每次计划运行都可以停止自身的调度，无论任务是通过聊天还是任务页面创建的；关闭时，则会要求该次运行报告规则已满足，而不是调用其不具备的工具。 While `scheduler.tool_enabled` is on, every scheduled run can stop its own schedule, whether a chat or the tasks page created the task; with it off, the run is asked to report a met rule instead of calling a tool it does not have.
现在，在任务页面创建的目标任务会像在聊天中创建的任务一样接受评估，其运行也会收到已保存的备注和上一次运行的引用。 Goal tasks created on the tasks page are now evaluated like chat-created ones, and their runs also receive the saved notes and the previous-run reference.
恢复操作会从当前时间起计算下一次运行时间，因此长时间暂停也绝不会触发补跑。 Resume computes the next run from now, so a long pause never causes a catch-up run. 执行时间已过的一次性任务会返回 `422 once_time_passed`，需要设置新的时间。 A one-time task whose time has passed returns `422 once_time_passed` and needs a new time. 恢复处于活动状态的任务不会产生任何变化；暂停已完成的任务会返回 `409 task_finished`。 Resuming an active task changes nothing; pausing a finished task returns `409 task_finished`.
`max_runs` 是任务整个生命周期内自动运行次数的总上限；试运行从不计入。 `max_runs` is a lifetime total of automatic runs; trial runs never count. 重新激活已达到限制的任务（通过恢复操作，或通过 PATCH 重新启用已完成任务的调度）会返回 `409 limits_exhausted`，除非同一请求同时更新已耗尽的限制：运行次数限制已用尽时，需要将 `max_runs` 调高或设为 `null`；结束时间已过时，需要将 `end_at` 设为更晚的时间或 `null`（仅推迟 `end_at` 并不能更新已用尽的 `max_runs`）。为此，`POST /api/scheduled-tasks/{task_id}/resume` 接受可选的 `{"max_runs": …, "end_at": …}` 请求体（`null` 会清除一项限制；通过聊天创建且运行间隔短于一小时的任务必须保留一项限制）。 Reactivating a task whose cap is used up (Resume, or a PATCH that re-arms a finished task's schedule) returns `409 limits_exhausted` unless the same request renews the limit that ran out: a used-up run limit needs a higher `max_runs` or `null`, a passed end time needs a later `end_at` or `null` (a later `end_at` alone does not renew a used-up `max_runs`). `POST /api/scheduled-tasks/{task_id}/resume` accepts an optional `{"max_runs": …, "end_at": …}` body for that (`null` clears a cap; chat-created sub-hourly tasks must keep one). 如果 PATCH 仅修改已完成任务的限制，则会保存该修改，并使任务保持已完成状态。 A PATCH that only changes the cap of a finished task saves it and leaves the task finished.
目标检查失败（评估器执行失败，或检查期间对话发生变化）既不会计入触发自动暂停的三次未达标计数，也不会重置该计数。 Goal-check failures (the evaluator failed, or the conversation changed during the check) neither count toward the three-miss automatic pause nor reset it. 更改目标、指令或停止条件，或添加备注，都会重新开始计数；恢复操作则会保留该计数。 Changing the goal, the instructions or the stop condition, or adding a note, starts a new count; Resume keeps it.
当此 Gateway 进程的调度器未运行时，创建任务（包括复制任务）会返回 `409 scheduler_not_running`，因为该任务将无法按计划运行。`GET /api/features` 会报告 `scheduled_tasks.available`、`running`、`tool_enabled` 和 `min_interval_seconds`。 While this Gateway process's scheduler is not running, creating a task (including Duplicate) returns `409 scheduler_not_running`, because the task would never run on schedule. `GET /api/features` reports `scheduled_tasks.available`, `running`, `tool_enabled` and `min_interval_seconds`.
来自 `/api/scheduled-tasks*` 的错误采用 `{"detail": {"code", "message", "params"}}` 格式；参见 [`backend/docs/API.md`](backend/docs/API.md#scheduled-tasks) 和 `contracts/scheduled_task_errors_contract.json`。 Errors from `/api/scheduled-tasks*` are `{"detail": {"code", "message", "params"}}`; see [`backend/docs/API.md`](backend/docs/API.md#scheduled-tasks) and `contracts/scheduled_task_errors_contract.json`.

一个目标执行实例最多可以使用九个智能体轮次，并在每个轮次后发出一次 A goal occurrence can use up to nine agent turns, with an evaluator request after

严格。 strict. 禁用对话定时调度工具时，此策略同样适用； This policy also applies when conversation schedule tools are disabled;

定时运行使用 `config.yaml` 中的 `scheduler.recursion_limit`（默认值为 `1000`，与网页 UI 的交互式预算一致）。 Scheduled runs use `scheduler.recursion_limit` in `config.yaml` (default `1000`, matching the web UI's interactive budget). 超过 `max_recursion_limit` 的值会被限制为该上限。 Values above `max_recursion_limit` are clamped. 此字段在分派时读取，因此下一次定时运行即可采用新值，无需重启 Gateway。 This field is read at dispatch, so the next scheduled run picks it up without a Gateway restart.
后台调度器默认以单实例方式运行。 The background scheduler is single-instance by default. 对于多 Pod 部署，请设置 `scheduler.multi_instance: true`，并使用共享 Postgres、`run_ownership.heartbeat_enabled: true` 和 `run_events.backend: db`；这样，启动恢复和定期恢复就会保留其他实例上仍然存活的运行，以原子方式将已过期的启动认领退回队列，仅接管已过期的运行租约，并阻止过时的启动写入。`max_concurrent_runs` 是跨 Pod 共享的全局上限，适用于处于 `launching`/`running` 状态的执行实例；处于等待状态的 `queued` 行不占用该额度。 For a multi-pod deployment, set `scheduler.multi_instance: true` and use shared Postgres, `run_ownership.heartbeat_enabled: true`, and `run_events.backend: db`; startup and periodic recovery then preserve live peer runs, atomically return expired launch claims to the queue, take over only expired run leases, and fence stale launch writes. `max_concurrent_runs` is a shared global cap across Pods for `launching`/`running` occurrences; waiting `queued` rows do not consume it. 如果没有这些设置，则只能在一个 Gateway pod 上启用调度器。 Without those settings, enable the scheduler on exactly one Gateway pod. 这些调度器字段仅在启动时生效；更改它们时，请同时重启所有 Gateway Pod。 These scheduler fields are startup-only; restart all Gateway Pods together when changing them.
通过 API 预览 cron 执行时间Preview cron occurrences through the API
具有 `threads:read` 权限的已认证客户端可以在创建任务前调用 `POST /api/scheduled-tasks/preview-cron`： Authenticated clients with `threads:read` can call `POST /api/scheduled-tasks/preview-cron` before creating a task: 
{"cron":"0 9 * * 1-5","timezone":"Asia/Shanghai","count":3,"start_at":"2026-09-12T00:00:00Z"}
响应包含规范化后的 `cron`、`timezone`、实际生效的 UTC `start_at`，以及 `occurrences`，其中包含 UTC `run_at` 和带时区偏移量的 `local_time`。 The response contains normalized `cron`, `timezone`, the effective UTC `start_at`, and `occurrences` with UTC `run_at` and offset-bearing `local_time`. 在此示例中，首次触发时间为 `2026-09-14T01:00:00Z` / `2026-09-14T09:00:00+08:00`。 In this example the first occurrence is `2026-09-14T01:00:00Z` / `2026-09-14T09:00:00+08:00`.
`count` 是 1 到 10 之间的整数（默认值为 5）。`start_at` 必须包含时区；省略该参数则会获取一次服务器时间。 `count` is an integer from 1 to 10 (default 5). `start_at` must include a timezone; omit it to capture server time once. Cron 表达式采用调度器的五字段语法（最多 256 个字符）；时区名称最多为 128 个字符。 Cron expressions use the scheduler's five-field syntax (maximum 256 characters); timezone names are at most 128 characters. 输入无效或调度无法提供所请求数量的未来触发实例时，返回 422。 Invalid inputs or schedules without the requested future occurrences return 422. 预览采用与调度器相同的夏令时（DST）处理行为，不创建任务、线程或运行，也不预留执行机会。 Preview shares the scheduler's DST behavior, creates no task, thread or run, and does not reserve execution. 这是 API 提供的功能；工作区表单尚未显示这些触发实例。 This is an API capability; the workspace form does not yet display these occurrences.
升级说明Upgrade Notes
触发实例排序适用于由已升级的 Gateway 实例接纳的记录行；这些实例仅将已分配序号的触发实例投影到父任务上，并且只要仍有任何触发实例处于活动状态，就会推迟恢复，而不论该实例由哪个 Gateway 实例接纳；对于历史记录全部未分配序号的任务，在首次接纳已分配序号的触发实例之前，仍沿用原有的时间戳排序方式。 Occurrence ordering applies to rows admitted by upgraded Gateway instances, which project only sequenced occurrences onto the parent task and defer recovery while any occurrence is still live, whichever instance admitted it; a task whose history is entirely unsequenced keeps the previous timestamp ordering until its first sequenced admission. 在滚动升级期间，由升级前实例接纳的记录行仍由这些实例自行投影，与升级前相同；当所有 Gateway 写入实例均运行升级后的版本时，排序保证才会生效。 During a rolling upgrade, rows admitted by pre-upgrade instances are projected by those instances themselves, as before the upgrade, and the ordering guarantees hold once every Gateway writer runs the upgraded version. 现有历史记录不会回填；此次升级不会重建过去的顺序，也不会修复历史计数。 Existing history is not backfilled; the upgrade does not reconstruct past order or repair historical counts.
在升级配置了 `GATEWAY_WORKERS > 1` 和 `scheduler.enabled: true` 的部署之前，要么确保调度器仅在一个 Gateway 工作进程上运行，要么配置 `scheduler.multi_instance: true`，并同时使用共享 Postgres、`run_ownership.heartbeat_enabled: true` 和 `run_events.backend: db`。 Before upgrading a deployment with `GATEWAY_WORKERS > 1` and `scheduler.enabled: true`, either keep the scheduler on exactly one Gateway worker or configure `scheduler.multi_instance: true` with shared Postgres, `run_ownership.heartbeat_enabled: true`, and `run_events.backend: db`. 升级后的 Gateway 会在启动时拒绝这种不安全的配置组合，而不会静默启动。 The upgraded Gateway rejects the unsafe combination at startup instead of starting silently.
在多实例模式下，`scheduler.max_concurrent_runs` 是整个集群的执行数量上限，而非每个 Pod 的上限。 In multi-instance mode, `scheduler.max_concurrent_runs` is a cluster-wide execution cap, not a per-Pod cap. 该上限计入处于 `launching` 和 `running` 状态的调度触发实例，因此容量不会随副本数量增加而成倍增长；持久化的等待记录行不计入该上限。 It includes `launching` and `running` scheduled occurrences, so capacity does not multiply with the number of replicas; durable waiting rows remain outside the cap.
`scheduler.multi_instance` 以及相关的调度器、所有权和运行事件设置仅在启动时生效。 `scheduler.multi_instance` and the related scheduler, ownership, and run-event settings are startup-only. 通过协调重启所有 Gateway Pod 来应用更改；仅更改 ConfigMap 不会启用多实例恢复。 Apply changes with a coordinated restart of all Gateway Pods; changing the ConfigMap alone does not activate multi-instance recovery.
终端工作台（TUI）Terminal Workbench (TUI)
`deerflow` 是一款面向习惯在 shell 中工作的用户的终端原生工作台。 `deerflow` is a terminal-native workbench for people who live in the shell. 它基于 `DeerFlowClient` 以**嵌入式**方式运行，无需 Gateway、前端、nginx 或 Docker，同时遵循与 DeerFlow 其余部分相同的 `config.yaml`、检查点保存器、技能、记忆、MCP 和沙箱设置。 It runs **embedded** over `DeerFlowClient` — no Gateway, frontend, nginx, or Docker required — while honoring the same `config.yaml`, checkpointer, skills, memory, MCP, and sandbox settings as the rest of DeerFlow.
并行的同步 stdio MCP 调用使用独立会话，各自在自己的事件 Parallel synchronous stdio MCP calls use independent sessions on their own event
循环上运行。 loops. 它们不会取消彼此的连接，但也不会共享 They do not cancel each other's connections, but they do not share
服务器端状态；会话复用要求使用同一个事件循环。 server-side state; session reuse requires the same loop. 请参阅 See the
[MCP 会话说明](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md)以了解详情。 [MCP session notes](backend/docs/MCP_SERVER.md) for details.
手动管理的事件循环必须在关闭前等待所有待处理的会话所有者完成清理； Manually managed event loops must drain pending session owners before closing;
常规的 `asyncio.run()` 执行路径会自动完成此操作。 the normal `asyncio.run()` path does this automatically. 
uv pip install 'deerflow-harness[tui]'        # optional 'textual' dependency

deerflow                                      # launch the terminal UI (TTY required)
deerflow --tui-transparent                    # use the terminal's default background
deerflow --continue                           # resume the most recent thread
deerflow --resume THREAD                      # resume a thread by id
deerflow --print "summarize this repo"        # headless one-shot answer to stdout
deerflow --json  "hello"                       # headless newline-delimited StreamEvents; failure -> one {"type": "error"} record, exit 1
deerflow --recursion-limit 250 --print "task" # override the headless agent-loop limit
无界面模式下的 `--print` 和 `--json` 会在运行失败时以状态码 `1` 退出，包括以回退消息形式返回的提供方错误。`--print` 仍会将回退文本写入标准输出；`--json` 则追加一条表示终止错误的记录。 Headless `--print` and `--json` exit with status `1` when the run fails, including provider errors returned as fallback messages. `--print` still writes the fallback text to stdout; `--json` appends a terminal error record.
一个由键盘驱动的聊天界面，提供流式对话记录（以 Markdown 渲染回答）、紧凑的工具活动卡片、`/` 斜杠命令面板、仅清除显示内容的 `/clear`、用于目标管理的 `/goal`、`/model` 和 `/threads` 选择器、输入历史记录、使用 PageUp/PageDown 浏览对话记录，以及通过 `Esc` / `Ctrl+C` 中断。 A keyboard-driven chat surface with a streaming transcript (Markdown-rendered answers), compact tool-activity cards, a `/` slash-command palette, display-only `/clear`, `/goal` goal management, `/model` and `/threads` pickers, input history, PageUp/PageDown transcript navigation, and `Esc` / `Ctrl+C` interrupt. 输入编辑器会保留所粘贴代码、堆栈跟踪和多段提示词中的换行与缩进；按 `Enter` 会发送完整文档。 The composer preserves line breaks and indentation in pasted code, stack traces, and multi-paragraph prompts; `Enter` sends the complete document. 向上滚动后，对话记录刷新时会保留你的阅读位置；返回底部后，则会恢复跟随新输出。`/clear` 会移除当前终端显示中的行，而不会删除线程或其持久化的对话；在运行进行期间，`/new` 和 `/clear` 会提示你等待，而不会重置正在进行的显示状态。 Transcript refreshes preserve your reading position after you scroll upward and resume following new output when you return to the bottom. `/clear` removes rows from the current terminal display without deleting the thread or its persisted conversation; `/new` and `/clear` ask you to wait during an active run instead of resetting in-flight display state. 在 TUI 中打开的会话也会出现在 Web UI 侧边栏中——它以本地默认用户的身份写入共享线程存储，因此终端与网页**无需运行 Gateway** 即可保持同步。 Sessions opened in the TUI also appear in the Web UI sidebar — it writes the shared thread store under the local default user, so terminal and web stay in sync **without running the Gateway**.
TUI 斜杠命令允许在参数前使用 Unicode 空白字符，包括 TUI slash commands accept Unicode whitespace before their arguments, including
空格、制表符、换行符和不换行空格。 spaces, tabs, line breaks, and nonbreaking spaces. 分隔符会关闭斜杠命令 A separator closes the slash
面板，使 Enter 提交已编写的文本。 palette so Enter submits the composed text. 粘贴的技能任务可以从 Pasted skill tasks can start on the
下一行开始；其内部的换行和缩进会得到保留。 next line; their internal line breaks and indentation are preserved.

切换对话。 switching conversations. 无效的 `/resume` 引用会显示错误，但不会 An invalid `/resume` reference displays an error without

⚠️ 安全提示⚠️ Security Notice
部署不当可能引入安全风险Improper Deployment May Introduce Security Risks
DeerFlow 具有关键的高权限能力，包括**系统命令执行、资源操作和业务逻辑调用**，默认设计为**部署在本地可信环境中（仅可通过 127.0.0.1 回环接口访问）**。 DeerFlow has key high-privilege capabilities including **system command execution, resource operations, and business logic invocation**, and is designed by default to be **deployed in a local trusted environment (accessible only via the 127.0.0.1 loopback interface)**. 如果你在未采取严格安全措施的情况下，将该智能体部署在不可信环境中，例如局域网、公有云服务器或其他可由多个端点访问的环境，则可能引入安全风险，包括： If you deploy the agent in untrusted environments — such as LAN networks, public cloud servers, or other multi-endpoint accessible environments — without strict security measures, it may introduce security risks, including:
**未经授权的非法调用**：智能体功能可能被未经授权的第三方或恶意互联网扫描程序发现，从而触发大量未经授权的请求，执行系统命令、文件读写等高风险操作，进而可能造成严重的安全后果。 **Unauthorized illegal invocation**: Agent functionality could be discovered by unauthorized third parties or malicious internet scanners, triggering bulk unauthorized requests that execute high-risk operations such as system commands and file read/write, potentially causing serious security consequences.
**合规与法律风险**：如果该智能体被非法调用，用于实施网络攻击、数据窃取或其他违法活动，则可能导致法律责任和合规风险。 **Compliance and legal risks**: If the agent is illegally invoked to conduct cyberattacks, data theft, or other illegal activities, it may result in legal liability and compliance risks.
Gateway 管理员权限等同于代码执行权限Gateway Admin Is Equivalent to Code Execution
管理员可以注册 stdio MCP 服务器，这些服务器会在 Gateway 内运行命令 An admin can register stdio MCP servers, which run commands inside the Gateway
的容器中。 container. API 将其限制在允许列表内（默认为 `npx`、`uvx`， The API restricts them to an allowlist (`npx`, `uvx` by default,

环境变量。 environment variables that would evaluate arbitrary code. 这属于纵深 That is defense in
防御，而非安全边界：这些启动器的用途就是获取并运行远程软件包， depth, not a boundary: these launchers exist to fetch and run remote packages,
因此，**应将 Gateway 管理权限视为等同于在主机上执行代码的权限**，并据此授予 so **treat Gateway admin as equivalent to code execution on the host** and grant
该权限。 it accordingly.

部署默认设置Deployment Defaults
Docker 栈仅在 `127.0.0.1` 上开放入口端口，而本地的 The Docker stack publishes its entry port on `127.0.0.1` only, and local
`make dev` / `make start` 将 nginx、Gateway 和前端绑定到回环地址， `make dev` / `make start` bind nginx, the Gateway, and the frontend to loopback,
与上述本地可信环境模型一致。 matching the local-trusted-environment model described above. 若要从 To reach it from
另一台机器访问它，请在 `.env` 中设置 `BIND_HOST`（例如 `BIND_HOST=0.0.0.0`）——且务必 another machine, set `BIND_HOST` in `.env` (e.g. `BIND_HOST=0.0.0.0`) — and only
先落实以下安全措施，再进行此设置。 after putting the security measures below in place. 本地运行时，`BIND_HOST` Local runs apply `BIND_HOST`
仅应用于端口 `2026` 上的 nginx；Gateway 和前端仍绑定到回环地址，位于 to nginx on port `2026` only; the Gateway and frontend stay on loopback behind
其后方。 it.
**在主机可被访问之前完成首次运行设置。**新建的 **Complete first-run setup before the host becomes reachable.** A fresh
实例尚无任何账户，因此应通过 `/setup` 创建管理员账户， instance has no accounts yet, so create the admin account through `/setup`
并在启动任何并非仅限回环地址访问的部署后立即完成此操作。 immediately after starting any deployment that is not loopback-only.
安全建议Security Recommendations
**注意：我们强烈建议在本地可信网络环境中部署 DeerFlow。**如果需要跨设备或跨网络部署，必须实施严格的安全措施，例如： **Note: We strongly recommend deploying DeerFlow in a local trusted network environment.** If you need cross-device or cross-network deployment, you must implement strict security measures, such as:
**IP 允许列表**：使用 `iptables`，或部署具有访问控制列表（ACL）的硬件防火墙 / 交换机，以**配置 IP 允许列表规则**，并拒绝所有其他 IP 地址的访问。 **IP allowlist**: Use `iptables`, or deploy hardware firewalls / switches with Access Control Lists (ACL), to **configure IP allowlist rules** and deny access from all other IP addresses.
**身份验证网关**：配置反向代理（例如 nginx），并**启用严格的前置身份验证**，阻止任何未经身份验证的访问。 **Authentication gateway**: Configure a reverse proxy (e.g., nginx) and **enable strong pre-authentication**, blocking any unauthenticated access.
**网络隔离**：尽可能将智能体和可信设备置于**同一个专用 VLAN** 中，与其他网络设备隔离。 **Network isolation**: Where possible, place the agent and trusted devices in the **same dedicated VLAN**, isolated from other network devices.
**保持更新**：持续关注 DeerFlow 的安全功能更新。 **Stay updated**: Continue to follow DeerFlow's security feature updates.

后端的 `make test` 会并发运行四个按耗时均衡划分的测试分片，并在 Backend `make test` runs four duration-balanced shards concurrently and reports
任一分片失败时报告失败。 failure if any shard fails. 使用 `make test TEST_JOBS=1` 可按顺序运行这些分片。 Use `make test TEST_JOBS=1` to run the shards sequentially.
其测试覆盖范围不包括真实外部 API 和阻塞式 I/O。 It excludes live external-API and blocking-I/O coverage.
运行 `cd backend && make test-blocking-io` 可执行严格的阻塞式 I/O 检查。 Run `cd backend && make test-blocking-io` for strict blocking-I/O checks.
维护者可通过 `cd backend && make test-live` 运行真实的 `DeerFlowClient` 测试套件。 Maintainers can run the real `DeerFlowClient` suite with `cd backend && make test-live`.
此命令需要根目录下有效的 `config.yaml` 和 API 凭据。 This command requires a valid root `config.yaml` and API credentials.
它可能产生 API 费用，并创建本地沙箱、产物或文件。 It can incur API costs and create local sandboxes, artifacts, or files.
直接运行 pytest 还需设置 `DEER_FLOW_RUN_LIVE_TESTS=1`。 Direct pytest runs additionally require `DEER_FLOW_RUN_LIVE_TESTS=1`.

Gateway 的产物提供功能现在强制将活动 Web 内容类型（`text/html` 以及 `.xml`、`.xhtml` 和 `.svg` 等 XML 文档）作为附件下载，而非内联渲染，从而降低生成产物的 XSS 风险。 Gateway artifact serving now forces active web content types (`text/html` and XML documents such as `.xml`, `.xhtml`, and `.svg`) to download as attachments instead of inline rendering, reducing XSS risk for generated artifacts.

Gateway 重启后，登录并选择 **恢复工作流**，即可利用保留的结果和针对确切版本的批准恢复兼容的运行。 After a Gateway restart, sign in and select **Recover workflow** to recover a compatible run using retained results and exact-version approvals. 子任务准入状态不确定时，需要进行核对；系统绝不会自动重试。 Uncertain child admissions require reconciliation; they are never automatically retried. 恢复要求运行时和配置保持不变，并且 Gateway 存储具有持久性。 Recovery requires unchanged runtime/configuration and durable Gateway storage. 可选的 PostgreSQL 模式支持跨 Gateway 控制，以及在租约到期后经会话授权进行接管，并提供带隔离保护的子任务准入和共享限额；参见扩展部署契约。 The optional PostgreSQL mode supports cross-Gateway controls and session-authorized takeover after lease expiry, with fenced child admission and shared limits; see the extension deployment contract. 即使已达到工作流准入上限，仍可接管已过期的取消操作以执行清理。 Expired cancellations can be taken over for cleanup even at workflow admission limits. 准入前因权限或配置问题而被明确拒绝时，会释放子任务容量；准入状态不确定时，则保留其预留容量。 Definitive pre-admission permission/configuration rejection releases child capacity; uncertain admissions retain their reservations. SQLite 仍仅支持单个 Gateway。 SQLite remains single-Gateway.
